================================================================================
# bc_rules_views.py
================================================================================
"""
bc_rules_views.py
-----------------
Views for the BC rules wizard.
Place in: businesscontinuity/bc_rules_views.py

Progress pattern: JSON polling via Django cache ('longcache', DB-backed —
shared across gunicorn workers, unlike the per-process 'default' cache).
No StreamingHttpResponse — no nginx/gunicorn buffering issues.

Flow:
  1. GET  /apply_bc_rules/                  -> wizard HTML (texts loaded from DB)
  2. GET  /apply_bc_rules/scope/            -> filter scope + sibling datacenter stats (JSON)
  3. GET  /apply_bc_rules/texts/            -> current saved rule texts (JSON)
  4. POST /apply_bc_rules/preview/          -> compute preview, no DB write (JSON)
  5. POST /apply_bc_rules/start/            -> start background thread, return job_id (JSON)
  6. GET  /apply_bc_rules/status/<job_id>/ -> poll job progress (JSON)
"""

import datetime
import json
import logging
import os
import threading
import uuid

logger = logging.getLogger('businesscontinuity')

from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib.auth.decorators import login_required
from django.core.cache import caches
from django.db import transaction
from django.db.models import Q
from django.http import JsonResponse
from django.shortcuts import render
from django.views.decorators.http import require_http_methods
from urllib.parse import parse_qs, unquote

from .bc_rules_logic import (
    ALL_RULES, DEFAULT_TEXTS, compute_bc_results, group_by_rule
)
from .models import BCRuleTexts, Server, ServerUnique
from userapp.models import SavedOptions, UserPermissions, UserProfile
from .utils import is_editor
from .views import _build_filtered_queryset, selection_mismatch

# 'longcache' (DB-backed) is shared across gunicorn workers — 'default' is
# per-process LocMemCache and would be invisible to workers other than the
# one running the background thread.
cache = caches['longcache']

# Value written by the sibling reset, same as the DR Reset feature
RESET_VALUE = 'EMPTY'
# Hostnames returned to the UI for the warning lists
LISTED_SERVERS_CAP = 2000


# ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

# Editor check is handled by utils.is_editor() — imported above


def _job_cache_key(job_id):
    return f"bc_rules_job_{job_id}"


def _set_job_status(job_id, data):
    """Write job status to cache (TTL 1 hour)."""
    cache.set(_job_cache_key(job_id), data, timeout=3600)


def _get_job_status(job_id):
    return cache.get(_job_cache_key(job_id))


def _load_rule_texts():
    """
    Load saved rule texts from BCRuleTexts.
    Falls back to DEFAULT_TEXTS for any missing rule_key.
    Always returns a complete dict with all 9 rule keys.
    """
    saved = {obj.rule_key: obj.action_text
             for obj in BCRuleTexts.objects.all()}
    # Use saved value if key exists in DB (even if empty string),
    # fall back to DEFAULT_TEXTS only if the key has never been saved.
    return {key: saved[key] if key in saved else DEFAULT_TEXTS[key]
            for key in ALL_RULES}


def _blank_rules_error(rule_texts):
    """Return an error message if a rule text is blank, None otherwise."""
    blank = [key for key, text in rule_texts.items() if not str(text or '').strip()]
    if not blank:
        return None
    return (
        f"Action texts cannot be empty: {', '.join(blank)}. "
        f"Enter {RESET_VALUE} if no action is wanted."
    )


def _save_rule_texts(rule_texts, username):
    """
    Persist rule texts to BCRuleTexts (upsert).
    Only saves keys present in ALL_RULES.
    """
    for rule_key, action_text in rule_texts.items():
        if rule_key not in ALL_RULES:
            continue
        BCRuleTexts.objects.update_or_create(
            rule_key=rule_key,
            defaults={
                'action_text': action_text,
                'updated_by':  username,
            }
        )


# ── View: wizard HTML ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@login_required
def apply_bc_rules_view(request):
    user_is_editor = is_editor(request)
    saved_texts = _load_rule_texts()
    return render(request, 'businesscontinuity/apply_bc_rules.html', {
        'is_user_editor': user_is_editor,
        'saved_texts':    json.dumps(saved_texts),
        'default_texts':  json.dumps(DEFAULT_TEXTS),
        'all_rules':      json.dumps(ALL_RULES),
        'appname':        'businesscontinuity',
    })


# ── Scope: filters from the server list ───────────────────────────────────────

class BCScopeError(Exception):
    """Raised when the requested scope or sibling datacenter is not usable."""


def _load_field_labels():
    """Load businesscontinuity/field_labels.json, empty dict if unreadable."""
    labels_path = os.path.join(
        settings.BASE_DIR, 'businesscontinuity', 'field_labels.json'
    )
    try:
        with open(labels_path, encoding='utf-8') as f:
            return json.load(f)
    except (FileNotFoundError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        return {}


def _resolve_scope(request, query):
    """
    Turn the server list query string into (queryset, datacenter).
    The filters must resolve to exactly one BC-eligible datacenter: the check
    runs on the matching rows because terms can be partial, negated or expanded.
    """
    query  = (query or '').lstrip('?')
    parsed = parse_qs(unquote(query), keep_blank_values=False)
    if not parsed.get('datacenter', [''])[0].strip():
        raise BCScopeError(
            'A Datacenter filter is required: filter the server list on one datacenter, '
            'then open Apply BC Rules again.'
        )

    servers_qs, _ = _build_filtered_queryset(request, query)
    found = list(
        servers_qs.order_by().values_list('DATACENTER', flat=True).distinct()[:11]
    )
    if not found:
        raise BCScopeError('No servers match the current filters.')
    if len(found) > 1:
        names = ', '.join(sorted(d or '(empty)' for d in found[:10]))
        raise BCScopeError(
            f'The filters must match exactly one datacenter, found: {names}. '
            'Tip: prefix the value with @ for an exact match.'
        )

    mismatch = selection_mismatch(
        servers_qs.order_by().values('server_unique_id').distinct().count(),
        parsed.get('expected_servers', [''])[0],
    )
    if mismatch:
        raise BCScopeError(mismatch)

    datacenter = found[0]
    if not datacenter:
        raise BCScopeError('The filters match servers without a datacenter.')
    if datacenter not in _load_field_labels().get('bc_datacenter_siblings', {}):
        raise BCScopeError(
            f'"{datacenter}" is not configured for BC rules '
            '(field_labels.json, key: bc_datacenter_siblings).'
        )
    return servers_qs, datacenter


def _validate_sibling(datacenter, sibling):
    """Return the cleaned sibling name (or None), raise if it cannot be used."""
    sibling = (sibling or '').strip() or None
    if sibling is None:
        return None
    if sibling == datacenter:
        raise BCScopeError('The sibling datacenter must differ from the affected datacenter.')
    if not Server.objects.filter(DATACENTER=sibling).exists():
        raise BCScopeError(f'No servers found for sibling datacenter "{sibling}".')
    return sibling


def _sibling_querysets(datacenter, sibling):
    """
    Return (all sibling servers, servers also in the affected datacenter, servers to reset).
    action_during_lp is stored per hostname: a server on both sites must keep the
    rule result, even when it is outside the filtered scope.
    """
    affected_ids = Server.objects.filter(DATACENTER=datacenter).values('server_unique_id')
    in_sibling   = ServerUnique.objects.filter(server_info__DATACENTER=sibling).distinct()
    to_reset = (
        in_sibling
        .exclude(id__in=affected_ids)
        .exclude(action_during_lp__isnull=True)
        .exclude(action_during_lp__in=['', RESET_VALUE])
    )
    return in_sibling, in_sibling.filter(id__in=affected_ids), to_reset


def _sibling_stats(datacenter, sibling):
    """Summarize the sibling reset for the wizard. None when no sibling is selected."""
    if not sibling:
        return None
    in_sibling, shared, to_reset = _sibling_querysets(datacenter, sibling)
    hostnames  = list(in_sibling.order_by('hostname').values_list('hostname', flat=True))
    reset      = set(to_reset.values_list('hostname', flat=True))
    shared_all = list(shared.order_by('hostname').values_list('hostname', flat=True))
    # The sibling reset ignores exceptions: tell how many of them it will reset
    reset_exceptions = sorted(to_reset.filter(bc_exception__iexact='YES').values_list('hostname', flat=True))
    return {
        'datacenter':        sibling,
        'total':             len(hostnames),
        'to_reset':          len(reset),
        'servers':           hostnames[:LISTED_SERVERS_CAP],
        'changed_servers':   [h for h in hostnames if h in reset][:LISTED_SERVERS_CAP],
        'unchanged_servers': [h for h in hostnames if h not in reset][:LISTED_SERVERS_CAP],
        'shared_count':      len(shared_all),
        'shared':            shared_all[:LISTED_SERVERS_CAP],
        'shared_truncated':  len(shared_all) > LISTED_SERVERS_CAP,
        'exceptions_reset':  {
            'count':     len(reset_exceptions),
            'servers':   reset_exceptions[:LISTED_SERVERS_CAP],
            'truncated': len(reset_exceptions) > LISTED_SERVERS_CAP,
        },
    }


def _split_exceptions(servers_qs):
    """Remove the servers in exception from the scope. Return (remaining scope, exception summary)."""
    in_exception = Q(server_unique__bc_exception__iexact='YES')
    hostnames = sorted(set(servers_qs.filter(in_exception).values_list('server_unique__hostname', flat=True)))
    return servers_qs.exclude(in_exception), {
        'count':     len(hostnames),
        'servers':   hostnames[:LISTED_SERVERS_CAP],
        'truncated': len(hostnames) > LISTED_SERVERS_CAP,
    }


def _skipped_with_action(servers_qs, results):
    """List skipped servers in scope that keep an existing action (never modified by the job)."""
    applied = {r.server_unique_id for r in results}
    rows = servers_qs.order_by().values_list(
        'server_unique_id', 'server_unique__hostname', 'server_unique__action_during_lp'
    )
    hostnames = sorted({
        hostname for su_id, hostname, action in rows
        if su_id not in applied and (action or '').strip() not in ('', RESET_VALUE)
    })
    return {
        'count':     len(hostnames),
        'servers':   hostnames[:LISTED_SERVERS_CAP],
        'truncated': len(hostnames) > LISTED_SERVERS_CAP,
    }


def _describe_filters(parsed, labels):
    """List the active server list filters as {label, value} for display."""
    described = []
    for field in labels.get('fields', {}).values():
        value = parsed.get(field.get('inputname'), [''])[0]
        if value:
            described.append({'label': field.get('displayname', ''), 'value': value})
    return described


def _active_permanent_filter(user, labels):
    """Return the name of the permanent filter applied to this user, if any."""
    name = (
        SavedOptions.objects
        .filter(user_profile__user=user)
        .values_list('businesscontinuity_permanentfilter', flat=True)
        .first()
    )
    return name if name in labels.get('permanentfilters', {}) else None


@login_required
@require_http_methods(["GET"])
def bc_scope(request):
    """
    Describe the scope built from the server list filters.
    Query params: query (server list query string), sibling (optional: the
    configured sibling is used when absent, an empty value means none).
    """
    query = request.GET.get('query', '')
    try:
        servers_qs, datacenter = _resolve_scope(request, query)
        labels     = _load_field_labels()
        configured = labels.get('bc_datacenter_siblings', {}).get(datacenter)
        options    = sorted(
            d for d in Server.objects.order_by().values_list('DATACENTER', flat=True).distinct()
            if d and d != datacenter
        )
        default_sibling = configured if configured in options else None
        requested = request.GET.get('sibling')
        sibling   = _validate_sibling(
            datacenter, default_sibling if requested is None else requested
        )
    except BCScopeError as exc:
        return JsonResponse({'error': str(exc)}, status=400)

    parsed = parse_qs(unquote(query.lstrip('?')), keep_blank_values=False)
    return JsonResponse({
        'datacenter':       datacenter,
        'filters':          _describe_filters(parsed, labels),
        'permanent_filter': _active_permanent_filter(request.user, labels),
        'count':            servers_qs.order_by().values('server_unique_id').distinct().count(),
        'count_total':      servers_qs.count(),
        'default_sibling':  default_sibling,
        'options':          options,
        'sibling':          _sibling_stats(datacenter, sibling),
    })


# ── API: current saved texts ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

@login_required
def bc_texts(request):
    """
    GET : return the currently saved rule texts (DB, falling back to defaults).
    POST: save rule texts without applying rules (called on step 2 changes).
    """
    if request.method == 'GET':
        return JsonResponse({'texts': _load_rule_texts()})

    if request.method == 'POST':
        try:
            data = json.loads(request.body)
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
            return JsonResponse({'error': 'Invalid JSON body'}, status=400)

        rule_texts   = data.get('rule_texts', {})
        unknown_keys = set(rule_texts.keys()) - set(ALL_RULES)
        if unknown_keys:
            return JsonResponse({'error': f'Unknown rule keys: {unknown_keys}'}, status=400)

        blank_error = _blank_rules_error(rule_texts)
        if blank_error:
            return JsonResponse({'error': blank_error}, status=400)

        _save_rule_texts(rule_texts, request.user.username)
        return JsonResponse({'status': 'ok'})

    return JsonResponse({'error': 'Method not allowed'}, status=405)


# ── API: preview ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@login_required
@require_http_methods(["POST"])
def bc_preview(request):
    """
    Compute BC rules for the filtered scope and return a grouped summary.
    Does NOT write anything to the database.

    Request body (JSON):
      {
        "query":      "datacenter=%40DC-PARIS-01&environment=PRD",
        "sibling":    "DC-PARIS-02",
        "rule_texts": { "cluster_yes_lp_yes": "...", ... }
      }
    """
    try:
        data = json.loads(request.body)
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
        return JsonResponse({'error': 'Invalid JSON body'}, status=400)

    rule_texts   = data.get('rule_texts', {})
    unknown_keys = set(rule_texts.keys()) - set(ALL_RULES)
    if unknown_keys:
        return JsonResponse({'error': f'Unknown rule keys: {unknown_keys}'}, status=400)

    blank_error = _blank_rules_error(rule_texts)
    if blank_error:
        return JsonResponse({'error': blank_error}, status=400)

    try:
        servers_qs, datacenter = _resolve_scope(request, data.get('query'))
        sibling = _validate_sibling(datacenter, data.get('sibling'))
    except BCScopeError as exc:
        return JsonResponse({'error': str(exc)}, status=400)

    servers_qs, exceptions = _split_exceptions(servers_qs)
    results = compute_bc_results(servers_qs, datacenter, rule_texts)
    grouped = group_by_rule(results)

    summary = []
    total   = 0
    for rule_key, bc_results in grouped.items():
        hostnames = [r.hostname for r in bc_results]
        summary.append({
            'rule_key':    rule_key,
            'action_text': bc_results[0].action_text,
            'count':       len(bc_results),
            'changed':     sum(r.changed for r in bc_results),
            'servers':     hostnames[:LISTED_SERVERS_CAP],
            'changed_servers':   [r.hostname for r in bc_results if r.changed][:LISTED_SERVERS_CAP],
            'unchanged_servers': [r.hostname for r in bc_results if not r.changed][:LISTED_SERVERS_CAP],
            'truncated':   len(hostnames) > LISTED_SERVERS_CAP,
        })
        total += len(bc_results)

    total_qs = servers_qs.order_by().values('server_unique_id').distinct().count()

    return JsonResponse({
        'datacenter': datacenter,
        'total':      total,
        'changed':    sum(r.changed for r in results),
        'skipped':    total_qs - total,
        'skipped_with_action': _skipped_with_action(servers_qs, results),
        'exceptions': exceptions,
        'summary':    summary,
        'sibling':    _sibling_stats(datacenter, sibling),
    })


# ── API: start job ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

@login_required
@require_http_methods(["POST"])
def bc_start(request):
    """
    Persist rule texts, then start the BC apply job in a background thread.
    Returns a job_id for polling.

    Request body (JSON):
      {
        "query":      "datacenter=%40DC-PARIS-01&environment=PRD",
        "sibling":    "DC-PARIS-02",
        "rule_texts": { ... }
      }
    """
    if not is_editor(request):
        return JsonResponse(
            {'error': 'Permission denied. Editor role required.'},
            status=403
        )

    try:
        data = json.loads(request.body)
    except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError):
        return JsonResponse({'error': 'Invalid JSON body'}, status=400)

    try:
        servers_qs, datacenter = _resolve_scope(request, data.get('query'))
        sibling = _validate_sibling(datacenter, data.get('sibling'))
    except BCScopeError as exc:
        return JsonResponse({'error': str(exc)}, status=400)

    rule_texts = data.get('rule_texts', {})
    username   = request.user.username

    blank_error = _blank_rules_error(rule_texts)
    if blank_error:
        return JsonResponse({'error': blank_error}, status=400)

    # Persist texts before starting the job
    _save_rule_texts(rule_texts, username)

    job_id = str(uuid.uuid4())
    _set_job_status(job_id, {
        'progress': 0,
        'message':  'Starting...',
        'log':      [],
        'done':     False,
        'error':    None,
        'stats':    None,
    })

    logger.info(
        f"[BC Rules] {username} started — datacenter={datacenter}, "
        f"sibling={sibling}, query={data.get('query')}"
    )
    threading.Thread(
        target=_run_bc_job,
        args=(job_id, servers_qs, datacenter, rule_texts, username, sibling),
        daemon=True,
    ).start()

    return JsonResponse({'job_id': job_id})


# ── Background thread ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

def _reset_sibling_servers(reset_ids, sibling, username, on_batch):
    """Set action_during_lp to EMPTY on the given servers, with their history."""
    batch_size = 100
    for i in range(0, len(reset_ids), batch_size):
        batch_ids  = reset_ids[i:i + batch_size]
        batch_objs = list(ServerUnique.objects.filter(id__in=batch_ids))
        # Per batch: changes.py pairs text and global history within a few seconds
        now_str = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")

        with transaction.atomic():
            for su in batch_objs:
                su.action_during_lp         = RESET_VALUE
                su.action_during_lp_history = (
                    f"[{now_str} - {username}] [BC Rules Reset] {RESET_VALUE}\n"
                    + (su.action_during_lp_history or '')
                )
                su.append_global_history(
                    changes={'action_during_lp': {
                        'info': f'Reset by BC Rules (sibling {sibling}) — check its history'
                    }},
                    username=username,
                    source='BC Rules Reset',
                )
            ServerUnique.objects.bulk_update(
                batch_objs,
                ['action_during_lp', 'action_during_lp_history', 'global_history']
            )
        on_batch(len(batch_objs))


def _run_bc_job(job_id, servers_qs, datacenter, rule_texts, username, sibling=None):
    """Runs in a background thread. Writes progress and the final recap to cache for polling."""

    def update(progress, message, log_line=None):
        status = _get_job_status(job_id) or {}
        log    = status.get('log', [])
        if log_line:
            ts = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M:%S")
            log.append({'ts': ts, 'msg': log_line})
        _set_job_status(job_id, {
            **status,
            'progress': round(progress, 1),
            'message':  message,
            'log':      log,
        })

    started_at = datetime.datetime.now()
    # Filled as the job goes: on failure it tells what was really saved
    stats = {
        'datacenter':          datacenter,
        'sibling':             sibling,
        'total':               0,
        'changed':             0,
        'unchanged':           0,
        'updated':             0,
        'skipped':             0,
        'skipped_with_action': {'count': 0, 'servers': [], 'truncated': False},
        'reset':               0,
        'reset_planned':       0,
        'shared':              0,
        'exceptions':          {'count': 0, 'servers': [], 'truncated': False},
        'exceptions_reset':    0,
        'rules':               [],
    }

    def finish(message, error=None):
        finished_at = datetime.datetime.now()
        stats['finished'] = finished_at.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        stats['duration'] = round((finished_at - started_at).total_seconds(), 1)
        status = _get_job_status(job_id) or {}
        _set_job_status(job_id, {
            **status,
            'progress': 100 if error is None else status.get('progress', 0),
            'message':  message,
            'done':     True,
            'error':    error,
            'stats':    stats,
        })

    try:
        update(5, 'Loading servers...')

        if not servers_qs.exists():
            finish('Error', f'No servers match the scope in datacenter "{datacenter}".')
            return

        update(10, 'Computing BC rules...', f'Datacenter: {datacenter}')

        servers_qs, exceptions = _split_exceptions(servers_qs)
        stats['exceptions'] = exceptions
        results = compute_bc_results(servers_qs, datacenter, rule_texts)
        grouped = group_by_rule(results)
        total   = len(results)
        changed = sum(r.changed for r in results)

        total_qs = servers_qs.order_by().values('server_unique_id').distinct().count()
        skipped  = total_qs - total

        reset_ids, shared_count = [], 0
        if sibling:
            _, shared, to_reset = _sibling_querysets(datacenter, sibling)
            reset_ids    = list(to_reset.values_list('id', flat=True))
            shared_count = shared.count()
            stats['exceptions_reset'] = to_reset.filter(bc_exception__iexact='YES').count()
        total_work = max(changed + len(reset_ids), 1)

        rule_stats = {
            rule_key: {
                'rule_key':    rule_key,
                'action_text': bc_results[0].action_text,
                'written':     0,
                'unchanged':   sum(not r.changed for r in bc_results),
            }
            for rule_key, bc_results in grouped.items()
        }
        stats.update({
            'total':               total,
            'changed':             changed,
            'unchanged':           total - changed,
            'skipped':             skipped,
            'skipped_with_action': _skipped_with_action(servers_qs, results),
            'reset_planned':       len(reset_ids),
            'shared':              shared_count,
            'rules':               list(rule_stats.values()),
        })

        update(20, f'{changed} servers to change, {total - changed} unchanged, {skipped} skipped.',
               f'{total}/{total_qs} servers evaluated across {len(grouped)} rules: '
               f'{changed} to change, {total - changed} unchanged'
               + (f', {skipped} skipped (EMPTY/unknown cluster or in_live_play)' if skipped else ''))

        now_str      = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        history_hdr  = f"[{now_str} - {username}] [BC Rules Auto]"
        updated_count = 0
        batch_size    = 100

        for rule_index, (rule_key, bc_results) in enumerate(grouped.items(), start=1):
            action_text = bc_results[0].action_text
            # Servers already holding this text are left alone: no write, no history entry
            su_ids      = [r.server_unique_id for r in bc_results if r.changed]

            for i in range(0, len(su_ids), batch_size):
                batch_ids  = su_ids[i:i + batch_size]
                batch_objs = list(ServerUnique.objects.filter(id__in=batch_ids))

                with transaction.atomic():
                    for su in batch_objs:
                        su.action_during_lp         = action_text
                        su.action_during_lp_history = (
                            f"{history_hdr} {action_text}\n"
                            + (su.action_during_lp_history or '')
                        )
                        su.append_global_history(
                            changes={'action_during_lp': {
                                'info': 'Updated by BC Rules — check its history'
                            }},
                            username=username,
                            source='BC Rules',
                        )
                    ServerUnique.objects.bulk_update(
                        batch_objs,
                        ['action_during_lp', 'action_during_lp_history', 'global_history']
                    )

                updated_count += len(batch_objs)
                stats['updated'] = updated_count
                rule_stats[rule_key]['written'] += len(batch_objs)
                update(
                    20 + (updated_count / total_work) * 75,
                    f'Rule {rule_index}/{len(grouped)} ({updated_count}/{changed})',
                )

            short = action_text[:60] + ('...' if len(action_text) > 60 else '')
            update(
                20 + (updated_count / total_work) * 75,
                f'Rule {rule_index}/{len(grouped)} done.',
                f'✓ {len(su_ids)} changed, {len(bc_results) - len(su_ids)} unchanged → "{short}"',
            )

        reset_count = 0
        if sibling:
            def on_reset_batch(batch_len):
                nonlocal reset_count
                reset_count += batch_len
                stats['reset'] = reset_count
                update(
                    20 + ((updated_count + reset_count) / total_work) * 75,
                    f'Resetting {sibling} ({reset_count}/{len(reset_ids)})',
                )

            _reset_sibling_servers(reset_ids, sibling, username, on_reset_batch)
            update(
                95, f'{sibling} done.',
                f'✓ {reset_count} servers reset to {RESET_VALUE} in {sibling}'
                + (f', {shared_count} kept (also in {datacenter})' if shared_count else ''),
            )

        update(97, 'Finalizing...', '✓ History updated.')

        logger.info(
            f"[BC Rules] {username} completed — datacenter={datacenter}, "
            f"{updated_count} servers updated, {total - changed} unchanged across {len(grouped)} rules, "
            f"sibling={sibling}, {reset_count} reset"
        )
        finish(
            f'{updated_count} servers updated'
            + (f', {reset_count} reset.' if sibling else '.')
        )

    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error(
            f"[BC Rules] {username} — datacenter={datacenter} — unexpected error: {exc}",
            exc_info=True
        )
        current = (_get_job_status(job_id) or {}).get('progress', 0)
        update(current, 'Error', f'✕ Failed: {exc}')
        finish('Error', f'Unexpected error: {exc}')


# ── API: job status polling ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

@login_required
@require_http_methods(["GET"])
def bc_job_status(request, job_id):
    """Polled by the JS frontend every 800ms. Returns the current job status."""
    status = _get_job_status(str(job_id))
    if status is None:
        return JsonResponse({'error': 'Job not found or expired'}, status=404)
    return JsonResponse(status)

================================================================================
# changes.py
================================================================================
"""
changes.py
----------
Build a flat summary of ServerUnique changes from global_history, enriched with
the real values stored in action_during_lp_history / annotation_history.

Shared by the export_bc_changes management command and the changes_summary view.
Servers are read in keyset-paginated chunks (hostname > last) so that memory and
query size stay bounded on both SQLite and PostgreSQL, without server-side cursors.
"""

import csv
import datetime
import json
import re

from .models import ServerUnique


TRACKED_FIELDS = [
    'priority_asset',
    'in_live_play',
    'action_during_lp',
    'annotation',
    'cluster',
    'cluster_type',
    'bc_exception',
]

# Fields whose real values live in a separate text history: "[YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS - user] text"
TEXT_HISTORY_FIELDS = {
    'action_during_lp': 'action_during_lp_history',
    'annotation': 'annotation_history',
}

SOURCES = [
    'Single Edit',
    'Bulk Edit',
    'CSV Import',
    'Application CSV Import',
    'BC Rules',
    'BC Rules Reset',
    'DR Reset',
    '(history only)',
]

HEADERS = ['date', 'user', 'source', 'hostname'] + TRACKED_FIELDS + ['other_changes']

DEFAULT_CHUNK_SIZE = 500

HISTORY_LINE_RE = re.compile(r'^\[(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2} \d{2}:\d{2}:\d{2}) - (.*?)\]\s?(.*)$')
DATE_FMT = '%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'
NOT_MODIFIED = 'Not set or not modified'
# Text history and global_history timestamps are computed separately and can differ by a second or two
MATCH_TOLERANCE = datetime.timedelta(seconds=5)


def clean_value(value):
    """Flatten a value to a single CSV-safe line."""
    if value is None:
        return ''
    text = str(value)
    text = text.replace('\r\n', ' ').replace('\n', ' ').replace('\r', ' ')
    return ' '.join(text.split())


def parse_date(value):
    """Parse a history timestamp, return None if malformed."""
    try:
        return datetime.datetime.strptime(value, DATE_FMT)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def date_bounds(d_from, d_to):
    """Return (start, end) datetimes covering both dates inclusively."""
    return (datetime.datetime.combine(d_from, datetime.time.min),
            datetime.datetime.combine(d_to, datetime.time.max))


def parse_text_history(text):
    """Parse a *_history text field into a list of {date, user, text, used} dicts."""
    entries = []
    for line in (text or '').splitlines():
        m = HISTORY_LINE_RE.match(line)
        if m:
            entries.append({
                'date': parse_date(m.group(1)),
                'user': m.group(2),
                'text': m.group(3),
                'used': False,
            })
        elif entries and line.strip():
            # Continuation line of a multi-line value
            entries[-1]['text'] += ' ' + line.strip()
    return [e for e in entries if e['date']]


def find_text_entry(entries, date, user):
    """Return the closest unused text history entry for the same user within tolerance."""
    best = None
    for e in entries:
        if e['used'] or e['user'] != user:
            continue
        delta = abs(e['date'] - date)
        if delta <= MATCH_TOLERANCE and (best is None or delta < abs(best['date'] - date)):
            best = e
    if best:
        best['used'] = True
    return best


def format_change(change):
    """Return 'old -> new', or '' when the change is a no-op."""
    if not isinstance(change, dict):
        return clean_value(change)
    if 'old' in change or 'new' in change:
        old, new = change.get('old'), change.get('new')
        if new == NOT_MODIFIED or old == new:
            return ''
        return f"{clean_value(old)} -> {clean_value(new)}"
    info = change.get('info', '')
    if NOT_MODIFIED in str(info):
        return ''
    return clean_value(info)


def rows_for_server(hostname, global_history, text_histories_raw, start, end):
    """Build change rows for one server within [start, end], newest first."""
    if isinstance(global_history, str):
        try:
            global_history = json.loads(global_history)
        except ValueError:
            global_history = []
    if not isinstance(global_history, list):
        global_history = []

    text_histories = {
        field: [e for e in parse_text_history(text_histories_raw.get(field)) if start <= e['date'] <= end]
        for field in TEXT_HISTORY_FIELDS
    }

    rows = []
    for entry in global_history:
        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
            continue
        date = parse_date(entry.get('date'))
        if not date or not (start <= date <= end):
            continue
        user = entry.get('user', '')
        row = dict.fromkeys(HEADERS, '')
        row.update(date=entry['date'], user=user, source=entry.get('fromsource', ''), hostname=hostname)

        others = []
        for field, change in (entry.get('changes') or {}).items():
            value = format_change(change)
            if field in TEXT_HISTORY_FIELDS and value and not (isinstance(change, dict) and 'old' in change):
                # Value is only "check its history": fetch the real text
                match = find_text_entry(text_histories[field], date, user)
                if match:
                    value = clean_value(match['text'])
            if not value:
                continue
            if field in TRACKED_FIELDS:
                row[field] = value
            else:
                others.append(value if field == 'history_wiped' else f"{field}: {value}")
        row['other_changes'] = ' | '.join(others)

        if any(row[f] for f in TRACKED_FIELDS) or row['other_changes']:
            rows.append(row)

    # Text history lines with no matching global_history entry (old data, wiped history...)
    for field, entries in text_histories.items():
        for e in entries:
            if e['used']:
                continue
            row = dict.fromkeys(HEADERS, '')
            row.update(date=e['date'].strftime(DATE_FMT), user=e['user'],
                       source='(history only)', hostname=hostname)
            row[field] = clean_value(e['text'])
            rows.append(row)

    rows.sort(key=lambda r: r['date'], reverse=True)
    return rows


def iter_server_chunks(hostname=None, chunk_size=DEFAULT_CHUNK_SIZE):
    """Yield lists of server tuples, keyset-paginated on the unique hostname column."""
    qs = ServerUnique.objects.order_by('hostname')
    if hostname:
        qs = qs.filter(hostname__icontains=hostname)
    columns = ['hostname', 'global_history'] + list(TEXT_HISTORY_FIELDS.values())

    last = None
    while True:
        page = qs.filter(hostname__gt=last) if last is not None else qs
        chunk = list(page.values_list(*columns)[:chunk_size])
        if not chunk:
            return
        yield chunk
        last = chunk[-1][0]


def iter_change_row_chunks(d_from, d_to, hostname=None, user=None, source=None,
                           chunk_size=DEFAULT_CHUNK_SIZE):
    """Yield lists of change rows, one list per server chunk, ordered by hostname then date desc."""
    start, end = date_bounds(d_from, d_to)
    user = (user or '').strip().lower()

    for chunk in iter_server_chunks(hostname, chunk_size):
        rows = []
        for host, gh, *texts in chunk:
            raw = dict(zip(TEXT_HISTORY_FIELDS.keys(), texts))
            for row in rows_for_server(host, gh, raw, start, end):
                if user and user not in row['user'].lower():
                    continue
                if source and row['source'] != source:
                    continue
                rows.append(row)
        yield rows


class _Echo:
    """File-like object whose write() returns the value, for streaming csv.writer output."""

    def write(self, value):
        return value


def iter_csv_lines(row_chunks):
    """Yield CSV text (BOM + header, then one block per chunk) for StreamingHttpResponse or a file."""
    writer = csv.DictWriter(_Echo(), fieldnames=HEADERS, delimiter=';')
    yield '﻿' + writer.writeheader()
    for rows in row_chunks:
        if rows:
            yield ''.join(writer.writerow(r) for r in rows)

================================================================================

# forms.py
================================================================================

from django import forms
from .models import ServerUnique
import datetime


# Form for Business Continuity Information
class BusinessContinuityInformationForm(forms.ModelForm):
    
    # Choices for priority asset, live play and cluster fields
    CHOICES_LIVE_PLAY = [
        ('YES', 'YES'),
        ('NO', 'NO'),
        ('EMPTY', 'EMPTY'),
    ]

    # Field for Priority Asset
    priority_asset = forms.ChoiceField( 
        choices=CHOICES_LIVE_PLAY,
        required=False,
        label="Is a Priority Asset",
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={'class': 'select-box'})
    )

    # Field for the Live Play status
    in_live_play = forms.ChoiceField( 
        choices=CHOICES_LIVE_PLAY,
        required=False,
        label="In Live Play",
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={'class': 'select-box'})
    )
    
    # Field for Cluster
    cluster = forms.ChoiceField( 
        choices=CHOICES_LIVE_PLAY,
        required=False,
        label="Cluster",
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={'class': 'select-box'})
    )

    # Field for Action During Live Play History
    action_during_lp_history = forms.CharField(
        widget=forms.Textarea(attrs={'rows': 4, 'readonly': 'readonly', 'class': 'history-display'}),
        required=False,
        label="Action During Live Play Historic"
    )
    
    # Field for Annotation History
    annotation_history = forms.CharField(
        widget=forms.Textarea(attrs={'rows': 4, 'readonly': 'readonly', 'class': 'history-display'}),
        required=False,
        label="Annotation Historic"
    )
    

    # Field for the BC exception: the server is left alone by BC rules on the affected site
    bc_exception = forms.ChoiceField(
        choices=CHOICES_LIVE_PLAY,
        required=False,
        label="BC Exception",
        widget=forms.Select(attrs={'class': 'select-box'})
    )

    # Meta class to specify the model and fields
    class Meta:
        model = ServerUnique
        fields = ['priority_asset', 'in_live_play', 'action_during_lp', 'annotation', 'cluster', 'cluster_type', 'bc_exception']


    def __init__(self, *args, loggedonuser=None, **kwargs):
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        
        self.loggedonuser = loggedonuser

        # Store original values for comparison
        self.previous_priority_asset = self.instance.priority_asset
        self.previous_in_live_play = self.instance.in_live_play
        self.previous_cluster = self.instance.cluster
        self.previous_cluster_type = self.instance.cluster_type
        self.previous_bc_exception = self.instance.bc_exception
        self.previous_action_during_lp = self.instance.action_during_lp
        self.previous_action_during_lp_history = self.instance.action_during_lp_history
        self.previous_annotation = self.instance.annotation
        self.previous_annotation_history = self.instance.annotation_history
            
        if self.instance and self.instance.pk:
            self.fields['action_during_lp_history'].initial = self.instance.action_during_lp_history
            self.fields['annotation_history'].initial = self.instance.annotation_history

        # Update form attributes for action_during_lp, annotation fields and cluster
        self.fields['action_during_lp'].widget.attrs.update({ 
            'placeholder': 'Enter the Action During LP...',
            'rows': 1
        })
            
        self.fields['annotation'].widget.attrs.update({ 
            'placeholder': 'Enter the Annotation...',
            'rows': 1
        })

        self.fields['cluster_type'].widget.attrs.update({ 
            'placeholder': 'Enter the Cluster Type...',
            'rows': 1
        })
        

    # Save method to handle form submission
    def save(self, commit=True):

        instance = super().save(commit=False)

        now_date = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        now_usr = self.loggedonuser
        now_str = f"[{now_date} - {now_usr}]"
        
        # Create a new history entry
        history_entry = {
            "date": now_date,
            "user": now_usr,
            "fromsource": "Single Edit",
            "changes": {}
        }

        # Update the Priority Asset field
        priority_asset_choice = self.cleaned_data.get('priority_asset')
        if priority_asset_choice:
            instance.priority_asset = priority_asset_choice
        else:  # nothing chosen: keep the stored value
            instance.priority_asset = self.previous_priority_asset
        history_entry["changes"]["priority_asset"] = {
            "old": self.previous_priority_asset,
            "new": self.instance.priority_asset if self.previous_priority_asset != self.instance.priority_asset else 'Not set or not modified'
        }
            
        # Update the Live Play status field
        in_live_play_choice = self.cleaned_data.get('in_live_play')
        if in_live_play_choice:
            instance.in_live_play = in_live_play_choice
        else:  # nothing chosen: keep the stored value
            instance.in_live_play = self.previous_in_live_play
        history_entry["changes"]["in_live_play"] = {
            "old": self.previous_in_live_play,
            "new": self.instance.in_live_play if self.previous_in_live_play != self.instance.in_live_play else 'Not set or not modified'
        }

        # Update the Action During Live Play and Action During Live Play History fields
        action_during_lp = self.cleaned_data.get('action_during_lp')
        if action_during_lp and action_during_lp != self.previous_action_during_lp:  # Check for non-empty string and compare with the previous value
            instance.action_during_lp = action_during_lp
            instance.action_during_lp_history = f"{now_str} {action_during_lp}\n" + (instance.action_during_lp_history or '')
            history_entry["changes"]["action_during_lp"] = {
                "info": "Has been edited, check its history"
            }
        else:
            instance.action_during_lp = self.previous_action_during_lp
            instance.action_during_lp_history = self.previous_action_during_lp_history
            history_entry["changes"]["action_during_lp"] = {
                "info": "Not set or not modified"
            }            

        # Update the Annotation and Annotation History fields
        annotation = self.cleaned_data.get('annotation')
        if annotation and annotation != self.previous_annotation:  # Check for non-empty string and compare with the previous value
            instance.annotation = annotation
            instance.annotation_history = f"{now_str} {annotation}\n" + (instance.annotation_history or '')
            history_entry["changes"]["annotation"] = {
                "info": "Has been edited, check its history"
            }            
        else:
            instance.annotation = self.previous_annotation
            instance.annotation_history = self.previous_annotation_history
            history_entry["changes"]["annotation"] = {
                "info": "Not set or not modified"
            }                 
        
        # Update the Cluster field
        cluster = self.cleaned_data.get('cluster')
        if cluster:
            instance.cluster = cluster
        else:  # nothing chosen: keep the stored value
            instance.cluster = self.previous_cluster
        history_entry["changes"]["cluster"] = {
            "old": self.previous_cluster,
            "new": self.instance.cluster if self.previous_cluster != self.instance.cluster else 'Not set or not modified'
        }

        # Update the Cluster Type field
        cluster_type = self.cleaned_data.get('cluster_type')
        if cluster_type:
            instance.cluster_type = cluster_type
        history_entry["changes"]["cluster_type"] = {
            "old": self.previous_cluster_type,
            "new": self.instance.cluster_type if self.previous_cluster_type != self.instance.cluster_type else 'Not set or not modified'
        }

        # Update the BC Exception field
        bc_exception_choice = self.cleaned_data.get('bc_exception')
        if bc_exception_choice:
            instance.bc_exception = bc_exception_choice
        else:
            instance.bc_exception = self.previous_bc_exception
        history_entry["changes"]["bc_exception"] = {
            "old": self.previous_bc_exception,
            "new": instance.bc_exception if self.previous_bc_exception != instance.bc_exception else 'Not set or not modified'
        }

        # Only add the history entry if there are changes
        if history_entry["changes"]:
            print("ici")
            instance.global_history.append(history_entry)
            
        if commit:
            instance.save()
        return instance


# Form for CSV file upload
class CsvUploadForm(forms.Form):
    csv_file = forms.FileField(label="Select a CSV file", widget=forms.FileInput(attrs={'accept': '.csv'}))
    

================================================================================
# management/commands/export_businesscontinuity.py
================================================================================
import csv
import datetime
import json
import os
import sys

from businesscontinuity.models import Server, ServerUnique
from openpyxl import Workbook
from openpyxl.utils import get_column_letter

export_pathname="/tmp"
export_filename="businesscontinuity_servers_export"
LOG_PATH="/data/DPR_DATA/logs/businesscontinuity_servers_export.log"


def write_log(message):
    print(message)
    with open(LOG_PATH, 'a') as log_file:
        log_file.write(message + '\n')


def export_to_xlsx(servers, file_path):
    wb = Workbook()
    ws = wb.active
    ws.title = 'Servers Data'

    headers = [
        'SERVER_ID', 'ITCONTINUITY_LEVEL', 'DAP_NAME', 'DAP_AUID', 'DATACENTER', 'TECH_FAMILY', 'MACHINE_TYPE', 'VM_TYPE', 'AFFINITY', 'DATABASE_TECHNO', 'DATABASE_DB_CI', 'VITAL_LEVEL',
        'SUPPORT_GROUP', 'APPLICATION_SUPPORT_GROUP', 'IT_CLUSTER', 'COUNTRY', 'priority_asset', 'in_live_play', 'action_during_lp', 'annotation', 'cluster', 'cluster_type', 'bc_exception'
    ]

    ws.append(headers)

    for server in servers:
        server_unique = server.server_unique
        data = [
            server.SERVER_ID, server.ITCONTINUITY_LEVEL, server.DAP_NAME, server.DAP_AUID, server.DATACENTER, server.TECH_FAMILY, server.MACHINE_TYPE, server.VM_TYPE, 
            server.AFFINITY, server.DATABASE_TECHNO, server.DATABASE_DB_CI, server.VITAL_LEVEL, server.SUPPORT_GROUP, server.APPLICATION_SUPPORT_GROUP, server.IT_CLUSTER, server.COUNTRY, 
            server_unique.priority_asset, server_unique.in_live_play, server_unique.action_during_lp, server_unique.annotation, server_unique.cluster, server_unique.cluster_type, server_unique.bc_exception
        ]
        ws.append(data)

    for col in ws.columns:
        max_length = 0
        column = col[0].column_letter
        for cell in col:
            try:
                if len(str(cell.value)) > max_length:
                    max_length = len(cell.value)
            except:
                pass
        adjusted_width = (max_length + 2)
        ws.column_dimensions[column].width = adjusted_width

    wb.save(file_path)


def export_to_csv(servers, file_path):
    data = []
    for server in servers:
        server_unique = server.server_unique
        data.append({
            'SERVER_ID': server.SERVER_ID,
            'ITCONTINUITY_LEVEL': server.ITCONTINUITY_LEVEL,
            'DAP_NAME': server.DAP_NAME,
            'DAP_AUID': server.DAP_AUID,
            'DATACENTER': server.DATACENTER,
            'TECH_FAMILY': server.TECH_FAMILY,
            'MACHINE_TYPE': server.MACHINE_TYPE,
            'VM_TYPE': server.VM_TYPE,
            'AFFINITY': server.AFFINITY,
            'DATABASE_TECHNO': server.DATABASE_TECHNO,
            'DATABASE_DB_CI': server.DATABASE_DB_CI,
            'VITAL_LEVEL': server.VITAL_LEVEL,
            'SUPPORT_GROUP': server.SUPPORT_GROUP,
            'APPLICATION_SUPPORT_GROUP': server.APPLICATION_SUPPORT_GROUP,
            'IT_CLUSTER': server.IT_CLUSTER,
            'COUNTRY': server.COUNTRY,
            'priority_asset': server_unique.priority_asset,
            'in_live_play': server_unique.in_live_play,
            'action_during_lp': server_unique.action_during_lp,
            'annotation': server_unique.annotation,
            'cluster': server_unique.cluster,
            'cluster_type': server_unique.cluster_type,
            'bc_exception': server_unique.bc_exception
        })

    # Write data to CSV file
    with open(file_path, 'w', newline='', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=data[0].keys(), delimiter=';')
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(data)


def export_to_json(servers, file_path):
    servers_list = []
    for server in servers:
        server_unique = server.server_unique
        data = {
            'SERVER_ID': server.SERVER_ID,
            'ITCONTINUITY_LEVEL': server.ITCONTINUITY_LEVEL,
            'DAP_NAME': server.DAP_NAME,
            'DAP_AUID': server.DAP_AUID,
            'DATACENTER': server.DATACENTER,
            'TECH_FAMILY': server.TECH_FAMILY,
            'MACHINE_TYPE': server.MACHINE_TYPE,
            'VM_TYPE': server.VM_TYPE,
            'AFFINITY': server.AFFINITY,
            'DATABASE_TECHNO': server.DATABASE_TECHNO,
            'DATABASE_DB_CI': server.DATABASE_DB_CI,
            'VITAL_LEVEL': server.VITAL_LEVEL,
            'SUPPORT_GROUP': server.SUPPORT_GROUP,
            'APPLICATION_SUPPORT_GROUP': server.APPLICATION_SUPPORT_GROUP,
            'IT_CLUSTER': server.IT_CLUSTER,
            'COUNTRY': server.COUNTRY,
            'priority_asset': server_unique.priority_asset,
            'in_live_play': server_unique.in_live_play,
            'action_during_lp': server_unique.action_during_lp,
            'annotation': server_unique.annotation,
            'cluster': server_unique.cluster,
            'cluster_type': server_unique.cluster_type,
            'bc_exception': server_unique.bc_exception
        }
        servers_list.append(data)

    with open(file_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as jsonfile:
        json.dump(servers_list, jsonfile, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)


def export_businesscontinuity(format):
    write_log("----------------------------------------------------------")
    write_log(f"Started at {datetime.datetime.now()}")
    servers = Server.objects.all().select_related('server_unique').order_by('SERVER_ID')

    if not servers.exists():
        write_log("No servers matching the applied filters were found.")
        return False, "No servers matching the applied filters were found."

    timestamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
    file_path = f'{export_pathname}/{export_filename}.{format}'

    if format == 'xlsx':
        export_to_xlsx(servers, file_path)
    elif format == 'csv':
        export_to_csv(servers, file_path)
    elif format == 'json':
        export_to_json(servers, file_path)

    write_log(f"Ended at {datetime.datetime.now()}")
    write_log(f'Saved {len(servers)} servers to {file_path}')
    return True, f'Saved {len(servers)} servers to {file_path}'


if __name__ == '__main__':
    if len(sys.argv) != 2 or sys.argv[1] not in ['xlsx', 'csv', 'json']:
        write_log('Invalid or missing export format argument.')
        sys.exit(1)
    export_businesscontinuity(sys.argv[1])

# models.py

from django.db import models

class ServerUnique(models.Model):

    # Represents the unique server, identified by his hostname.

    hostname = models.CharField(max_length=255, unique=True, verbose_name="Unique hostname")
    
    priority_asset = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, null=True, verbose_name="Priority Asset")
    in_live_play = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, null=True, verbose_name="In Live Play")
    action_during_lp = models.TextField(blank=True, null=True, verbose_name="Action during Live Play")
    action_during_lp_history = models.TextField(blank=True, null=True, verbose_name="Action during Live Play History")
    annotation = models.TextField(blank=True, null=True, verbose_name="Annotation")
    annotation_history = models.TextField(blank=True, null=True, verbose_name="Annotation History")
    cluster = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, null=True, verbose_name="Cluster")
    cluster_type = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, null=True, verbose_name="Cluster Type")
    bc_exception = models.CharField(max_length=255, blank=True, null=True, default='EMPTY', verbose_name="BC Exception")
    global_history = models.JSONField(default=list, help_text="Historical entries")

    def __str__(self):
        return self.hostname

    def in_exception(self) -> bool:
        """True when the server is a BC exception: BC rules leave it alone on the affected site."""
        return (self.bc_exception or '').strip().upper() == 'YES'

    def append_global_history(self, changes: dict, username: str, source: str) -> None:
        """
        Append a structured entry to global_history.
        Matches the format produced by BusinessContinuityInformationForm.save()
        so that the history view is consistent regardless of the edit source.

        Args:
            changes  : dict of field-level change descriptors, e.g.:
                       { 'priority_asset': {'old': 'YES', 'new': 'NO'} }
                       { 'action_during_lp': {'info': 'Updated, check its history'} }
            username : user or process name (e.g. request.user.username, 'BC Rules')
            source   : human-readable source label:
                       'Single Edit' | 'Bulk Edit' | 'CSV Import' |
                       'Application CSV Import' | 'BC Rules'

        Notes:
            - No-ops silently if changes is empty.
            - Caps global_history at 500 entries to prevent unbounded growth.
            - Does NOT call self.save() — caller is responsible for saving,
              typically via bulk_update(..., ['global_history']).
        """
        import datetime

        if not changes:
            return

        entry = {
            'date':       datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'),
            'user':       username,
            'fromsource': source,
            'changes':    changes,
        }

        gh = self.global_history if isinstance(self.global_history, list) else []
        gh.insert(0, entry)
        self.global_history = gh[:500]

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Unique server"
        verbose_name_plural = "Unique servers"


class Server(models.Model):

    # Server info. Its ID is automatically generated by Django as primary key
    
    # Link to the ServerUnique information to display information relative to the hostname
    server_unique = models.ForeignKey(
        ServerUnique,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE, # if the server is deleted, its entries in ServerUnique are deleted too
        related_name='server_info', # Gives the possibility to access to Server information from ServerUnique
        verbose_name="Associated Server (Unique)"
    )

    SERVER_ID = models.CharField(max_length=100, default='UNKNOWN', db_index=True)
    ITCONTINUITY_LEVEL = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    DAP_NAME = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    DAP_AUID = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    DATACENTER = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    ENVIRONMENT = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    TECH_FAMILY = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    MACHINE_TYPE = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    VM_TYPE = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    AFFINITY = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    DATABASE_TECHNO = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    DATABASE_DB_CI = models.CharField(max_length=255, null=True, blank=True)
    VITAL_LEVEL = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    SUPPORT_GROUP = models.CharField(max_length=255, null=True, blank=True)
    APPLICATION_SUPPORT_GROUP = models.CharField(max_length=255, null=True, blank=True)
    IT_CLUSTER = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    COUNTRY = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)


    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.SERVER_ID} - App: {self.DAP_NAME} (ID: {self.id})"

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Server entry"
        verbose_name_plural = "Server entries"

  
class ServerStaging(models.Model):

    # Server info. Its ID is automatically generated by Django as primary key
    
    # Link to the ServerUnique information to display information relative to the hostname
    server_unique = models.ForeignKey(
        ServerUnique,
        on_delete=models.CASCADE, # if the server is deleted, its entries in ServerUnique are deleted too
        related_name='server_info_staging', # Gives the possibility to access to Server information from ServerUnique
        verbose_name="Associated Server (Unique)"
    )

    SERVER_ID = models.CharField(max_length=100, default='UNKNOWN', db_index=True)
    ITCONTINUITY_LEVEL = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    DAP_NAME = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    DAP_AUID = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    DATACENTER = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    ENVIRONMENT = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    TECH_FAMILY = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    MACHINE_TYPE = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    VM_TYPE = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    AFFINITY = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    DATABASE_TECHNO = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    DATABASE_DB_CI = models.CharField(max_length=255, null=True, blank=True)
    VITAL_LEVEL = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    SUPPORT_GROUP = models.CharField(max_length=255, null=True, blank=True)
    APPLICATION_SUPPORT_GROUP = models.CharField(max_length=255, null=True, blank=True)
    IT_CLUSTER = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)
    COUNTRY = models.CharField(max_length=100, null=True, blank=True)


    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.SERVER_ID} - App: {self.DAP_NAME} (ID: {self.id})"

    class Meta:
        verbose_name = "Server entry Staging"
        verbose_name_plural = "Server entries Staging"
        managed = False


class ImportStatus(models.Model):
    date_import = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    success = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    message = models.TextField(blank=True, null=True)
    nb_entries_created = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    source_url = models.URLField(blank=True, null=True)

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{'OK' if self.success else 'KO'} {self.date_import.strftime('%d.%m.%Y %H:%M')}"


class CsvImportLog(models.Model):
    """One line per CSV import launched from the import page."""

    date = models.DateTimeField(auto_now_add=True)
    username = models.CharField(max_length=150)
    filename = models.CharField(max_length=255)
    file_format = models.CharField(max_length=50)
    updated = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    unchanged = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    ignored = models.IntegerField(default=0)
    failed = models.BooleanField(default=False)
    message = models.TextField(blank=True)
    report_csv = models.TextField(blank=True)  # server by server details, kept for the latest imports only

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.date:%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} {self.username} {self.filename}"


class BCRuleTexts(models.Model):
    """
    Stores the user-defined action texts for each BC decision rule.
    One row per rule_key — updated in place each time the wizard is applied.
    A single set of texts is shared across all datacenters (one DR at a time).
    """

    rule_key    = models.CharField(
        max_length=50,
        unique=True,
        db_index=True,
        help_text="BC rule identifier — matches constants in bc_rules_logic.py",
    )
    action_text = models.TextField(
        help_text="Text written into action_during_lp for servers matching this rule",
    )
    updated_at  = models.DateTimeField(auto_now=True)
    updated_by  = models.CharField(
        max_length=150,
        blank=True,
        help_text="Username of the last editor",
    )

    class Meta:
        verbose_name        = "BC Rule Text"
        verbose_name_plural = "BC Rule Texts"
        ordering            = ["rule_key"]

    def __str__(self):
        return f"{self.rule_key}: {self.action_text[:60]}"

# static/businesscontinuity/js/servers.js
================================================================================
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {  // Wait for the DOM to be fully loaded before executing the script

    // Get needed references
    const editModalOverlay = document.getElementById('editModalOverlay');
    const closeModalButton = document.getElementById('closeModalButton');
    const cancelModalButton = document.getElementById('cancelModalButton');
    const editForm = document.getElementById('editForm');
    var serversTable = document.getElementById('serversTable');
    var userCanEdit = serversTable.dataset.userCanedit === 'True';

    if (userCanEdit) {

        // Close button inside bulk edit modal
        const bulkModalClose = document.getElementById('bulkEditModal')
                                       .querySelector('.close-button');
        if (bulkModalClose) {
            bulkModalClose.addEventListener('click', closeBulkModal);
        }

        // Standalone bulk edit button — may no longer exist if grouped under Edit dropdown
        const bulkEditBtn = document.getElementById('bulkEditButton');
        if (bulkEditBtn) {
            bulkEditBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {
                document.getElementById('bulkEditModal').style.display = 'flex';
            });
        }

    }

    
    // Check the stored value of a YES/NO/EMPTY field; an unknown value checks nothing and is kept on save
    function setEditChoice(name, value) {
        const wanted = (value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        document.querySelectorAll(`#editForm input[name="${name}"]`).forEach(radio => {
            radio.checked = radio.value === wanted;
        });
    }


    function openModal() {  // Open the edit modal and focus on the first input field in the form
        editModalOverlay.style.display = 'flex';
        const firstInput = document.querySelector('#editForm input[name="priority_asset"]:checked')
            || document.querySelector('#editForm input[name="priority_asset"]');
        if (firstInput) firstInput.focus();
    }

    
    function closeModal() {  // Close the edit modal
        editModalOverlay.style.display = 'none';
    }

    
    document.querySelectorAll('.edit-button').forEach(button => {  // Add an event listener to each edit button

        button.addEventListener('click', function () {

            currentServerId = this.dataset.serverId;
            const editForm = document.getElementById('editForm');
            const historyContainer = document.getElementById('historyContainer');
            if (historyContainer.style.display === 'block') {
                historyContainer.style.display = 'none';
                editForm.style.display = 'block';
                document.getElementById('historyButton').style.display = 'inline-block';
            }

            const hostnameUnique = this.dataset.serverId;  // Get the hostname unique ID from the button's dataset
            const row = this.closest('tr');  // Get the row that the button is in
            const priorityAsset = row.dataset.priorityAsset;  // Get the values for the row from the dataset
            const inLivePlay = row.dataset.inLivePlay;
            const actionDuringLp = row.dataset.actionDuringLp;
            const actionDuringLpHistory = row.dataset.actionDuringLpHistory;
            const actionDuringLpHistoryLines = actionDuringLpHistory.split('\n').filter(line => !line.includes('EMPTY'));
            const annotation = row.dataset.annotation;
            const annotationHistory = row.dataset.annotationHistory;
            const annotationHistoryLines = annotationHistory.split('\n').filter(line => !line.includes('EMPTY'));
            const cluster = row.dataset.cluster;
            const clusterType = row.dataset.clusterType;
            const bcException = row.dataset.bcException;

            // Inject the values into the edit form
            document.getElementById('editModalLabel').innerText = `Edit the information for ${hostnameUnique}`;
            setEditChoice('priority_asset', priorityAsset);
            setEditChoice('in_live_play', inLivePlay);
            document.getElementById('id_action_during_lp').value = actionDuringLp === 'EMPTY' ? '' : actionDuringLp;
            document.getElementById('id_annotation_history').value = annotationHistoryLines.join('\n');
            document.getElementById('id_annotation').value = annotation === 'EMPTY' ? '' : annotation;
            document.getElementById('id_action_during_lp_history').value = actionDuringLpHistoryLines.join('\n');
            setEditChoice('cluster', cluster);
            document.getElementById('id_cluster_type').value = clusterType === 'EMPTY' ? '' : clusterType;
            setEditChoice('bc_exception', bcException);

            if (!userCanEdit) {
                // Set all input fields to readonly
                document.querySelectorAll('#editForm input, #editForm select, #editForm textarea').forEach(field => {
                    field.readOnly = true;
                    field.disabled = true; // Disable select and textarea fields
                });
                document.querySelector('#editForm button[type="submit"]').style.display = 'none';
                document.querySelector('#editForm .btn-secondary').innerText = 'Close';
            }
            else {
                editForm.action = `/${appname}/edit/${hostnameUnique}/`;  // Set the form action to the edit URL for the hostname unique ID
            }
            
            openModal();
            
        });

    });

    
    closeModalButton.addEventListener('click', function () {  // Event listener to the close button in the edit modal
        closeModal();
        closeBulkModal();
    });

    
    cancelModalButton.addEventListener('click', function () {  // Event listener to the cancel button in the edit modal
        closeModal();
        closeBulkModal();
    });

    
    editModalOverlay.addEventListener('click', function (event) {  // Event listener to the edit modal overlay: If the user clicks on the overlay, close the modal
        if (event.target === editModalOverlay) {
            closeModal();
        }
    });

    
    document.getElementById('bulkEditModal').addEventListener('click', function (event) {  // Event listener to the bulk edit modal: If the user clicks on the modal background, close the modal
        if (event.target === this) {
            closeBulkModal();
        }
    });

    
    document.getElementById('bulkEditModal').querySelector('.btn-secondary').addEventListener('click', closeBulkModal);  // Event listener to the secondary button in the bulk edit modal


    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {  // Event listener for the Escape key
        if (e.key === 'Escape') {
            closeModal();
            closeBulkModal();
        }
    });
    

    document.getElementById('historyButton').addEventListener('click', function() {
    
        const editForm = document.getElementById('editForm');
        const historyContainer = document.getElementById('historyContainer');
        const historyButton = document.getElementById('historyButton');

        // Display the historic
        historyContainer.style.display = 'block';
        editForm.style.display = 'none';
        historyButton.style.display = 'none';

        // Get the current server ID
        const serverId = currentServerId

        const fieldNames = {
            "priority_asset": "Priority Asset",
            "in_live_play": "In Live Play",
            "action_during_lp": "Action During LP",
            "annotation": "Annotation",
            "cluster": "Cluster",
            "cluster_type": "Cluster Type",
            "bc_exception": "BC Exception",
            "history_wiped": "History"
        };

        // Get the historic data from the server
        fetch(`${getServerHistoryUrl}?server_id=${serverId}`)
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                const historyList = document.getElementById('historyList');
                historyList.innerHTML = '';

                // Sort the historic entries by descending date
                data.sort((a, b) => new Date(b.date) - new Date(a.date));

                data.forEach(entry => {
                    const listItem = document.createElement('li');
                    listItem.classList.add('history-entry');
                    listItem.textContent = `${entry.date} - ${entry.user} - ${entry.fromsource}`;
                    const changesList = document.createElement('ul');
                    changesList.classList.add('changes-list');
                    Object.keys(entry.changes).forEach(field => {
                        const changeItem = document.createElement('li');
                        changeItem.classList.add('change-item');
                        if (entry.changes[field].info) {
                                changeItem.textContent = `${fieldNames[field]}: ${entry.changes[field].info}`;
                            } else {
                                changeItem.textContent = `${fieldNames[field]}: ${entry.changes[field].new} (was ${entry.changes[field].old})`;
                            }
                        changesList.appendChild(changeItem);
                    });
                    listItem.appendChild(changesList);
                    historyList.appendChild(listItem);
                });
            });

    });


    document.getElementById('backToEditButton').addEventListener('click', function() {
        const editForm = document.getElementById('editForm');
        const historyContainer = document.getElementById('historyContainer');
        const historyButton = document.getElementById('historyButton');

        // Display the edit form
        historyContainer.style.display = 'none';
        editForm.style.display = 'block';
        historyButton.style.display = 'inline-block';
        
    }); 
    

});
