<script>
(function () {
'use strict';

// ── Data from Django ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const SAVED_TEXTS   = JSON.parse('{{ saved_texts|escapejs }}');
const DEFAULT_TEXTS = JSON.parse('{{ default_texts|escapejs }}');
const ALL_RULES     = JSON.parse('{{ all_rules|escapejs }}');
const CSRF          = '{{ csrf_token }}';
const IS_EDITOR     = {{ is_user_editor|yesno:"true,false" }};

const URL_SCOPE   = "{% url 'businesscontinuity:bc_scope' %}";
const URL_TEXTS   = "{% url 'businesscontinuity:bc_texts' %}";
const URL_PREVIEW = "{% url 'businesscontinuity:bc_preview' %}";
const URL_START   = "{% url 'businesscontinuity:bc_start' %}";
const URL_STATUS  = "{% url 'businesscontinuity:bc_job_status' job_id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' %}";

// Color per rule key (matches SVG result box colors)
const RULE_COLORS = {
    cluster_yes_lp_yes: '#ce93d8',
    cluster_yes_lp_no:  '#f48fb1',
    no_yes_phys:        '#90caf9',
    no_yes_virt_fixed:  '#90caf9',
    no_yes_virt_str:    '#ffd54f',
    no_no_phys:         '#a5d6a7',
    no_no_virt_fixed:   '#a5d6a7',
    no_no_virt_str:     '#ffd54f',
    hypervisor:         '#b0e3e6',
};
const SIBLING_COLOR = '#adb5bd';
const EXCEPTION_COLOR = '#b5179e';

const RULE_LABELS = {
    cluster_yes_lp_yes: 'A — Cluster YES · LP YES',
    cluster_yes_lp_no:  'B — Cluster YES · LP NO',
    no_yes_phys:        'C — Cluster NO · LP YES · Physical',
    no_yes_virt_fixed:  'D — Cluster NO · LP YES · Virtual Fixed',
    no_yes_virt_str:    'E — Cluster NO · LP YES · Virtual Stretched',
    no_no_phys:         'F — Cluster NO · LP NO · Physical',
    no_no_virt_fixed:   'G — Cluster NO · LP NO · Virtual Fixed',
    no_no_virt_str:     'H — Cluster NO · LP NO · Virtual Stretched',
    hypervisor:         'I — Machine Type HYPERVISOR',
};

// Letter (as used in the "Import Actions" CSV) -> rule key
const LETTER_TO_RULE = {
    A: 'cluster_yes_lp_yes',
    B: 'cluster_yes_lp_no',
    C: 'no_yes_phys',
    D: 'no_yes_virt_fixed',
    E: 'no_yes_virt_str',
    F: 'no_no_phys',
    G: 'no_no_virt_fixed',
    H: 'no_no_virt_str',
    I: 'hypervisor',
};

// ── State ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
let currentStep  = 1;
let selectedDC   = null;
let pollInterval = null;
let scopeRequest = 0;   // latest scope request, older responses are ignored
let scopeFilters = [];  // filters of the loaded scope, repeated in the final recap

// Server list filters, carried by the URL
const SCOPE_QUERY = window.location.search.slice(1);
// The same filters without the expected count, for the links back to the server list
const LIST_QUERY = (() => {
    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    params.delete('expected_servers');
    const text = params.toString();
    return text ? '?' + text : '';
})();

// ── Stepper ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function updateStepper() {
    for (let i = 1; i <= 4; i++) {
        const sc  = document.getElementById(`sc${i}`);
        const sl  = document.getElementById(`sl${i}`);
        const sep = document.getElementById(`sep${i}`);
        sc.className = 'step-circle';
        sl.className = 'step-label';
        if (sep) sep.className = 'step-sep';
        if (i < currentStep) {
            sc.classList.add('done'); sc.textContent = '✓';
            sl.classList.add('done');
            if (sep) sep.classList.add('done');
        } else if (i === currentStep) {
            sc.classList.add('active'); sc.textContent = i;
            sl.classList.add('active');
        } else {
            sc.textContent = i;
        }
    }
}

function goStep(n) {
    document.getElementById(`step${currentStep}`).classList.remove('visible');
    currentStep = n;
    document.getElementById(`step${currentStep}`).classList.add('visible');
    updateStepper();
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
    if (n === 2) loadDiagramSvg();
}

// ── Step 1: scope ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Selected sibling, null when the reset is disabled
function getSibling() {
    const enabled = document.getElementById('siblingEnabled');
    if (!enabled.checked) return null;
    return document.getElementById('siblingSelect').value || null;
}

function refreshNextButton(loading) {
    const missingSibling = document.getElementById('siblingEnabled').checked
        && !document.getElementById('siblingSelect').value;
    document.getElementById('btn1Next').disabled = !selectedDC || loading || missingSibling;
}

function showScopeError(message) {
    selectedDC = null;
    document.getElementById('scopeLoading').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('scopeContent').style.display = 'none';
    const err = document.getElementById('scopeError');
    err.textContent   = message;
    err.style.display = 'block';
    refreshNextButton(false);
}

// sibling: undefined uses the configured default, '' means no sibling
function fetchScope(sibling) {
    const requestId = ++scopeRequest;
    let url = `${URL_SCOPE}?query=${encodeURIComponent(SCOPE_QUERY)}`;
    if (sibling !== undefined) url += `&sibling=${encodeURIComponent(sibling)}`;
    refreshNextButton(true);

    return fetch(url)
        .then(r => r.json())
        .then(data => (requestId === scopeRequest ? data : null));
}

function loadScope() {
    selectedDC = null;
    document.getElementById('scopeLoading').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('scopeError').style.display   = 'none';
    document.getElementById('scopeContent').style.display = 'none';

    fetchScope()
        .then(data => {
            if (!data) return;
            if (data.error) { showScopeError(data.error); return; }
            renderScope(data);
        })
        .catch(() => showScopeError('Error loading the scope.'));
}

function renderScope(data) {
    selectedDC   = data.datacenter;
    scopeFilters = data.filters;
    document.getElementById('scopeLoading').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('scopeContent').style.display = 'block';

    document.getElementById('scopeDatacenter').textContent = data.datacenter;
    document.getElementById('scopeFilters').textContent =
        data.filters.map(f => `${f.label} = ${f.value}`).join(' · ');
    document.getElementById('scopeCount').textContent      = data.count;
    document.getElementById('scopeCountTotal').textContent = data.count_total;

    const hasPermanent = !!data.permanent_filter;
    document.getElementById('scopePermanentLabel').style.display = hasPermanent ? '' : 'none';
    const permanent = document.getElementById('scopePermanent');
    permanent.style.display = hasPermanent ? '' : 'none';
    permanent.textContent   = data.permanent_filter || '';

    const select = document.getElementById('siblingSelect');
    select.innerHTML = '<option value="">Select a datacenter</option>' +
        data.options.map(d => `<option value="${escHtml(d)}">${escHtml(d)}</option>`).join('');
    select.value = data.default_sibling || '';
    document.getElementById('siblingEnabled').checked = !!data.default_sibling;

    renderSibling(data.sibling);
}

function renderSibling(stats) {
    const enabled = document.getElementById('siblingEnabled').checked;
    const info    = document.getElementById('siblingInfo');
    document.getElementById('siblingSelect').disabled = !enabled;

    if (!enabled) {
        info.textContent = 'No sibling reset: action_during_lp stays unchanged in every other datacenter.';
    } else if (!stats) {
        info.textContent = 'Select the sibling datacenter to reset.';
    } else {
        info.textContent =
            `${stats.total} servers in ${stats.datacenter}: ${stats.to_reset} will be reset to EMPTY, ` +
            'whatever the filters above.';
    }
    renderShared('sharedWarning', enabled ? stats : null);
    refreshNextButton(false);
}

// Warning for servers present on both sites: they get the rule, never the reset
function renderShared(elementId, stats) {
    const el = document.getElementById(elementId);
    if (!stats || !stats.shared_count) {
        el.style.display = 'none';
        return;
    }
    el.innerHTML =
        `<button type="button" class="shared-link">${stats.shared_count} server${stats.shared_count !== 1 ? 's are' : ' is'}</button> ` +
        `present in both ${escHtml(selectedDC)} and ${escHtml(stats.datacenter)}. ` +
        'BC rules still apply to those in scope, and none of them will be reset.';
    el.style.display = 'block';
    el.querySelector('.shared-link').addEventListener('click', () => {
        previewServersAll.shared = {
            servers:    stats.shared,
            truncated:  stats.shared_truncated,
            count:      stats.shared_count,
            actionText: `Servers in both ${selectedDC} and ${stats.datacenter}`,
            color:      '#ffc107',
        };
        openSrvModal('shared');
    });
}

// Warning for skipped servers: the job never touches their existing action
function renderSkipped(element, info) {
    if (!info || !info.count) {
        element.style.display = 'none';
        return;
    }
    element.innerHTML =
        `<button type="button" class="shared-link">${info.count} skipped server${info.count !== 1 ? 's keep' : ' keeps'}</button> ` +
        'an existing action because cluster or in_live_play is undetermined. ' +
        'Review them, and use Bulk Edit if the action is obsolete.';
    element.style.display = 'block';
    element.querySelector('.shared-link').addEventListener('click', () => {
        previewServersAll.skipped = {
            servers:    info.servers,
            truncated:  info.truncated,
            count:      info.count,
            actionText: 'Skipped servers keeping an existing action',
            color:      '#ffc107',
        };
        openSrvModal('skipped');
    });
}

function onSiblingChange() {
    const enabled = document.getElementById('siblingEnabled').checked;
    const value   = document.getElementById('siblingSelect').value;
    document.getElementById('siblingSelect').disabled = !enabled;

    if (!enabled || !value) {
        scopeRequest++;  // drop any pending response
        renderSibling(null);
        return;
    }
    document.getElementById('siblingInfo').textContent = 'Computing...';
    document.getElementById('sharedWarning').style.display = 'none';
    fetchScope(value)
        .then(data => {
            if (!data) return;
            if (data.error) { showScopeError(data.error); return; }
            renderSibling(data.sibling);
        })
        .catch(() => showScopeError('Error loading the sibling datacenter.'));
}

document.getElementById('siblingEnabled').addEventListener('change', onSiblingChange);
document.getElementById('siblingSelect').addEventListener('change', onSiblingChange);

// ── Step 2: texts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Debounce helper
function debounce(fn, delay) {
    let timer;
    return (...args) => { clearTimeout(timer); timer = setTimeout(() => fn(...args), delay); };
}

// Autosave rule texts to DB (debounced — fires 1.5s after last keystroke)
const autoSaveTexts = debounce(() => {
    fetch(URL_TEXTS, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': CSRF },
        body: JSON.stringify({ rule_texts: getFilledRuleTexts() }),
    }).catch(() => {}); // silent — non-critical
}, 1500);

function initTexts() {
    // Load saved texts (from DB via Django template variable)
    let hasSaved = false;
    ALL_RULES.forEach(key => {
        const inp = document.getElementById(`inp_${key}`);
        if (!inp) return;
        inp.value = SAVED_TEXTS[key] !== undefined
            ? SAVED_TEXTS[key]
            : (DEFAULT_TEXTS[key] || '');
        if (SAVED_TEXTS[key] !== undefined && SAVED_TEXTS[key] !== DEFAULT_TEXTS[key]) hasSaved = true;
        // Autosave on every change
        inp.addEventListener('input', autoSaveTexts);
        inp.addEventListener('input', refreshTextsError);
    });
    if (hasSaved) {
        document.getElementById('savedNotice').style.display = 'block';
    }
    refreshTextsError();
}

function restoreDefault(ruleKey) {
    const inp = document.getElementById(`inp_${ruleKey}`);
    if (!inp) return;
    inp.value = DEFAULT_TEXTS[ruleKey] || '';
    updateSvgCell(ruleKey, inp.value);
    refreshTextsError();
    autoSaveTexts();
}

function getRuleTexts() {
    const out = {};
    ALL_RULES.forEach(key => {
        const inp = document.getElementById(`inp_${key}`);
        out[key]  = inp ? inp.value.trim() : (DEFAULT_TEXTS[key] || '');
    });
    return out;
}

// Texts being typed can be blank for a moment: autosave only sends the filled ones
function getFilledRuleTexts() {
    const texts = getRuleTexts();
    return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(texts).filter(([, text]) => text));
}

// An action text can't be empty: EMPTY is the explicit way to ask for no action
function refreshTextsError() {
    const blank = ALL_RULES.filter(key => {
        const inp     = document.getElementById(`inp_${key}`);
        const isBlank = !!inp && !inp.value.trim();
        if (inp) inp.classList.toggle('invalid', isBlank);
        return isBlank;
    });
    const letters = blank.map(key => (RULE_LABELS[key] || key).split(' ')[0]);
    const error   = document.getElementById('textsError');
    error.textContent = blank.length
        ? `Empty action${blank.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}: ${letters.join(', ')}. Enter a text, or EMPTY if no action is wanted.`
        : '';
    error.style.display = blank.length ? 'block' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('btn2Preview').disabled = blank.length > 0;
}

// ── Import Actions (CSV: "Action;Text", one row per letter A-I) ────────────────
let pendingImportTexts = null;

// Parses and strictly validates the CSV. Returns { ok:true, texts } or { ok:false, errors }.
function parseActionsCsv(raw) {
    const lines = String(raw).split(/\r\n|\r|\n/).map(l => l.trim()).filter(l => l.length > 0);
    if (!lines.length) {
        return { ok: false, errors: ["The file is empty."] };
    }

    const letterRe = /^[A-Ia-i]$/;

    // Optional header row: skipped only if its first column isn't a valid letter.
    let dataLines  = lines;
    let lineOffset = 1;
    if (!letterRe.test(lines[0].split(';')[0].trim())) {
        dataLines  = lines.slice(1);
        lineOffset = 2;
    }
    if (!dataLines.length) {
        return { ok: false, errors: ["No data rows found after the header."] };
    }

    const errors = [];
    const seenAt = {};   // letter -> line number of first occurrence
    const texts  = {};   // rule_key -> text

    dataLines.forEach((line, idx) => {
        const lineNo  = idx + lineOffset;
        const sepIdx  = line.indexOf(';');
        if (sepIdx === -1) {
            errors.push(`Line ${lineNo}: missing ";" separator ("${line}").`);
            return;
        }

        const letterRaw = line.slice(0, sepIdx).trim();
        const letter     = letterRaw.toUpperCase();
        let text          = line.slice(sepIdx + 1).trim();
        if (text.length >= 2 && text.startsWith('"') && text.endsWith('"')) {
            text = text.slice(1, -1).trim();
        }

        if (!letterRe.test(letter)) {
            errors.push(`Line ${lineNo}: unknown action "${letterRaw}" (expected A to I).`);
            return;
        }
        if (seenAt[letter] !== undefined) {
            errors.push(`Line ${lineNo}: action "${letter}" is duplicated (already seen on line ${seenAt[letter]}).`);
            return;
        }
        seenAt[letter] = lineNo;

        if (!text) {
            errors.push(`Line ${lineNo} (action ${letter}): empty text.`);
            return;
        }

        texts[LETTER_TO_RULE[letter]] = text;
    });

    const missing = Object.keys(LETTER_TO_RULE).filter(l => seenAt[l] === undefined);
    if (missing.length) {
        errors.push(`Missing action(s) in the file: ${missing.join(', ')}.`);
    }

    if (errors.length) {
        return { ok: false, errors };
    }
    return { ok: true, texts };
}

function handleImportFile(event) {
    const file = event.target.files[0];
    event.target.value = ''; // allow re-selecting the same file next time
    if (!file) return;

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = () => {
        const result = parseActionsCsv(reader.result);
        if (!result.ok) {
            pendingImportTexts = null;
            renderImportModal('error', result.errors);
        } else {
            pendingImportTexts = result.texts;
            renderImportModal('confirm', result.texts);
        }
    };
    reader.onerror = () => renderImportModal('error', ["Impossible de lire le fichier."]);
    reader.readAsText(file);
}

function renderImportModal(mode, payload) {
    const title  = document.getElementById('importModalTitle');
    const body   = document.getElementById('importModalBody');
    const footer = document.getElementById('importModalFooter');

    if (mode === 'error') {
        title.textContent = '✕ Invalid file';
        body.innerHTML =
            '<div class="alert alert-err" style="margin-top:0;">' +
            '<ul class="import-error-list">' +
            payload.map(e => `<li>${escHtml(e)}</li>`).join('') +
            '</ul></div>';
        footer.innerHTML = '<button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeImportModal()">Close</button>';
    } else {
        title.textContent = '⚠ Replace all 9 actions?';
        body.innerHTML =
            '<p style="font-size:12px;color:var(--text-muted);margin-bottom:10px;">' +
            'This will overwrite the current text for all 9 actions below (and in the diagram). ' +
            'This set of texts is shared across all datacenters.</p>' +
            '<table class="confirm-table"><thead><tr><th>Action</th><th>Text</th></tr></thead><tbody>' +
            Object.keys(LETTER_TO_RULE).map(letter => {
                const t = payload[LETTER_TO_RULE[letter]];
                return `<tr><td><strong>${letter}</strong></td><td>${escHtml(t)}</td></tr>`;
            }).join('') +
            '</tbody></table>';
        footer.innerHTML =
            '<button class="btn btn-ghost" onclick="closeImportModal()">Cancel</button>' +
            '<button class="btn btn-primary" onclick="confirmImport()">Replace all 9 actions</button>';
    }

    document.getElementById('importModalOverlay').classList.add('open');
}

function closeImportModal() {
    document.getElementById('importModalOverlay').classList.remove('open');
    pendingImportTexts = null;
}

function closeImportModalOverlay(event) {
    if (event.target === document.getElementById('importModalOverlay')) {
        closeImportModal();
    }
}

function confirmImport() {
    if (!pendingImportTexts) return;
    const texts = pendingImportTexts;

    ALL_RULES.forEach(key => {
        const text = texts[key];
        const inp  = document.getElementById(`inp_${key}`);
        if (text === undefined || !inp) return;
        inp.value = text;
        updateSvgCell(key, text);
    });
    refreshTextsError();

    fetch(URL_TEXTS, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': CSRF },
        body: JSON.stringify({ rule_texts: texts }),
    })
    .then(r => { if (!r.ok) throw new Error('save failed'); })
    .then(() => {
        document.getElementById('savedNotice').style.display = 'block';
    })
    .catch(() => {
        alert('Texts were updated on screen but saving to the server failed. Please retry.');
    })
    .finally(() => {
        closeImportModal();
    });
}

// ── Step 3: preview ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function loadPreview() {
    goStep(3);
    document.getElementById('previewLoading').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('previewContent').style.display = 'none';
    if (IS_EDITOR) document.getElementById('btn3Apply').disabled = true;

    fetch(URL_PREVIEW, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': CSRF },
        body: JSON.stringify({ query: SCOPE_QUERY, sibling: getSibling(), rule_texts: getRuleTexts() }),
    })
    .then(r => r.json())
    .then(data => {
        document.getElementById('previewLoading').style.display = 'none';
        if (data.error) {
            document.getElementById('previewLoading').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('previewLoading').textContent = '❌ ' + data.error;
            return;
        }
        buildConfirmTable(data);
        document.getElementById('previewContent').style.display = 'block';

        const hasWork = data.changed > 0 || !!(data.sibling && data.sibling.to_reset > 0);
        if (IS_EDITOR) {
            const btn = document.getElementById('btn3Apply');
            btn.disabled = !hasWork;
            btn.style.opacity = hasWork ? '1' : '.45';
            btn.style.cursor  = hasWork ? 'pointer' : 'not-allowed';
        }

        if (data.total === 0) {
            document.getElementById('previewLoading').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('previewLoading').textContent =
                'ℹ No servers meet the criteria — all servers may have undetermined cluster or in_live_play values.';
        } else if (!hasWork) {
            document.getElementById('previewLoading').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('previewLoading').textContent =
                'ℹ Nothing to change: every server already has its action.';
        }
    })
    .catch(err => {
        document.getElementById('previewLoading').textContent = '❌ Network error: ' + err;
    });
}

// Store full server lists for modal access
let previewServersAll = {};
let srvModalFullCount = 0;  // real size of the list displayed in the modal

function serverList(servers, count, actionText, color) {
    return { servers, count, actionText, color, truncated: servers.length < count };
}

// Clickable count, plain text when there is nothing to list
function countLink(key, count, text) {
    return count > 0
        ? `<button class="confirm-count-link" data-idx="${key}">${text}</button>`
        : text;
}

function addConfirmRow(tbody, key, label, color, item) {
    const unchanged = item.count - item.changed;
    previewServersAll[key] = serverList(item.servers, item.count, item.action_text, color);
    previewServersAll[`${key}_changed`] =
        serverList(item.changed_servers, item.changed, `${item.action_text} — changed`, color);
    previewServersAll[`${key}_unchanged`] =
        serverList(item.unchanged_servers, unchanged, `${item.action_text} — unchanged`, color);

    // Main row — chevron toggles chips, count links open the modal
    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
    tr.innerHTML = `
        <td>
            <button class="chips-toggle" data-idx="${key}" title="Show/hide servers">
                <span class="chevron">▶</span>
            </button>
            <span class="color-dot" style="background:${color}"></span>
            <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:bold;color:var(--text-muted);margin-right:6px;">${escHtml(label)}</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;">${escHtml(item.action_text)}</span>
        </td>
        <td class="confirm-count">
            ${countLink(key, item.count, `${item.count} server${item.count !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`)}
        </td>
        <td class="confirm-changes">
            ${countLink(`${key}_changed`, item.changed, `${item.changed} changed`)},
            ${countLink(`${key}_unchanged`, unchanged, `${unchanged} unchanged`)}
        </td>
    `;
    tbody.appendChild(tr);

    // Preview chips row (max 50) — hidden by default
    const preview   = item.servers.slice(0, 50);
    const remaining = item.count - preview.length;
    const trC = document.createElement('tr');
    trC.style.display = 'none';  // shown by the chevron
    trC.innerHTML = `<td colspan="3" style="padding-top:0;">
        <div class="server-chips" id="chips_${key}">
            ${preview.map(s => `<span class="chip">${escHtml(s)}</span>`).join('')}
            ${remaining > 0
                ? `<button class="confirm-count-link" data-idx="${key}" style="font-size:10px;margin-left:4px;">
                       +${remaining} more…
                   </button>`
                : ''}
        </div>
    </td>`;
    tbody.appendChild(trC);
}

// Sibling reset: same format as the rules, in its own frame
function addSiblingRow(tbody, sibling) {
    if (sibling) {
        addConfirmRow(tbody, 'sibling', `X — Sibling reset · ${sibling.datacenter}`, SIBLING_COLOR, {
            action_text:       'Set to EMPTY, whatever the filters',
            count:             sibling.total,
            changed:           sibling.to_reset,
            servers:           sibling.servers,
            changed_servers:   sibling.changed_servers,
            unchanged_servers: sibling.unchanged_servers,
        });
        return;
    }
    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
    tr.innerHTML = `
        <td colspan="3">
            <span class="color-dot" style="background:${SIBLING_COLOR};margin-left:18px;"></span>
            <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:bold;color:var(--text-muted);margin-right:6px;">X — Sibling reset</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;">None: action_during_lp stays unchanged in every other datacenter.</span>
        </td>`;
    tbody.appendChild(tr);
}

// Servers in exception, as a row of the frame they belong to: no chevron, no rule letter
function addExceptionRow(tbody, key, label, text, info, changesText) {
    if (!info || !info.count) return;
    previewServersAll[key] = serverList(info.servers, info.count, text, EXCEPTION_COLOR);
    const plural = info.count !== 1 ? 's' : '';
    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
    tr.className = 'exception-row';
    tr.innerHTML = `
        <td>
            <span class="color-dot" style="background:${EXCEPTION_COLOR};margin-left:18px;"></span>
            <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:bold;color:${EXCEPTION_COLOR};margin-right:6px;">${escHtml(label)}</span>
            <span style="font-size:12px;">${escHtml(text)}</span>
        </td>
        <td class="confirm-count">${countLink(key, info.count, `${info.count} server${plural}`)}</td>
        <td class="confirm-changes">${countLink(key, info.count, escHtml(changesText))}</td>
    `;
    tbody.appendChild(tr);
}

function buildConfirmTable(data) {
    const tbody = document.getElementById('confirmTbody');
    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    previewServersAll = {};

    data.summary.forEach((item, idx) => {
        addConfirmRow(tbody, idx, RULE_LABELS[item.rule_key] || item.rule_key,
                      RULE_COLORS[item.rule_key] || '#aaa', item);
    });
    const exceptions = (data.exceptions && data.exceptions.count) || 0;
    addExceptionRow(tbody, 'exceptions', 'Exceptions', 'BC Exception = YES: will be ignored',
                    data.exceptions, `${exceptions} ignored`);
    const siblingTbody = document.getElementById('confirmSiblingTbody');
    siblingTbody.innerHTML = '';
    addSiblingRow(siblingTbody, data.sibling);
    if (data.sibling && data.sibling.exceptions_reset) {
        const reset = data.sibling.exceptions_reset;
        addExceptionRow(siblingTbody, 'exceptions_reset', `Exceptions · ${data.sibling.datacenter}`,
                        'BC Exception = YES: reset anyway, the sibling reset ignores exceptions',
                        reset, `${reset.count} reset`);
    }

    const skippedNote = data.skipped > 0
        ? ` — ${data.skipped} skipped (undetermined values)`
        : '';
    const exceptionNote = exceptions ? `, ${exceptions} in exception ignored` : '';
    document.getElementById('confirmTotal').textContent =
        `${data.total + exceptions} servers: ${data.changed} changed, ${data.total - data.changed} unchanged${exceptionNote}${skippedNote}`;

    renderShared('confirmShared', data.sibling);
    renderSkipped(document.getElementById('confirmSkipped'), data.skipped_with_action);

    // Chevron toggle: show/hide chips
    const content = document.getElementById('previewContent');
    content.querySelectorAll('.chips-toggle').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            const idx    = btn.dataset.idx;
            const chips  = document.getElementById(`chips_${idx}`);
            const chev   = btn.querySelector('.chevron');
            const isOpen = chips.classList.toggle('open');
            chips.closest('tr').style.display = isOpen ? '' : 'none';
            chev.textContent  = isOpen ? '▼' : '▶';
            chev.style.color  = isOpen ? 'var(--btn-primary)' : '';
        });
    });

    // Count links and "+N more" — open the detail modal
    content.querySelectorAll('.confirm-count-link').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => openSrvModal(btn.dataset.idx));
    });
}

// ── Server list modal ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

function openSrvModal(idx) {
    const data  = previewServersAll[idx];
    if (!data) return;

    // Set title + dot color
    document.getElementById('srvModalDot').style.background   = data.color;
    document.getElementById('srvModalTitle').textContent      = data.actionText;
    document.getElementById('srvModalSearch').value           = '';
    document.getElementById('srvModalEmpty').style.display    = 'none';

    // Build list — server items use data-hostname for reliable filtering
    // Truncation note uses a separate class so it is never filtered
    const list  = document.getElementById('srvModalList');
    const total = data.servers.length;

    list.innerHTML = data.servers
        .map(s => `<div class="srv-modal-item" data-hostname="${escHtml(s)}">${escHtml(s)}</div>`)
        .join('');

    if (data.truncated) {
        list.innerHTML +=
            `<div class="srv-modal-trunc">… list capped at ${total} — full count: ${data.count} servers.</div>`;
    }

    srvModalFullCount = data.count;
    updateSrvModalCount(total, total);
    document.getElementById('srvModalOverlay').classList.add('open');
    // Reset search input and empty notice
    document.getElementById('srvModalSearch').value        = '';
    document.getElementById('srvModalEmpty').style.display = 'none';
    setTimeout(() => document.getElementById('srvModalSearch').focus(), 50);
}

function filterSrvModal(query) {
    const q     = query.toLowerCase().trim();
    const items = document.querySelectorAll('#srvModalList .srv-modal-item');
    let visible = 0;

    items.forEach(item => {
        const hostname = item.dataset.hostname.toLowerCase();
        const match    = !q || hostname.includes(q);
        item.classList.toggle('hidden', !match);
        if (match) visible++;
    });

    updateSrvModalCount(visible, items.length);
    document.getElementById('srvModalEmpty').style.display =
        (q && visible === 0) ? 'block' : 'none';
}

function updateSrvModalCount(visible, total) {
    const el = document.getElementById('srvModalCount');
    if (visible !== total) {
        el.textContent = `${visible} / ${total} servers`;
    } else if (srvModalFullCount > total) {
        el.textContent = `${total} of ${srvModalFullCount} servers`;  // list capped by the server
    } else {
        el.textContent = `${total} server${total !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
    }
}

function closeSrvModal(event) {
    // Close only if clicking the overlay itself, not the modal
    if (event.target === document.getElementById('srvModalOverlay')) {
        document.getElementById('srvModalOverlay').classList.remove('open');
    }
}

// Close modal on Escape key
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        document.getElementById('srvModalOverlay').classList.remove('open');
    }
});

// ── Step 4: apply + polling ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
const STALL_MS = 120000;  // no visible progress for this long: warn the user

function resetStep4() {
    document.getElementById('step4Title').textContent = '4. Applying rules';
    const bar = document.getElementById('progressBar');
    bar.className   = 'progress-bar-inner';
    bar.style.width = '0%';
    document.getElementById('progressStatus').textContent = 'Sending request...';
    document.getElementById('progressLog').innerHTML      = '';
    document.getElementById('stallWarning').style.display  = 'none';
    document.getElementById('resultWrap').style.display    = 'none';
    document.getElementById('resultActions').style.display = 'none';
}

function startJob() {
    goStep(4);
    resetStep4();

    fetch(URL_START, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'X-CSRFToken': CSRF },
        body: JSON.stringify({ query: SCOPE_QUERY, sibling: getSibling(), rule_texts: getRuleTexts() }),
    })
    .then(r => r.json())
    .then(data => {
        if (data.error) {
            showResult(`The job did not start: ${data.error} Nothing was changed.`, null, true);
            return;
        }
        startPolling(data.job_id);
    })
    .catch(err => showResult(`Network error: ${err}. Check the server history before applying again.`, null, true));
}

function startPolling(jobId) {
    const url = URL_STATUS.replace('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', jobId);
    let lastSeen   = null;
    let lastChange = Date.now();

    pollInterval = setInterval(() => {
        document.getElementById('stallWarning').style.display =
            (Date.now() - lastChange > STALL_MS) ? 'block' : 'none';

        fetch(url)
            .then(r => r.json())
            .then(status => {
                if (status.error && !status.done) {
                    clearInterval(pollInterval);
                    showResult(`${status.error}. Check the server history before applying again.`, null, true);
                    return;
                }
                const seen = `${status.progress}|${status.message}|${(status.log || []).length}`;
                if (seen !== lastSeen) {
                    lastSeen   = seen;
                    lastChange = Date.now();
                }
                updateProgress(status);
                if (status.done) {
                    clearInterval(pollInterval);
                    showResult(status.error, status.stats, !!status.error);
                }
            })
            .catch(() => {}); // transient network error — retry next tick
    }, 800);
}

function updateProgress(status) {
    document.getElementById('progressBar').style.width    = (status.progress || 0) + '%';
    document.getElementById('progressStatus').textContent = status.message || '';
    const logEl   = document.getElementById('progressLog');
    const entries = status.log || [];
    // The log only grows: render the entries not displayed yet
    for (let i = logEl.children.length; i < entries.length; i++) {
        const entry = entries[i];
        const div   = document.createElement('div');
        const cls   = entry.msg.startsWith('✓') ? 'log-ok'
                    : entry.msg.startsWith('✕') ? 'log-err' : 'log-inf';
        div.innerHTML = `<span class="log-ts">${entry.ts}</span>`
                      + `<span class="${cls}">${escHtml(entry.msg)}</span>`;
        logEl.appendChild(div);
        logEl.scrollTop = logEl.scrollHeight;
    }
}

// Final state, displayed under the progress that stays on screen
function showResult(error, stats, failed) {
    const bar = document.getElementById('progressBar');
    bar.classList.add(failed ? 'failed' : 'ok');
    if (!failed) bar.style.width = '100%';
    document.getElementById('stallWarning').style.display = 'none';

    document.getElementById('step4Title').textContent = failed ? '4. Not completed' : '4. Done';
    document.getElementById('sc4').className   = `step-circle ${failed ? 'failed' : 'done'}`;
    document.getElementById('sc4').textContent = failed ? '✕' : '✓';
    document.getElementById('sl4').className   = `step-label ${failed ? 'failed' : 'done'}`;

    document.getElementById('resultBanner').className = `result-banner ${failed ? 'failed' : 'ok'}`;
    document.getElementById('resultIcon').textContent  = failed ? '✕' : '✓';
    document.getElementById('resultTitle').textContent = failed ? 'Not completed' : 'Done';
    document.getElementById('resultMsg').textContent   = failed
        ? error.replace(/\.?$/, '.') + (stats ? ' The changes counted below are saved, the others were not applied.' : '')
        : resultSummary(stats);
    renderRecap(stats, failed);

    document.getElementById('resultWrap').style.display    = 'block';
    document.getElementById('resultActions').style.display = 'flex';
    document.getElementById('btnBackConfirm').style.display = failed ? '' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('resultBanner').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
}

function resultSummary(s) {
    if (!s.updated && !s.reset) return 'Nothing needed to change.';
    return `${s.updated} servers updated in ${s.datacenter}.` +
        (s.sibling ? ` ${s.reset} servers reset in ${s.sibling}.` : '');
}

function renderRecap(s, failed) {
    const recap = document.getElementById('recap');
    if (!s) {
        recap.innerHTML = '';
        return;
    }
    const facts = [
        ['Affected datacenter', escHtml(s.datacenter)],
        ['Filters', escHtml(scopeFilters.map(f => `${f.label} = ${f.value}`).join(' · '))],
        ['Servers evaluated', `${s.total}`],
        ['Updated', failed ? `${s.updated} of ${s.changed}` : `${s.updated}`],
        ['Already up to date', `${s.unchanged}`],
        ['Skipped', `${s.skipped}`],
        ['Exceptions ignored', `${(s.exceptions && s.exceptions.count) || 0}`],
    ];
    if (s.sibling) {
        facts.push(['Sibling reset', failed
            ? `${s.reset} of ${s.reset_planned} in ${escHtml(s.sibling)}`
            : `${s.reset} in ${escHtml(s.sibling)}`]);
        if (s.shared) facts.push(['On both sites', `${s.shared} kept, not reset`]);
        if (s.exceptions_reset) facts.push(['Exceptions reset', `${s.exceptions_reset} in ${escHtml(s.sibling)}`]);
    } else {
        facts.push(['Sibling reset', 'None']);
    }
    facts.push(['Finished', `${escHtml(s.finished)} (${s.duration}s)`]);

    const rows = s.rules.map(r => `<tr>
        <td>
            <span class="color-dot" style="background:${RULE_COLORS[r.rule_key] || '#aaa'}"></span>
            <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:bold;color:var(--text-muted);margin-right:6px;">${escHtml(RULE_LABELS[r.rule_key] || r.rule_key)}</span>
            ${escHtml(r.action_text)}
        </td>
        <td class="confirm-count">${r.written}</td>
        <td class="confirm-count">${r.unchanged}</td>
    </tr>`).join('');

    recap.innerHTML =
        '<dl class="scope-summary">' +
        facts.map(([k, v]) => `<dt>${k}</dt><dd>${v}</dd>`).join('') +
        '</dl>' +
        (rows
            ? '<table class="confirm-table"><thead><tr><th>Action</th>' +
              '<th style="text-align:center;">Updated</th><th style="text-align:center;">Unchanged</th>' +
              `</tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table>`
            : '') +
        '<div class="alert alert-warn" id="recapSkipped" style="display:none;"></div>';
    renderSkipped(document.getElementById('recapSkipped'), s.skipped_with_action);
}

// ── Reset ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ── Util ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function escHtml(str) {
    return String(str)
        .replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
}

// ── Init ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
document.getElementById('backLink').href += LIST_QUERY;
loadScope();
initTexts();

window.goStep         = goStep;
window.loadPreview    = loadPreview;
window.startJob       = startJob;
window.restoreDefault = restoreDefault;
window.closeSrvModal  = closeSrvModal;
window.filterSrvModal = filterSrvModal;
window.handleImportFile        = handleImportFile;
window.closeImportModal        = closeImportModal;
window.closeImportModalOverlay = closeImportModalOverlay;
window.confirmImport           = confirmImport;

// ── Live SVG sync ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// Maps each rule input to its corresponding SVG text cell (data-cell-id)
const SVG_CELL_MAP = {
    cluster_yes_lp_yes: 'rB_text',
    cluster_yes_lp_no:  'rC_text',
    no_yes_phys:        'rD_text',
    no_yes_virt_fixed:  'rE_text',
    no_yes_virt_str:    'rF_text',
    no_no_phys:         'rG_text',
    no_no_virt_fixed:   'rH_text',
    no_no_virt_str:     'rI_text',
    hypervisor:         'rJ_text',
};

function updateSvgCell(ruleKey, text) {
    const cellId = SVG_CELL_MAP[ruleKey];
    if (!cellId) {
        return;
    }

    const cellGroup = document.querySelector(`[data-cell-id="${cellId}"]`);
    if (!cellGroup) {
        return;
    }

    const displayText = (text || '').trim() || ' ';

    // Try foreignObject first (present only if cell had text at export time)
    const fo = cellGroup.querySelector('foreignObject');
    if (fo) {
        const selectors = [
            'foreignObject div div div',
            'foreignObject div div',
            'foreignObject div',
        ];
        let updated = false;
        for (const sel of selectors) {
            const el = cellGroup.querySelector(sel);
            if (el) {
                el.textContent = displayText;
                updated = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!updated) {
        }
    } else {
        // No foreignObject — cell was empty at export time.
        // Fallback: use the SVG <text> element, or create a foreignObject.
    }

    // Fallback: SVG <text> element
    const textEl = cellGroup.querySelector('text');
    if (textEl) {
        textEl.textContent = displayText.length > 35
            ? displayText.slice(0, 33) + '…'
            : displayText;
    }

    // Last resort: cell has no foreignObject and no <text>.
    // This happens when the draw.io cell was exported empty.
    // We create a foreignObject dynamically so the text is visible.
    if (!fo && !textEl) {
        const svgNS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
        const htmlNS = 'http://www.w3.org/1999/xhtml';

        // Read the cell group's bounding rect to size the foreignObject
        const rect = cellGroup.querySelector('rect');
        const x  = rect ? parseFloat(rect.getAttribute('x')  || 0) : 0;
        const y  = rect ? parseFloat(rect.getAttribute('y')  || 0) : 0;
        const w  = rect ? parseFloat(rect.getAttribute('width')  || 160) : 160;
        const h  = rect ? parseFloat(rect.getAttribute('height') || 54)  : 54;

        const newFo = document.createElementNS(svgNS, 'foreignObject');
        newFo.setAttribute('x', x);
        newFo.setAttribute('y', y);
        newFo.setAttribute('width',  w);
        newFo.setAttribute('height', h);
        newFo.setAttribute('requiredFeatures',
            'http://www.w3.org/TR/SVG11/feature#Extensibility');

        const outerDiv = document.createElementNS(htmlNS, 'div');
        outerDiv.setAttribute('xmlns', htmlNS);
        outerDiv.style.cssText =
            'display:flex;align-items:center;' +
            `width:${w}px;height:${h}px;overflow:hidden;`;

        const innerDiv = document.createElementNS(htmlNS, 'div');
        innerDiv.style.cssText =
            'font-size:10px;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;' +
            'text-align:left;word-break:break-word;padding:2px 6px;';
        innerDiv.textContent = displayText;

        outerDiv.appendChild(innerDiv);
        newFo.appendChild(outerDiv);
        cellGroup.appendChild(newFo);
    }
}

function initSvgSync() {

    let bound = 0;
    ALL_RULES.forEach(key => {
        const inp = document.getElementById(`inp_${key}`);
        if (!inp) {
            return;
        }

        // Live update on every keystroke
        inp.addEventListener('input', () => updateSvgCell(key, inp.value));
        bound++;

        // Initial sync with current textarea value
        updateSvgCell(key, inp.value);
    });
}

// Load SVG from static file, inject inline, then sync texts
function loadDiagramSvg() {
    const wrap = document.getElementById('bcDiagramSvg');

    // Avoid re-fetching if SVG is already loaded
    if (wrap.querySelector('svg')) {
        initSvgSync();
        return;
    }

    const url = "{% static 'businesscontinuity/img/bc_diagram.svg' %}";
    fetch(url)
        .then(r => {
            if (!r.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + r.status);
            return r.text();
        })
        .then(svgText => {
            wrap.innerHTML = svgText;
            // The SVG's own light-dark() colors (edges, text) follow the OS/browser
            // color-scheme preference, not this app's independent dark-mode toggle.
            // Force it explicitly so it always matches what the user actually sees.
            const svgEl = wrap.querySelector('svg');
            if (svgEl) {
                const appIsDark = document.documentElement.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark-mode';
                svgEl.style.colorScheme = appIsDark ? 'dark' : 'light';
            }
            // Wait two animation frames so foreignObject divs are in the DOM
            requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                requestAnimationFrame(() => {
                    initSvgSync();
                });
            });
        })
        .catch(() => {
            wrap.innerHTML =
                '<p style="color:var(--text-muted);padding:20px 0;">Diagram unavailable.</p>';
        });
}

// SVG is loaded via loadDiagramSvg() called from goStep(2)

})();
</script>

</body>
</html>

    <div class="container">
        <h1>Bulk CSV Import</h1>

        <ul id="messages" class="messages"></ul>

        <div id="detailed-errors"></div>

        <div id="stepFile">
        <p>Upload a CSV file to perform a bulk update. The format is auto-detected from the header row.</p>

        <details class="format-docs">
            <summary>Supported formats: Server Import / Server Export / Application Import</summary>

            <div class="format-section">
                <h3>Server Import</h3>
                <p>Updates server fields. <code>hostname_unique</code> is mandatory. Other fields are optional (empty = unchanged). Priority Asset, Live Play and Cluster only accept YES, NO or EMPTY: a line with another value is ignored and listed before the import, like a server listed several times with different values.</p>
                <ul>
                    <li><code>hostname_unique</code> (mandatory): Server hostname</li>
                    <li><code>priority_asset</code>: Priority Asset (YES/NO/EMPTY)</li>
                    <li><code>in_live_play</code>: Live Play Status (YES/NO/EMPTY)</li>
                    <li><code>action_during_lp</code>: Action during the Live Play</li>
                    <li><code>annotation</code>: Annotation</li>
                    <li><code>cluster</code>: Cluster (YES/NO/EMPTY)</li>
                    <li><code>cluster_type</code>: Cluster Type</li>
                    <li><code>bc_exception</code>: BC Exception (YES/NO/EMPTY): BC rules leave the server alone on the affected site</li>
                </ul>
                <pre><code>hostname_unique;priority_asset;in_live_play;action_during_lp;annotation;cluster;cluster_type;bc_exception
server1;Yes;Yes;In ME until 24th;;No;;No
server2;No;Yes;In ME until 25th;;Yes;OPENSVC Cluster;
server3;;No;;;;;Yes</code></pre>
            </div>

            <div class="format-section">
                <h3>Server Export</h3>
                <p>The scheduled full export (<code>;</code> separated) can be loaded as is, to restore the values it holds. It is reduced to one line per server, then handled as a Server Import: <code>SERVER_ID</code> is the hostname, other columns are not imported. Values changed since the export will be overwritten and fields empty in the export will be cleared; a server whose lines disagree is refused.</p>
            </div>

            <div class="format-section">
                <h3>Application Import</h3>
                <p>Updates application Live Play status. Both fields are mandatory. The file is the source: an application listed at least once with YES sets its servers to YES, and a server goes to YES as soon as one of its listed applications is YES. Applications absent from the file have no influence.</p>
                <ul>
                    <li><code>application_id</code> (mandatory): Application identifier</li>
                    <li><code>in_live_play</code> (mandatory): Live Play Status (YES/NO)</li>
                </ul>
                <pre><code>application_id;in_live_play
APP001;Yes
APP002;No
APP003;Yes</code></pre>
            </div>
        </details>

    <script>

        const isDarkMode = localStorage.getItem('darkMode') === 'true';
        if (isDarkMode) {
            document.documentElement.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark-mode');
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            const form = document.getElementById('csv-upload-form');
            const uploadButton = document.getElementById('submitButton');
            const messagesContainer = document.getElementById('messages');
            const detailedErrorsContainer = document.getElementById('detailed-errors');
            const fileInput = form.querySelector('input[type="file"]');
            const progressContainer = document.getElementById('progressContainer');
            const progressBar = document.getElementById('progressBar');
            const progressMessage = document.getElementById('progressMessage');
            const formatBadge = document.getElementById('formatBadge');
            const importTypeInput = document.getElementById('importType');

            const FORMATS = {
                server: {
                    key: 'server',
                    label: 'Server Import',
                    requiredHeaders: ['hostname_unique'],
                    badgeClass: 'server',
                },
                export: {
                    key: 'export',
                    label: 'Server Export',
                    requiredHeaders: ['SERVER_ID', 'priority_asset', 'in_live_play', 'action_during_lp', 'annotation', 'cluster', 'cluster_type', 'bc_exception'],
                    badgeClass: 'server',
                },
                application: {
                    key: 'application',
                    label: 'Application Import',
                    requiredHeaders: ['application_id', 'in_live_play'],
                    badgeClass: 'application',
                },
            };

            let detectedFormat = null;

            function detectFormat(headers) {
                if (headers.includes('hostname_unique')) return FORMATS.server;
                if (headers.includes('application_id')) return FORMATS.application;
                if (FORMATS.export.requiredHeaders.every(h => headers.includes(h))) return FORMATS.export;
                return null;
            }

            function updateButtonState(isDisabled) {
                uploadButton.disabled = isDisabled;
                if (isDisabled) {
                    uploadButton.classList.add('disabled');
                } else {
                    uploadButton.classList.remove('disabled');
                }
            }

            updateButtonState(true);

            fileInput.addEventListener('change', function () {
                formatBadge.style.display = 'none';
                formatBadge.className = '';
                detectedFormat = null;
                importTypeInput.value = '';

                if (fileInput.files.length === 0) {
                    updateButtonState(true);
                    return;
                }

                const file = fileInput.files[0];
                const previewReader = new FileReader();
                previewReader.onload = function (event) {
                    const firstLine = event.target.result.split('\n')[0] || '';
                    const headers = firstLine.trim().split(';').map(h => h.trim());
                    detectedFormat = detectFormat(headers);

                    formatBadge.style.display = 'inline-block';
                    if (detectedFormat) {
                        formatBadge.textContent = 'Detected format: ' + detectedFormat.label;
                        formatBadge.className = detectedFormat.badgeClass;
                        importTypeInput.value = detectedFormat.key;
                        updateButtonState(false);
                    } else {
                        formatBadge.textContent = 'Unknown format — check the header row';
                        formatBadge.className = 'unknown';
                        updateButtonState(true);
                    }
                };
                previewReader.readAsText(file);
            });

            form.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
                e.preventDefault();

                updateButtonState(true);
                messagesContainer.innerHTML = '';
                detailedErrorsContainer.style.display = 'none';
                detailedErrorsContainer.innerHTML = '';
                progressContainer.style.display = 'block';
                progressBar.style.width = '0%';
                progressMessage.textContent = 'Starting import...';

                const file = fileInput.files[0];
                if (!file) {
                    alert("Please select a CSV file.");
                    progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    updateButtonState(false);
                    return;
                }

                const reader = new FileReader();
                reader.onload = function (event) {
                    const contents = event.target.result;
                    const lines = contents.split('\n').filter(line => line.trim() !== '');

                    if (lines.length < 2) {
                        alert("This CSV file has no data!");
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        updateButtonState(false);
                        return;
                    }

                    const headers = lines[0].trim().split(';').map(h => h.trim());
                    const format = detectFormat(headers);

                    if (!format) {
                        alert("Unknown CSV format. Please check the header row against the supported formats.");
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        updateButtonState(false);
                        return;
                    }

                    const missingHeaders = format.requiredHeaders.filter(h => !headers.includes(h));
                    if (missingHeaders.length > 0) {
                        alert("Missing required columns: " + missingHeaders.join(', '));
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        updateButtonState(false);
                        return;
                    }

                    const showFailure = (error) => {
                        showStep('file');
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        const li = document.createElement('li');
                        li.className = 'error';
                        li.textContent = `An error occurred: ${error}`;
                        messagesContainer.appendChild(li);
                        updateButtonState(false);
                    };

                    const cancelImport = () => {
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                        updateButtonState(false);
                    };

                    const startImport = () => {
                        showStep('progress');
                        progressBar.classList.remove('failed');
                        progressBar.style.width = '0%';
                        progressMessage.textContent = 'Starting import...';
                        const formData    = new FormData(form);
                        let   pollInterval = null;

                        // Step 1 — POST to start the background import job
                        fetch(form.action, {
                            method: 'POST',
                            headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' },
                            body: formData,
                        })
                        .then(r => r.json())
                        .then(data => {
                            // Handle synchronous validation errors returned before the job starts
                            if (data.error) {
                                showStep('file');
                                progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                                handleSSEData(data);
                                updateButtonState(false);
                                return;
                            }
                            if (!data.job_id) {
                                showStep('file');
                                progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                                const li = document.createElement('li');
                                li.className = 'error';
                                li.textContent = 'Could not start import job.';
                                messagesContainer.appendChild(li);
                                updateButtonState(false);
                                return;
                            }

                            // Step 2 — poll for progress every 800ms
                            const statusUrl = `{% url appname|add:':import_csv_status' job_id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' %}`.replace('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', data.job_id);

                            pollInterval = setInterval(() => {
                                fetch(statusUrl)
                                    .then(r => r.json())
                                    .then(status => {
                                        progressBar.style.width     = `${status.progress || 0}%`;
                                        progressMessage.textContent = status.message || '';

                                        if (status.done) {
                                            clearInterval(pollInterval);
                                            if (status.stats && status.stats.recap) {
                                                renderRecap(status.stats);
                                                return;
                                            }
                                            showStep('file');
                                            progressContainer.style.display = 'none';

                                            if (status.error) {
                                                handleSSEData({ error: status.error });
                                            }

                                            updateButtonState(true);
                                            fileInput.value           = '';
                                            formatBadge.style.display = 'none';
                                            formatBadge.className     = '';
                                            importTypeInput.value     = '';
                                            detectedFormat            = null;
                                        }
                                    })
                                    .catch(() => {}); // transient network error — retry next tick
                            }, 800);
                        })
                        .catch(showFailure);
                    };

                    // Show what would really change before starting
                    const runPreview = () => {
                        showStep('file');
                        messagesContainer.innerHTML = '';
                        updateButtonState(true);
                        progressContainer.style.display = 'block';
                        progressBar.classList.remove('failed');
                        progressBar.style.width = '0%';
                        progressMessage.textContent = 'Comparing the file with the database...';
                        const previewData = new FormData(form);
                        previewData.append('dry_run', '1');
                        fetch(form.action, {
                            method: 'POST',
                            headers: { 'X-Requested-With': 'XMLHttpRequest' },
                            body: previewData,
                        })
                        .then(r => r.json())
                        .then(preview => {
                            if (preview.error) {
                                cancelImport();
                                handleSSEData(preview);
                                return;
                            }
                            renderReview(format, preview, startImport);
                        })
                        .catch(showFailure);
                    };
                    document.getElementById('btnRecapBack').onclick = runPreview;
                    runPreview();
                };
                reader.readAsText(file);
            });

            /* -- Review and recap -- */

            const COLORS = { changed: '#e8910c', unchanged: '#4caf50', notFound: '#d9534f', noHostname: '#888888', notSaved: '#d9534f', exception: '#b5179e' };
            const stepFile  = document.getElementById('stepFile');
            const stepReview = document.getElementById('stepReview');
            const recapBox  = document.getElementById('recapBox');

            function showStep(step) {
                stepFile.style.display   = step === 'file' ? '' : 'none';
                stepReview.style.display = step === 'review' ? 'block' : 'none';
                recapBox.style.display   = step === 'recap' ? 'block' : 'none';
                progressContainer.style.display = (step === 'progress' || step === 'recap') ? 'block' : 'none';
                if (step === 'file' || step === 'review') {
                    detailedErrorsContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    detailedErrorsContainer.innerHTML = '';
                }
            }

            // One line of a frame: colored dot, count (a link when there is a list to show), label
            function addCountRow(parent, count, label, color, list, noun = 'server') {
                const row = document.createElement('div');
                row.className = 'import-row';
                const dot = document.createElement('span');
                dot.className = 'import-dot';
                dot.style.background = color;
                const number = document.createElement(count > 0 && list ? 'a' : 'span');
                number.className = 'import-count';
                number.textContent = count;
                if (count > 0 && list) {
                    number.dataset.list = label;
                    number.addEventListener('click', () => openSrvModal(`${count} ${label}`, color, list, count, noun));
                }
                const text = document.createElement('span');
                text.textContent = label;
                row.append(dot, number, text);
                parent.appendChild(row);
            }

            // Lines shared by the review and the recap, according to the kind of import
            function addIgnoredRows(parent, data) {
                const app = data.kind === 'application';
                const many = n => n === 1 ? '' : 's';
                if (data.not_found) addCountRow(parent, data.not_found, app ? `application${many(data.not_found)} without a server` : 'not found in the database',
                    COLORS.notFound, data.lists.not_found, app ? 'application' : 'server');
                if (data.invalid) addCountRow(parent, data.invalid, `line${many(data.invalid)} with an invalid value`, COLORS.notFound, data.lists.invalid, 'line');
                if (data.no_hostname) addCountRow(parent, data.no_hostname, app ? `line${many(data.no_hostname)} without an application id` : 'without a hostname', COLORS.noHostname);
            }

            // The file is the source: servers in exception are modified too, the user is told how many
            function addExceptionRow(parent, data, verb) {
                if (!data.exceptions) return;
                addCountRow(parent, data.exceptions, `server${data.exceptions !== 1 ? 's' : ''} in exception ${verb}`,
                    COLORS.exception, data.lists.exceptions);
            }

            function renderFieldCounts(frame, counts) {
                frame.innerHTML = '';
                const fields = Object.keys(counts || {});
                frame.style.display = fields.length ? '' : 'none';
                if (!fields.length) return;
                const title = document.createElement('h3');
                title.textContent = 'Changes by field';
                frame.appendChild(title);
                fields.forEach(field => {
                    const row = document.createElement('div');
                    row.className = 'import-row';
                    const number = document.createElement('span');
                    number.className = 'import-count';
                    number.textContent = counts[field];
                    const text = document.createElement('code');
                    text.textContent = field;
                    row.append(number, text);
                    frame.appendChild(row);
                });
            }

            function renderReview(format, preview, startImport) {
                const plural = (n, word) => `${n} ${word}${n !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
                let scope;
                if (preview.kind === 'application') {
                    scope = `${plural(preview.lines, 'line')}, ${plural(preview.apps, 'application')}`;
                } else {
                    scope = (preview.is_export ? `${preview.lines} lines, ` : '') + plural(preview.servers, 'server');
                }
                document.getElementById('reviewTitle').textContent = `${format.label}: ${scope}`;

                const counts = document.getElementById('reviewCounts');
                counts.innerHTML = '';
                addCountRow(counts, preview.changed, 'will be modified', COLORS.changed, preview.lists.changed);
                addExceptionRow(counts, preview, 'among them');
                addCountRow(counts, preview.unchanged, 'already up to date', COLORS.unchanged, preview.lists.unchanged);
                addIgnoredRows(counts, preview);
                renderFieldCounts(document.getElementById('reviewFields'), preview.field_counts);

                const note = document.getElementById('reviewNote');
                const start = document.getElementById('btnReviewStart');
                start.disabled = preview.changed === 0;
                start.style.display = preview.changed === 0 ? 'none' : '';
                start.textContent = `Start the import (${preview.changed} server${preview.changed !== 1 ? 's' : ''})`;
                start.onclick = () => { start.disabled = true; startImport(); };
                if (preview.changed === 0) {
                    note.textContent = 'Nothing to import: no server would change.';
                } else if (preview.is_export) {
                    note.textContent = 'Values changed since this export will be overwritten, and fields empty in the export will be cleared.';
                } else {
                    note.textContent = '';
                }
                note.style.display = note.textContent ? '' : 'none';
                showStep('review');
            }

            function renderRecap(stats) {
                showStep('recap');
                const status = document.getElementById('recapStatus');
                status.innerHTML = '';
                const li = document.createElement('li');
                if (stats.failed) {
                    li.className = 'error';
                    li.textContent = `Import failed: ${stats.changed} of ${stats.total} server${stats.total !== 1 ? 's' : ''} saved. ${stats.error.replace(/\.?$/, '.')} `
                        + 'Go back to the confirmation to start again: only the servers still different will be modified.';
                    progressBar.classList.add('failed');
                    progressBar.style.width = `${stats.total ? (stats.changed / stats.total) * 100 : 0}%`;
                    progressMessage.textContent = `Stopped after ${stats.changed} of ${stats.total} server${stats.total !== 1 ? 's' : ''}.`;
                } else {
                    li.className = 'success';
                    li.textContent = stats.message;
                    progressBar.style.width = '100%';
                    progressMessage.textContent = 'Done.';
                }
                status.appendChild(li);

                const counts = document.getElementById('recapCounts');
                counts.innerHTML = '';
                addCountRow(counts, stats.changed, 'updated', COLORS.changed, stats.lists.changed);
                addExceptionRow(counts, stats, 'among them');
                if (stats.failed) addCountRow(counts, stats.not_saved.count, 'not saved', COLORS.notSaved, stats.not_saved.list);
                addCountRow(counts, stats.unchanged, 'already up to date', COLORS.unchanged, stats.lists.unchanged);
                addIgnoredRows(counts, stats);
                renderFieldCounts(document.getElementById('recapFields'), stats.field_counts);
                document.getElementById('recapActions').style.display = stats.failed ? '' : 'none';
                const download = document.getElementById('recapDownload');
                download.style.display = stats.log_id ? 'block' : 'none';
                if (stats.log_id) {
                    download.href = `{% url appname|add:':import_csv_report' log_id=0 %}`.replace('/0/', `/${stats.log_id}/`);
                }
            }

            document.getElementById('btnReviewBack').addEventListener('click', () => {
                messagesContainer.innerHTML = '';
                showStep('file');
                updateButtonState(false);
            });

            /* -- Server list modal -- */

            const srvOverlay = document.getElementById('srvModalOverlay');
            const srvList    = document.getElementById('srvModalList');
            const srvSearch  = document.getElementById('srvModalSearch');
            const srvEmpty   = document.getElementById('srvModalEmpty');
            let srvFullCount = 0;
            let srvNoun = 'server';

            function srvItem(entry) {
                const item = document.createElement('div');
                item.className = 'srv-modal-item';
                const host = document.createElement('div');
                host.className = 'srv-modal-host';
                host.textContent = typeof entry === 'string' ? entry : entry.hostname;
                item.appendChild(host);
                (entry.changes || []).forEach(change => {
                    const line = document.createElement('div');
                    line.className = 'srv-modal-change';
                    const field = document.createElement('span');
                    field.className = 'srv-modal-field';
                    field.textContent = `${change.field}: `;
                    line.append(field, `${change.old || '(empty)'} \u2192 ${change.new || '(empty)'}` + (change.via ? ` (via ${change.via})` : ''));
                    item.appendChild(line);
                });
                item.dataset.search = item.textContent.toLowerCase();
                return item;
            }

            function updateSrvCount(visible, total) {
                const el = document.getElementById('srvModalCount');
                if (visible !== total) {
                    el.textContent = `${visible} / ${total} ${srvNoun}s`;
                } else if (srvFullCount > total) {
                    el.textContent = `${total} of ${srvFullCount} ${srvNoun}s`;  // list capped by the server
                } else {
                    el.textContent = `${total} ${srvNoun}${total !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
                }
            }

            function openSrvModal(title, color, list, count, noun) {
                srvNoun = noun;
                document.getElementById('srvModalDot').style.background = color;
                document.getElementById('srvModalTitle').textContent = title;
                srvSearch.value = '';
                srvEmpty.style.display = 'none';
                srvList.innerHTML = '';
                list.forEach(entry => srvList.appendChild(srvItem(entry)));
                srvFullCount = count;
                updateSrvCount(list.length, list.length);
                srvOverlay.classList.add('open');
                setTimeout(() => srvSearch.focus(), 50);
            }

            srvSearch.addEventListener('input', () => {
                const query = srvSearch.value.toLowerCase().trim();
                const items = srvList.querySelectorAll('.srv-modal-item');
                let visible = 0;
                items.forEach(item => {
                    const match = !query || item.dataset.search.includes(query);
                    item.classList.toggle('hidden', !match);
                    if (match) visible++;
                });
                updateSrvCount(visible, items.length);
                srvEmpty.style.display = query && visible === 0 ? 'block' : 'none';
            });

            document.getElementById('srvModalClose').addEventListener('click', () => srvOverlay.classList.remove('open'));
            srvOverlay.addEventListener('click', e => {
                if (e.target === srvOverlay) srvOverlay.classList.remove('open');
            });
            document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
                if (e.key === 'Escape') srvOverlay.classList.remove('open');
            });

            function handleSSEData(data) {
                if (data.progress !== undefined) {
                    progressBar.style.width = `${data.progress}%`;
                    progressMessage.textContent = `${data.message || 'Processing...'} ${data.progress.toFixed(0)}%`;
                }
                if (data.warning) {
                    const li = document.createElement('li');
                    li.className = 'warning';
                    li.textContent = data.warning;
                    messagesContainer.appendChild(li);
                }
                if (data.error) {
                    const li = document.createElement('li');
                    li.className = 'error';
                    li.textContent = data.error;
                    messagesContainer.appendChild(li);
                }
                if (data.success) {
                    const li = document.createElement('li');
                    li.className = 'success';
                    li.textContent = data.success;
                    messagesContainer.appendChild(li);
                }
                if (data.detailed_errors) {
                    detailedErrorsContainer.style.display = 'block';
                    let ul = document.createElement('ul');
                    data.detailed_errors.forEach(err => {
                        let li = document.createElement('li');
                        li.textContent = err;
                        ul.appendChild(li);
                    });
                    detailedErrorsContainer.appendChild(ul);
                }
            }
        });
    </script>
</body>
</html>

                    {% endif %} 

                </div>  <!-- log-date-container -->

            <div class="tabs">
            
                <div id="menu">
                
                    <button class="menu-button" onclick="validateAndShowSaveSearchModal()">Save Search</button>
                    {% if saved_searches %}
                        <div class="search-dropdown-container">
                        
                            <button class="search-btn" onclick="toggleDropdown()" id="selectedSearch">Saved Searches &nbsp;▼</button>
                            <div class="search-dropdown-content" id="dropdownContent">

                                {% endfor %}

                            <hr class="export-separator"> <!-- Separator -->
                            <div class="export-option" data-value="chart">Charts Generation</div>

                                    {{ filter }}
                                </div>

                        </div>

                    <div id="totalRecords">
                        Total records: <span class="totalRecords-info" data-info="Distinct servers: {{ unique_servers_count }}">{{ page_obj.paginator.count }}</span>
                    </div>        

                </div>  <!-- menu -->

                <div class="tab-content">

                    <div id="tab1" class="tab active">

                        <div class="main-content">

                                  <div id="filters-container" class="collapsed" data-clickable="true">
                                       <span id="chevron" class="chevron">... ▼</span>
                                    <div class="filter-tags" id="filterTags">
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="table-container">
                                
                                <table id="serversTable" data-user-canedit="{{ is_user_editor }}">
                                
                                    <thead>
                
                                        <tr>
                                            <th class="action resizable sticky-col-1">
                                                <button type="button" class="filter-help-button" id="filterHelpButton"
                                                        aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="filterHelp" title="Filter syntax">?</button>
                                            </th>
                                            
                                            {% for field in table_fields %}
                
                                                <th class="{{ field.name }} resizable {% if field.name == 'SERVER_ID'%}sticky-col-2{% endif %}">
                                                
                                                    <input type="text" class="search-input" name="{{ field.inputname }}" placeholder="Search {{ field.displayname }}">
                                                    {% if field.listbox != '' %}
                                                        <select id="{{ field.listid }}-listbox" class="{{ field.listid }}-listbox listbox-centered">
                                                            <option value="">{{ field.listboxmsg }}</option>

                                                            {% endfor %}
