                                                    {% endif %}

                                                </th>        

                                            {% endfor %}

                                        </tr>
                                        
                                    </thead>
            
                                    <tbody id="tableBody">
                                        
                                        {% for server_entry in servers %}  <!-- page_obj.object_list -->
                                            <tr
                                                data-hostname="{{ server_entry.hostname_unique }}"
                                                data-priority-asset="{{ server_entry.priority_asset|default_if_none:'N/A' }}"

                                            >
                                                {% if is_user_editor %}
                                                    <td class="sticky-col-1"><button class="edit-button" data-server-id="{{ server_entry.hostname_unique }}">EDIT</button></td>
                                                {% else %}
                                                    <td class="sticky-col-1"><button class="edit-button" data-server-id="{{ server_entry.hostname_unique }}">VIEW</button></td>
                                                {% endif %}
                                                                                                       
                                                {% for field in table_fields %}
                                                    <td class="{{ field.name }} {% if field.name|is_lowercase %}highlight-td{% endif %} {% if field.name == 'SERVER_ID'%}sticky-col-2{% endif %}">

    <div class="filter-help" id="filterHelp" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="filterHelpTitle" hidden>
        <div class="filter-help-head">
            <strong id="filterHelpTitle">Filter syntax</strong>
            <button type="button" class="close-button" id="filterHelpClose" aria-label="Close">&times;</button>
        </div>
        <table class="filter-help-table">
            <tbody>
                <tr><td><code>prod</code></td><td>Contains "prod"</td></tr>
                <tr><td><code>@APP10060P</code></td><td>Exact value</td></tr>
                <tr><td><code>!test</code></td><td>Excludes the values containing "test"</td></tr>
                <tr><td><code>!@DEV</code></td><td>Excludes this exact value</td></tr>
                <tr><td><code>#prod</code></td><td>Keeps every line of the servers having at least one line containing "prod"</td></tr>
                <tr><td><code>#@APP10060P</code></td><td>Same, with an exact value</td></tr>
                <tr><td><code>prod,dev</code></td><td>"prod" or "dev"</td></tr>
            </tbody>
        </table>
        <ul class="filter-help-notes">
            <li>Type in the box above a column and press Enter. Remove a filter with the &times; of its tag.</li>
            <li>Upper and lower case are not distinguished, whatever the syntax.</li>
            <li>Several values in the same column act as OR; exclusions (<code>!</code>, <code>!@</code>) then apply as AND.</li>
            <li>Filters on different columns act as AND.</li>
            <li>Choosing a value in a drop-down list is the same as <code>@value</code>.</li>
            <li>In Server Name, spaces and semicolons also separate values: you can paste a list.</li>
        </ul>
    </div>

    <script src="{% static appname|add:'/js/servers.js' %}"></script>
    <script src="{% static appname|add:'/js/filter.js' %}"></script>
    <script src="{% static 'common/js/chart-modal.js' %}"></script>
    <script src="{% static 'common/js/settings-modal.js' %}"></script>
    
    <script>

        const filterTagsDiv = document.getElementById('filterTags');
        const json_data = JSON.parse('{{ json_data|escapejs }}');
        const filtersContainer = document.getElementById('filters-container');
        const chevron = document.getElementById('chevron');
        const noFiltersMessage = document.createElement('div');
        const dropdownContent = document.getElementById('exportDropdownContent');
        const sidebarContainer = document.querySelector('.sidebar-container');
        const toggleButton = document.getElementById('toggleSidebar');
        const appname = '{{ appname }}';
        const searchForm = document.getElementById('saveSearchForm');
        const savedSearchNames = [
            {% for search in saved_searches %}
              "{{ search.name|escapejs }}"{% if not forloop.last %},{% endif %}
            {% endfor %}
        ];
        const getServerHistoryUrl = '{% url appname|add:":get_server_history" %}';
        const select = document.getElementById('app-select');
        noFiltersMessage.id = 'no-filters-message';
        noFiltersMessage.textContent = 'No filters';
        noFiltersMessage.style.textAlign = 'left';
        noFiltersMessage.style.color = '#777';
    
        let isExpandable = false;
        let previousValue = select.value;
     
        const jsonData = {{ json_data|safe }};
        initChartModal(jsonData, 'businesscontinuity');
        initSettingsModal('businesscontinuity');


        /* -- URL Update when changing the page -- */

        function updateURL(href) {
            const url = new URL(href, window.location.origin);
            const searchParams = new URLSearchParams(url.search);

            // Update the URL with the current state of visible columns
            const visibleColumns = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.column-checkbox'))
                .filter(checkbox => checkbox.checked)
                .map(checkbox => checkbox.getAttribute('data-column'))
                .join(',');

            searchParams.set('visible_columns', visibleColumns);

            // Include the cat_ parameters
            const currentParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            for (const [key, value] of currentParams.entries()) {
                if (key.startsWith('cat_')) {
                    searchParams.set(key, value);
                }
            }            

            window.location.href = `${url.pathname}?${searchParams.toString()}`;
        }


        window.addEventListener('pageshow', function(event) {
            if (event.persisted) {
                // Page was restored from BFCache
                document.getElementById('app-select').value = '{% url "businesscontinuity:server_view" %}';
            }
        });


        document.getElementById('app-select').addEventListener('change', function () {
            const url = this.value;               // the URL of the selected option

            if (this.options[this.selectedIndex].dataset.newTab) {
                select.value=previousValue;
                window.open(url, '_blank');
            } else {
                window.location.href = url;
            }
        });            

        /* -- Categories Sidebar -- */
        
        function updateSidebarState() {  // Function to update the sidebar state and save it to localStorage
            const isCollapsed = sidebarContainer.classList.contains('collapsed');
            toggleButton.querySelector('i').innerHTML = isCollapsed ? '&gt;' : '&lt;'; // Update chevron icon based on state
            localStorage.setItem(`${appname}sidebarCollapsed`, isCollapsed); // Save the state to localStorage
            loadExpandedState();
            checkIfMultipleLines();
        }


        toggleButton.addEventListener('click', () => {  // Function to toggle the sidebar state and update local storage
            sidebarContainer.classList.toggle('collapsed');
            updateSidebarState();
        });


        function loadSidebarState() {  // Function to load the sidebar state from localStorage on page load
            const isCollapsed = localStorage.getItem(`${appname}sidebarCollapsed`) === 'true';
            if (isCollapsed) {
                sidebarContainer.classList.add('collapsed');
            } else {
                sidebarContainer.classList.remove('collapsed');
            }
            updateSidebarState(); 
        }

        window.addEventListener('load', loadSidebarState);  // Load the sidebar state on page load


        /* -- Filter Bar -- */
        
        function loadExpandedState() {  // Function to load the state from local storage
            const isExpanded = localStorage.getItem(`${appname}filtersContainerExpanded`) === 'true';
            filtersContainer.classList.toggle('expanded', isExpanded);
            updateChevron();
            if (isExpanded) {
                filtersContainer.style.width = localStorage.getItem(`${appname}filtersContainerWidth`);
            }
        }

        
        function saveExpandedState(isExpanded) {  // Function to save the state to local storage
            localStorage.setItem(`${appname}filtersContainerExpanded`, isExpanded);
            if (isExpanded) {
                localStorage.setItem(`${appname}filtersContainerExpanded`, filtersContainer.offsetWidth + 'px');
            }
        }


        chevron.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
            const isExpanded = !filtersContainer.classList.contains('expanded');

            filtersContainer.classList.toggle('expanded', isExpanded);
            if (isExpanded) {
                   filtersContainer.style.width = (filtersContainer.offsetWidth -27) + 'px';
               } else {
                   filtersContainer.style.width = '';
               }
            saveExpandedState(isExpanded);
            updateChevron();
            event.stopPropagation();
            checkIfMultipleLines();
        });


        function updateChevron() {
            const isExpanded = filtersContainer.classList.contains('expanded');
            chevron.textContent = isExpanded ? '... ▲' : '... ▼';
        }


        function checkIfMultipleLines() {
        
            const filterTagsDiv = document.getElementById('filterTags');
            const clone = filterTagsDiv.cloneNode(true);
            clone.style.visibility = 'hidden';
            clone.style.position = 'absolute';
            clone.style.width = filterTagsDiv.offsetWidth + 'px';
            clone.style.flexWrap = 'nowrap';
            document.body.appendChild(clone);

            clone.style.flexWrap = 'wrap';
            const fullHeight = clone.offsetHeight;
            document.body.removeChild(clone);
            const isExpandable = fullHeight > 50; // Bigger than the default height
            const isExpanded = localStorage.getItem(`${appname}filtersContainerExpanded`) === 'true';

            if (isExpandable) {
                filtersContainer.setAttribute('data-clickable', 'true');
                chevron.style.display = 'block';
                if (!isExpanded) {
                    localStorage.removeItem(`${appname}filtersContainerExpanded`);
                    localStorage.removeItem(`${appname}filtersContainerWidth`);
                }
            } else {
                filtersContainer.removeAttribute('data-clickable');
                filtersContainer.classList.toggle('expanded', false);
                filtersContainer.classList.remove('expanded');
                chevron.style.display = 'none';
                filtersContainer.style.width = '';
            }

            updateChevron();
            updateFiltersDisplay();
        
        }

        
        function updateFiltersDisplay() {
            if (filterTagsDiv.children.length === 0) {
                if (!filtersContainer.contains(noFiltersMessage)) {
                    filtersContainer.insertBefore(noFiltersMessage, filterTagsDiv);
                }
            } else {
                sessionStorage.removeItem('selectedSearchName');
                if (filtersContainer.contains(noFiltersMessage)) {
                    filtersContainer.removeChild(noFiltersMessage);
                }
            }
        }


        /* -- Load, Resize and Scroll events -- */
        
        window.addEventListener('load', () => {
            loadSidebarState(); // Load the state when the page loads
            loadExpandedState(); // Load the state when the page loads
            checkIfMultipleLines();
        });


        window.addEventListener('resize', () => {
            const wasExpanded = filtersContainer.classList.contains('expanded');
            checkIfMultipleLines();
            if (isExpandable && wasExpanded) {
                filtersContainer.classList.add('expanded');
            } else {
                filtersContainer.classList.remove('expanded');
                updateChevron();
                updateFiltersDisplay();
            }
        });
        
        window.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
            checkIfMultipleLines();
        });


        /* -- Update on load -- */

        checkIfMultipleLines();
        updateChevron();

 
        function ActiveFiltersDetected() {
            const filterTags = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-tag');
            return filterTags.length > 0;
        }


        /* -- Searches Operations -- */
        
        function showSaveSearchModal() {
            document.getElementById('saveSearchModal').style.display = 'block';
        }

        
        function closeSaveSearchModal() {
            document.getElementById('saveSearchModal').style.display = 'none';
            applyFilters();
        }


        function loadSearch(searchId) {
            if (searchId) {
                window.location.href = `{% url appname|add:':load_search' search_id=0 %}`.replace('0', searchId);
            }
        }        


        function deleteSearch(searchId) {
            if (searchId && confirm('Are you sure you want to delete this search?')) {
                window.location.href = `{% url appname|add:':delete_search' search_id=0 %}`.replace('0', searchId);
            }
        }
        
        
        document.querySelectorAll('.search-item').forEach(item => {
            item.addEventListener('click', function(event) {
                const searchId = this.getAttribute('data-id');
                const searchName = this.getAttribute('data-name');
                if (event.target.classList.contains('delete-button')) {
                    deleteSearch(searchId);
                } else {
                    loadSearch(searchId);
                    updateSelectedSearch(searchName);
                }
                toggleDropdown(); // Hide the dropdown after selection
            });
        });


        function updateSelectedSearch(searchName) {
            document.getElementById('selectedSearch').textContent = searchName + ' ▼';
            sessionStorage.setItem('selectedSearchName', searchName); 
        }


        function toggleDropdown() {
            const dropdownContent = document.getElementById('dropdownContent');
            dropdownContent.style.display = dropdownContent.style.display === 'block' ? 'none' : 'block';
        }

        const selectedSearchName = sessionStorage.getItem('selectedSearchName'); // Retrieve the selected search name from sessionStorage
        if (selectedSearchName) {
            updateSelectedSearch(selectedSearchName);
        }        
        
        
        /* -- Export Operations -- */
        
        function toggleExportDropdown() {
            const dropdownContent = document.getElementById('exportDropdownContent');
            dropdownContent.style.display = dropdownContent.style.display === 'block' ? 'none' : 'block';
        }

        function toggleEditDropdown() {
            const editDropdown = document.getElementById('editDropdownContent');
            if (editDropdown) {
                editDropdown.style.display = editDropdown.style.display === 'block' ? 'none' : 'block';
            }
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function () {
            const editImportCSV = document.getElementById('editImportCSV');
            const editBulkEdit  = document.getElementById('editBulkEdit');
            const editBCRules   = document.getElementById('editBCRules');

            if (editImportCSV) {
                editImportCSV.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    toggleEditDropdown();
                    window.location.href = "{% url appname|add:':bulk_import_csv' %}";
                });
            }
            if (editBulkEdit) {
                editBulkEdit.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    toggleEditDropdown();
                    if (!bulkRunning) {
                        document.getElementById('bulkEditForm').reset();  // no value left over from another selection
                        updateBulkScope();
                    }
                    document.getElementById('bulkEditModal').style.display = 'flex';
                });
            }
            if (editBCRules) {
                editBCRules.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    toggleEditDropdown();
                    const wizardParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
                    wizardParams.set('expected_servers', '{{ unique_servers_count }}');  // checked by the wizard
                    window.location.href = "{% url appname|add:':apply_bc_rules' %}?" + wizardParams.toString();
                });
            }

            const editResetDR = document.getElementById('editResetDR');
            if (editResetDR) {
                editResetDR.addEventListener('click', function () {
                    toggleEditDropdown();
                    openResetModal();
                });
            }
        });

        // ── Reset DR ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

        function openResetModal() {
            document.getElementById('resetDRModal').style.display = 'flex';
            document.getElementById('resetConfirmInput').value = '';
            document.getElementById('resetProgressWrap').style.display  = 'none';
            document.getElementById('resetSuccessWrap').style.display   = 'none';
            document.getElementById('resetErrorWrap').style.display     = 'none';
            document.getElementById('resetFieldsWarning').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('resetProgressBar').style.width     = '0%';
            document.getElementById('resetProgressMsg').textContent     = '';
            document.getElementById('resetApplyBtn').disabled           = true;
            document.getElementById('resetApplyBtn').style.opacity      = '.5';
            document.getElementById('resetApplyBtn').style.cursor       = 'not-allowed';
            document.querySelector('input[name="resetHistoryOption"][value="keep"]').checked = true;
            // Default: all unchecked — user must make a conscious choice
            document.querySelectorAll('.reset-field-cb').forEach(cb => cb.checked = false);
            // Load scope info
            loadResetScope();
        }

        function loadResetScope() {
            const scopeMsg = document.getElementById('resetScopeMsg');
            const scopeWrap = document.getElementById('resetScopeWrap');
            scopeMsg.textContent = 'Loading scope...';
            scopeWrap.style.borderColor = '#dee2e6';

            const query = window.location.search.slice(1); // strip leading '?'
            const url   = "{% url appname|add:':reset_dr_preview' %}"
                          + '?expected_servers={{ unique_servers_count }}'
                          + (query ? '&query=' + encodeURIComponent(query) : '');

            fetch(url)
                .then(r => r.json())
                .then(data => {
                    if (data.error) {
                        scopeMsg.textContent = '❌ ' + data.error;
                        return;
                    }
                    if (!data.is_filtered) {
                        // No active filter — strong warning
                        scopeWrap.style.borderColor = '#dc3545';
                        scopeMsg.innerHTML =
                            '<span style="color:#dc3545;font-weight:bold;">⚠ No active filter</span> — '
                            + `this will reset <strong>all ${data.count_unique} servers</strong> `
                            + `(${data.count_total} total occurrences) in the database.`;
                    } else {
                        scopeWrap.style.borderColor = '#198754';
                        scopeMsg.innerHTML =
                            '<span style="color:#198754;">✓ Filtered scope</span> — '
                            + `<strong>${data.count_unique} unique servers</strong> `
                            + `(${data.count_total} total occurrences) will be affected.`;
                    }
                })
                .catch(() => {
                    scopeMsg.textContent = '❌ Could not load scope.';
                });
        }

        function closeResetModal() {
            document.getElementById('resetDRModal').style.display = 'none';
        }

        function setAllResetFields(checked) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.reset-field-cb').forEach(cb => cb.checked = checked);
        }

        function getSelectedResetFields() {
            return [...document.querySelectorAll('.reset-field-cb:checked')].map(cb => cb.value);
        }

        function checkResetConfirm() {
            const val    = document.getElementById('resetConfirmInput').value.trim().toUpperCase();
            const fields = getSelectedResetFields();
            const ok     = val === 'CONFIRM' && fields.length > 0;
            const btn    = document.getElementById('resetApplyBtn');
            btn.disabled       = !ok;
            btn.style.opacity  = ok ? '1'       : '.5';
            btn.style.cursor   = ok ? 'pointer' : 'not-allowed';
            document.getElementById('resetFieldsWarning').style.display =
                fields.length === 0 ? 'block' : 'none';
        }

        // Also recheck when checkboxes change
        document.addEventListener('change', function(e) {
            if (e.target.classList.contains('reset-field-cb')) checkResetConfirm();
        });

        function startResetDR() {
            const keepHistory = document.querySelector('input[name="resetHistoryOption"]:checked').value === 'keep';
            const fields      = getSelectedResetFields();
            const csrf        = document.querySelector('[name=csrfmiddlewaretoken]').value;

            if (!fields.length) {
                showResetError('Please select at least one field to reset.');
                return;
            }

            document.getElementById('resetApplyBtn').disabled      = true;
            document.getElementById('resetProgressWrap').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('resetSuccessWrap').style.display  = 'none';
            document.getElementById('resetErrorWrap').style.display    = 'none';

            fetch("{% url appname|add:':reset_dr' %}", {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',
                    'X-CSRFToken':  csrf,
                },
                body: new URLSearchParams({
                    confirm_token: 'CONFIRM',
                    keep_history:  keepHistory ? '1' : '0',
                    fields:        fields.join(','),
                    query:         window.location.search.slice(1),
                    expected_servers: '{{ unique_servers_count }}',
                }),
            })
            .then(r => r.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.error) {
                    showResetError(data.error);
                    return;
                }
                pollResetStatus(data.job_id);
            })
            .catch(err => showResetError('Network error: ' + err));
        }

        function pollResetStatus(jobId) {
            const statusUrl = "{% url appname|add:':reset_dr_status' job_id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' %}"
                              .replace('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', jobId);
            const interval  = setInterval(() => {
                fetch(statusUrl)
                    .then(r => r.json())
                    .then(status => {
                        document.getElementById('resetProgressBar').style.width =
                            (status.progress || 0) + '%';
                        document.getElementById('resetProgressMsg').textContent =
                            status.message || '';

                        if (status.done) {
                            clearInterval(interval);
                            if (status.error) {
                                showResetError(status.error);
                            } else {
                                document.getElementById('resetSuccessMsg').textContent =
                                    (status.stats && status.stats.message) || 'Reset complete.';
                                document.getElementById('resetSuccessWrap').style.display = 'block';
                                document.getElementById('resetProgressWrap').style.display = 'none';
                                // Reload the server list after 2s
                                setTimeout(() => { closeResetModal(); window.location.reload(); }, 2500);
                            }
                        }
                    })
                    .catch(() => {});
            }, 800);
        }

        function showResetError(msg) {
            document.getElementById('resetErrorMsg').textContent  = msg;
            document.getElementById('resetErrorWrap').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('resetProgressWrap').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('resetApplyBtn').disabled = false;
            document.getElementById('resetApplyBtn').style.opacity = '1';
            document.getElementById('resetApplyBtn').style.cursor  = 'pointer';
        }


        document.querySelectorAll('.export-option').forEach(item => {
            item.addEventListener('click', function () {
                const selectedValue = this.getAttribute('data-value');
                if (selectedValue === 'chart') {
                    openChartModal();
                } else if (selectedValue === 'changes') {
                    window.open("{% url appname|add:':changes_summary' %}", '_blank');
                } else if (selectedValue === 'excel') {
                    if (ActiveFiltersDetected()) {
                        startExport('xlsx');
                    } else {
                        const userConfirmation = confirm("No filters are applied. Do you really want to export all data?");
                        if (userConfirmation) {
                            startExport('xlsx');
                        }
                    }
                } else if (selectedValue === 'csv') {
                    if (ActiveFiltersDetected()) {
                        startExport('csv');
                    } else {
                        const userConfirmation = confirm("No filters are applied. Do you really want to export all data?");
                        if (userConfirmation) {
                            startExport('csv');
                        }
                    }
                }
                toggleExportDropdown();
            });
        });
        
       
        function startExport(filetype) {
            const visibleColumns = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.column-checkbox'))
                                        .filter(checkbox => checkbox.checked)
                                        .map(checkbox => checkbox.getAttribute('data-column'));

            const searchParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
            const filters = Object.fromEntries(searchParams);
            const exportBtn = document.querySelector('.export-btn');
            const originalText = exportBtn.textContent;

            exportBtn.textContent = '⏳ Exporting...';
            exportBtn.disabled = true;
            document.body.classList.add('waiting-cursor');
            
            // Step 1: Start the export
            fetch(`/${appname}/export/${filetype}/`, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                    'X-CSRFToken': document.querySelector('[name=csrfmiddlewaretoken]').value
                },
                body: JSON.stringify({
                    filters: filters,
                    columns: visibleColumns,
                    permanentfilterselection: '{{ permanent_filter_selection }}'
                })
            })
            .then(response => response.json())
            .then(data => {
                const jobId = data.job_id;

                // Step 2 : check the statut every 2s
                const interval = setInterval(() => {
                    fetch(`/${appname}/export/status/${jobId}/${filetype}/`)

                        .then(resp => resp.json())
                        .then(statusData => {
                            if (statusData.status === 'ready') {
                                clearInterval(interval);

                                // Step 3 : force the download
                                const a = document.createElement('a');
                                a.href = `/${appname}/export/download/${jobId}/${filetype}/`;
                                a.download = 'export.${filetype}';
                                document.body.appendChild(a);
                                a.click();
                                a.remove();

                                exportBtn.textContent = originalText;
                                exportBtn.disabled = false;
                                document.body.classList.remove('waiting-cursor');
                            }
                        });
                }, 2000);
            })
            .catch(error => {

                console.error('Export error:', error);
                exportBtn.textContent = originalText;
                exportBtn.disabled = false;
                document.body.classList.remove('waiting-cursor');
            });
        }        


        /* -- Bulk Edit Operations -- */

        let bulkRunning = false;  // a job is running: Apply stays locked until the page reloads

        // Real number of servers the edit applies to, following the exception option
        function updateBulkScope() {
            const scope = document.getElementById('bulkScope');
            const option = document.getElementById('bulk_include_exceptions');
            if (!scope || !option) return;
            const servers = Number(scope.dataset.servers);
            const exceptions = Number(scope.dataset.exceptions);
            const plural = n => n === 1 ? '' : 's';
            scope.innerHTML = '';
            const strong = text => { const b = document.createElement('strong'); b.textContent = text; return b; };
            if (option.checked) {
                const included = document.createElement('span');
                included.className = 'bulk-scope-included';
                included.append(strong(`including the ${exceptions} in exception`));
                scope.append('Applies to ', strong(`${servers}`), ` server${plural(servers)}, `, included, '.');
            } else {
                const kept = servers - exceptions;
                const ignored = document.createElement('span');
                ignored.className = 'bulk-scope-included';
                ignored.append(strong(`The ${exceptions} server${plural(exceptions)} in exception ${exceptions === 1 ? 'is' : 'are'} ignored.`));
                scope.append('Applies to ', strong(`${kept}`), ` server${plural(kept)}. `, ignored);
            }
            option.closest('label').classList.toggle('is-checked', option.checked);
        }

        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            const option = document.getElementById('bulk_include_exceptions');
            if (option) option.addEventListener('change', updateBulkScope);
            updateBulkScope();
        });


        // Filter syntax help, next to the filters: opens on click, closes on a click elsewhere or Escape
        (function () {
            const button = document.getElementById('filterHelpButton');
            const panel = document.getElementById('filterHelp');
            if (!button || !panel) return;

            function place() {
                const rect = button.getBoundingClientRect();
                const width = Math.min(panel.offsetWidth || 560, window.innerWidth - 32);
                panel.style.top = `${rect.bottom + 6}px`;
                panel.style.left = `${Math.max(16, Math.min(rect.left, window.innerWidth - width - 16))}px`;
            }
            function show(open) {
                panel.hidden = !open;
                button.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(open));
                if (open) place();
            }
            button.addEventListener('click', event => {
                event.stopPropagation();
                show(panel.hidden);
            });
            document.getElementById('filterHelpClose').addEventListener('click', () => show(false));
            document.addEventListener('click', event => {
                if (!panel.hidden && !panel.contains(event.target)) show(false);
            });
            document.addEventListener('keydown', event => {
                if (event.key === 'Escape' && !panel.hidden) show(false);
            });
            window.addEventListener('resize', () => { if (!panel.hidden) place(); });
        })();


        function showBulkForm() {
            document.getElementById('bulkEditForm').style.display = '';
            document.getElementById('bulkReview').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('progressContainer').style.display = 'none';
        }


        // Count of a review line: a link opening the list when there is something to list
        function addBulkCountRow(parent, count, label, color, list) {
            const row = document.createElement('div');
            row.className = 'bulk-review-row';
            const dot = document.createElement('span');
            dot.className = 'bulk-review-dot';
            dot.style.background = color;
            const number = document.createElement(count > 0 ? 'a' : 'span');
            number.className = 'bulk-review-count';
            number.textContent = count;
            if (count > 0) {
                number.dataset.list = label;
                number.addEventListener('click', () => openBulkList(label, list, count));
            }
            const text = document.createElement('span');
            text.textContent = label;
            row.append(dot, number, text);
            parent.appendChild(row);
        }


        let bulkListFull = 0;
        let bulkFingerprint = '';  // identifies the reviewed changes

        function updateBulkListCount(visible, total) {
            const el = document.getElementById('bulkReviewListCount');
            if (visible !== total) {
                el.textContent = `${visible} / ${total} servers`;
            } else if (bulkListFull > total) {
                el.textContent = `${total} of ${bulkListFull} servers`;  // list capped by the server
            } else {
                el.textContent = `${total} server${total !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
            }
        }


        function openBulkList(label, list, count) {
            const box = document.getElementById('bulkReviewList');
            box.innerHTML = '';
            list.forEach(entry => {
                const item = document.createElement('div');
                item.className = 'bulk-review-item';
                const host = document.createElement('div');
                host.className = 'bulk-review-host';
                host.textContent = typeof entry === 'string' ? entry : entry.hostname;
                item.appendChild(host);
                (entry.changes || []).forEach(change => {
                    const line = document.createElement('div');
                    line.className = 'bulk-review-change';
                    line.textContent = `${change.field}: ${change.old || '(empty)'} \u2192 ${change.new}`;
                    item.appendChild(line);
                });
                item.dataset.search = item.textContent.toLowerCase();
                box.appendChild(item);
            });
            bulkListFull = count;
            document.getElementById('bulkReviewListTitle').textContent = `${count} ${label}`;
            document.getElementById('bulkReviewSearch').value = '';
            updateBulkListCount(list.length, list.length);
            document.getElementById('bulkReviewListBox').style.display = 'block';
        }


        document.getElementById('bulkReviewSearch').addEventListener('input', function () {
            const query = this.value.toLowerCase().trim();
            const items = document.querySelectorAll('#bulkReviewList .bulk-review-item');
            let visible = 0;
            items.forEach(item => {
                const match = !query || item.dataset.search.includes(query);
                item.style.display = match ? '' : 'none';
                if (match) visible++;
            });
            updateBulkListCount(visible, items.length);
        });


        function renderBulkReview(preview) {
            const hasFilter = document.querySelectorAll('.filter-tag').length > 0;
            document.getElementById('bulkReviewScope').textContent =
                `Selection: ${preview.servers} server${preview.servers !== 1 ? 's' : ''}` + (hasFilter ? ', from the active filters.' : '.');

            const counts = document.getElementById('bulkReviewCounts');
            counts.innerHTML = '';
            addBulkCountRow(counts, preview.changed, 'will be modified', '#e8910c', preview.lists.changed);
            addBulkCountRow(counts, preview.unchanged, 'already up to date', '#4caf50', preview.lists.unchanged);
            if (preview.skipped_exceptions) {
                addBulkCountRow(counts, preview.skipped_exceptions, 'in exception, ignored', '#8a8f98', preview.lists.skipped_exceptions);
            }
            if (preview.exceptions) {
                addBulkCountRow(counts, preview.exceptions, 'in exception, will be modified', '#b5179e', preview.lists.exceptions);
            }

            const fields = document.getElementById('bulkReviewFields');
            fields.innerHTML = '';
            const names = Object.keys(preview.field_counts);
            fields.style.display = names.length ? '' : 'none';
            names.forEach(name => {
                const row = document.createElement('div');
                row.className = 'bulk-review-row';
                const number = document.createElement('span');
                number.className = 'bulk-review-count';
                number.textContent = preview.field_counts[name];
                const text = document.createElement('code');
                text.textContent = name;
                row.append(number, text);
                fields.appendChild(row);
            });

            bulkFingerprint = preview.fingerprint || '';
            const warning = document.getElementById('bulkReviewWarning');
            if (preview.requested.length === 0) {
                warning.textContent = 'No value entered: nothing to apply.';
            } else if (preview.changed === 0 && preview.skipped_exceptions) {
                warning.textContent = 'Nothing to apply outside the servers in exception. Tick "Also apply to servers in exception" to include them.';
            } else if (preview.changed === 0) {
                warning.textContent = 'Nothing to apply: no server would change.';
            } else if (!hasFilter) {
                warning.textContent = 'No filter is active: this applies to every server of the current view.';
            } else {
                warning.textContent = '';
            }
            warning.style.display = warning.textContent ? 'block' : 'none';

            const apply = document.getElementById('btnBulkApply');
            apply.style.display = preview.changed === 0 ? 'none' : '';
            apply.disabled = false;
            apply.textContent = `Apply to ${preview.changed} server${preview.changed !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`;
            document.getElementById('btnBulkBack').disabled = false;
            document.getElementById('bulkReviewListBox').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('bulkEditForm').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('bulkReview').style.display = 'block';
        }


        function reviewBulkUpdate() {
            const button = document.getElementById('btnBulkReview');
            const body = new URLSearchParams(new FormData(document.getElementById('bulkEditForm')));
            body.append('dry_run', '1');
            button.disabled = true;
            fetch("{% url appname|add:':servers_bulk_update' %}", {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',
                    'X-CSRFToken': document.querySelector('[name=csrfmiddlewaretoken]').value
                },
                body: body
            })
            .then(r => r.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.status !== 'preview') {
                    alert(data.message || 'Could not prepare the bulk update.');
                    return;
                }
                renderBulkReview(data);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                console.error('Fetch error:', error);
                alert('An error occurred while preparing the bulk update.');
            })
            .finally(() => { button.disabled = false; });
        }


        function startBulkUpdate() {

            if (bulkRunning) {
                return;
            }
            bulkRunning = true;
            const applyButton = document.getElementById('btnBulkApply');
            applyButton.disabled = true;
            applyButton.textContent = 'Applying...';
            document.getElementById('btnBulkBack').disabled = true;
            const form         = document.getElementById('bulkEditForm');
            const formData     = new FormData(form);
            const queryParams  = new URLSearchParams(formData);
            queryParams.append('fingerprint', bulkFingerprint);
            const progressContainer = document.getElementById('progressContainer');
            const progressBar       = document.getElementById('progressBar');
            const progressMessage   = document.getElementById('progressMessage');
            let   pollInterval      = null;

            progressContainer.style.display = 'block';
            progressBar.style.width = '0%';
            progressMessage.textContent = 'Starting bulk update...';

            // Step 1 — POST to start the background job
            fetch("{% url appname|add:':servers_bulk_update' %}", {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {
                    'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded',
                    'X-CSRFToken': document.querySelector('[name=csrfmiddlewaretoken]').value
                },
                body: queryParams
            })
            .then(r => r.json())
            .then(data => {
                if (data.status === 'error' || data.status === 'warning') {
                    progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    alert(data.message);
                    bulkRunning = false;
                    reviewBulkUpdate();
                    return;
                }
                if (!data.job_id) {
                    progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                    alert('Could not start bulk update job.');
                    bulkRunning = false;
                    reviewBulkUpdate();
                    return;
                }

                // Step 2 — poll for progress every 800ms
                const statusUrl = `{% url appname|add:':bulk_update_status' job_id='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' %}`.replace('00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000', data.job_id);

                pollInterval = setInterval(() => {
                    fetch(statusUrl)
                        .then(r => r.json())
                        .then(status => {
                            progressBar.style.width    = `${status.progress || 0}%`;
                            progressMessage.textContent = status.message || '';

                            if (status.done) {
                                clearInterval(pollInterval);
                                if (status.error) {
                                    progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                                    alert('Bulk update error: ' + status.error);
                                    bulkRunning = false;
                                    reviewBulkUpdate();  // shows what is still to apply, saved batches stay saved
                                } else {
                                    progressBar.style.width = '100%';
                                    progressMessage.textContent = (status.stats && status.stats.message)
                                        ? status.stats.message
                                        : 'Bulk update completed!';
                                    setTimeout(() => {
                                        progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                                        window.location.reload();
                                    }, 2000);
                                }
                            }
                        })
                        .catch(() => {}); // transient network error — retry next tick
                }, 800);
            })
            .catch(error => {
                progressContainer.style.display = 'none';
                console.error('Fetch error:', error);
                alert('An error occurred while starting the bulk update.');
                bulkRunning = false;
                reviewBulkUpdate();
            });
        }
        

        function closeBulkModal() {  // Hide the bulk edit modal
            document.getElementById('bulkEditModal').style.display = 'none';
            if (!bulkRunning) {
                showBulkForm();
            }
        }


        /* -- Export and Search buttons click events -- */

        window.onclick = function (event) {

            if (!event.target.matches('.search-btn') && !event.target.closest('.search-dropdown-content')) {  // Close the saved searches dropdown if the user clicks outside of it
                const savedSearchesDropdown = document.getElementById("dropdownContent");
                if (savedSearchesDropdown && savedSearchesDropdown.style.display === 'block') {
                    savedSearchesDropdown.style.display = 'none';
                }
            }
            if (!event.target.matches('.export-btn') && !event.target.closest('.export-dropdown-content')) {  // Close the export dropdown if the user clicks outside of it
                const exportDropdownContent = document.getElementById("exportDropdownContent");
                if (exportDropdownContent && exportDropdownContent.style.display === 'block') {
                    exportDropdownContent.style.display = 'none';
                }
            }
            if (!event.target.matches('.edit-btn') && !event.target.closest('#editDropdownContent')) {  // Close the edit dropdown if the user clicks outside of it
                const editDropdownContent = document.getElementById('editDropdownContent');
                if (editDropdownContent && editDropdownContent.style.display === 'block') {
                    editDropdownContent.style.display = 'none';
                }
            }
            if (!event.target.matches('.permanentfilter-btn') && !event.target.closest('.permanentfilter-dropdown-content')) {  // Close the permanent filter dropdown if the user clicks outside of it
                const permanentFilterDropdown = document.getElementById("permanentFilterDropdownContent");
                if (permanentFilterDropdown && permanentFilterDropdown.style.display === 'block') {
                    permanentFilterDropdown.style.display = 'none';
                }
            }

        };
        
        
        /* -- Permanent Filter -- */

        function togglePermanentFilterDropdown() {
            const dropdownContent = document.getElementById('permanentFilterDropdownContent');
            dropdownContent.style.display = dropdownContent.style.display === 'block' ? 'none' : 'block';
        }

        function selectPermanentFilter(filter) {
            const form = document.createElement('form');
            form.method = 'post';
            form.action = "{% url appname|add:':update_permanentfilter_field' %}";

            const csrfToken = document.createElement('input');
            csrfToken.type = 'hidden';
            csrfToken.name = 'csrfmiddlewaretoken';
            csrfToken.value = '{{ csrf_token }}';
            form.appendChild(csrfToken);

            const filterInput = document.createElement('input');
            filterInput.type = 'hidden';
            filterInput.name = 'permanentfilter_choice';
            filterInput.value = filter;
            form.appendChild(filterInput);

            document.body.appendChild(form);
            form.submit();

            const dropdownBtn = document.querySelector('#permanentfilter-container .permanentfilter-btn');  // Update the button text to reflect the selected filter
            dropdownBtn.innerHTML = `${filter} &nbsp;&nbsp; ▼`;
        }

        (function() {
            function fixColumns() {
                const table = document.querySelector('#serversTable');
                if (!table) return;
                
                const col1 = table.querySelectorAll('th:nth-child(1), td:nth-child(1)');
                const col2 = table.querySelectorAll('th:nth-child(2), td:nth-child(2)');
                
                const w1 = col1[0]?.offsetWidth || 0;
                const w2 = col2[0]?.offsetWidth || 0;
                col1.forEach(cell => cell.style.left = '0px');
                col2.forEach(cell => cell.style.left = w1 + 'px');

            }
            
            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fixColumns);
            } else {
                fixColumns();
            }
            }
        )();        
  
    </script>

================================================================================
# tests/base.py

"""Shared helpers for the businesscontinuity tests."""
import json
from importlib import import_module
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest import mock

from django.conf import settings
from django.contrib.auth import get_user_model
from django.core.files.uploadedfile import SimpleUploadedFile
from django.test import Client, TestCase
from django.urls import reverse

from accessrights.models import Permission, UserPermission
from businesscontinuity.models import Server, ServerUnique

CLASSIC_HEADER = 'hostname_unique;priority_asset;in_live_play;action_during_lp;annotation;cluster;cluster_type;bc_exception\r\n'
EXPORT_HEADER = 'SERVER_ID;DATACENTER;priority_asset;in_live_play;action_during_lp;annotation;cluster;cluster_type;bc_exception\r\n'
APP_HEADER = 'application_id;in_live_play\r\n'


class _ImmediateThread:
    """Runs the job in the test's own database transaction."""

    def __init__(self, target, args=(), kwargs=None, daemon=None):
        self.target, self.args, self.kwargs = target, args, kwargs or {}

    def start(self):
        self.target(*self.args, **self.kwargs)


def logged_client(user):
    """Return a client logged in without firing user_logged_in, which needs real request data."""
    store = import_module(settings.SESSION_ENGINE).SessionStore()
    store['_auth_user_id'] = str(user.pk)
    store['_auth_user_backend'] = settings.AUTHENTICATION_BACKENDS[0]
    store['_auth_user_hash'] = user.get_session_auth_hash()
    store.save()
    client = Client()
    client.cookies[settings.SESSION_COOKIE_NAME] = store.session_key
    return client


def make_server(hostname, rows=None, **values):
    """Create a ServerUnique and its inventory lines."""
    defaults = {'priority_asset': 'NO', 'in_live_play': 'NO', 'action_during_lp': 'OLD ACTION',
                'annotation': 'OLD NOTE', 'cluster': 'NO', 'cluster_type': 'OLD TYPE', 'bc_exception': 'NO', 'global_history': []}
    su = ServerUnique.objects.create(hostname=hostname, **{**defaults, **values})
    for row in rows or [{}]:
        Server.objects.create(server_unique=su, SERVER_ID=hostname, **row)
    return su


class BCTestCase(TestCase):
    """Logged-in editor, with background jobs run synchronously."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        cls.user = get_user_model().objects.create_user(username='tester', password='x')
        permission = Permission.objects.create(codename='businesscontinuity.edit', app='businesscontinuity', action='edit')
        UserPermission.objects.create(user=cls.user, permission=permission)

    def setUp(self):
        self.client = logged_client(self.user)
        immediate = SimpleNamespace(Thread=_ImmediateThread)
        for target in ('businesscontinuity.views.threading', 'businesscontinuity.bc_rules_views.threading'):
            patcher = mock.patch(target, immediate)
            patcher.start()
            self.addCleanup(patcher.stop)

    # Import page

    def upload(self, content, name='file.csv', import_type='server', **extra):
        raw = content if isinstance(content, bytes) else content.encode('utf-8')
        file = SimpleUploadedFile(name, raw, content_type='text/csv')
        return self.client.post(reverse('businesscontinuity:bulk_import_csv'),
                                {'csv_file': file, 'import_type': import_type, **extra})

    def preview(self, content, import_type='server'):
        response = self.upload(content, import_type=import_type, dry_run='1')
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.content)
        return response.json()

    def run_import(self, content, name='file.csv', import_type='server'):
        """Launch an import and return its finished status."""
        response = self.upload(content, name=name, import_type=import_type)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.content)
        return self.job_status('businesscontinuity:import_csv_status', response.json()['job_id'])

    def job_status(self, url_name, job_id):
        status = self.client.get(reverse(url_name, kwargs={'job_id': job_id})).json()
        self.assertTrue(status['done'], status)
        return status

    # Bulk edit

    def bulk(self, query, **values):
        return self.client.post(reverse('businesscontinuity:servers_bulk_update'), {'query': query, **values})

    def run_bulk(self, query, **values):
        response = self.bulk(query, **values)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.content)
        return self.job_status('businesscontinuity:bulk_update_status', response.json()['job_id'])

    # JSON views

    def post_json(self, url_name, payload):
        return self.client.post(reverse(url_name), json.dumps(payload), content_type='application/json')

================================================================================
# tests/test_bc_rules.py
================================================================================
"""Apply BC Rules: scope, sibling reset, shared servers, identical reruns."""
import copy
from unittest import mock

from django.urls import reverse

from businesscontinuity import bc_rules_views as rules
from businesscontinuity.bc_rules_logic import DEFAULT_TEXTS, RULE_HYPERVISOR
from businesscontinuity.models import ServerUnique
from businesscontinuity.tests.base import BCTestCase, make_server

P1, P2, OTHER = 'TEST-DC-A', 'TEST-DC-B', 'TEST-DC-C'
QUERY = f'datacenter=%40{P1}'


def hypervisor(datacenter):
    return {'DATACENTER': datacenter, 'MACHINE_TYPE': 'HYPERVISOR'}


class BCRulesTests(BCTestCase):

    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        # The real file holds site-specific names: tests bring their own pairs
        labels = copy.deepcopy(rules._load_field_labels())
        labels['bc_datacenter_siblings'] = {P1: P2, P2: P1}
        patcher = mock.patch.object(rules, '_load_field_labels', return_value=labels)
        patcher.start()
        self.addCleanup(patcher.stop)

        self.in_scope = make_server('IN-SCOPE', rows=[hypervisor(P1)])
        self.sibling = make_server('ON-SIBLING', rows=[hypervisor(P2)])
        self.shared = make_server('ON-BOTH', rows=[hypervisor(P1), hypervisor(P2)])
        self.elsewhere = make_server('ELSEWHERE', rows=[hypervisor(OTHER)])

    def scope(self, query, **extra):
        return self.client.get(reverse('businesscontinuity:bc_scope'), {'query': query, **extra})

    def payload(self, sibling=P2, rule_texts=None, query=QUERY):
        return {'query': query, 'sibling': sibling, 'rule_texts': rule_texts or {}}

    def run_job(self, **kwargs):
        response = self.post_json('businesscontinuity:bc_start', self.payload(**kwargs))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.content)
        return self.job_status('businesscontinuity:bc_job_status', response.json()['job_id'])

    def value(self, su):
        su.refresh_from_db()
        return su.action_during_lp

    def test_scope_refusals(self):
        for label, query, message in (
            ('no filter', '', 'required'),
            ('no datacenter', 'environment=PRD', 'required'),
            ('two datacenters', f'datacenter={P1},{P2}', 'exactly one'),
            ('no match', 'datacenter=NOPE', 'No servers'),
        ):
            with self.subTest(label):
                response = self.scope(query)
                self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
                self.assertIn(message, response.json()['error'])

    def test_datacenter_outside_the_configuration_is_refused(self):
        with mock.patch.object(rules, '_load_field_labels', return_value={'bc_datacenter_siblings': {P2: P1}}):
            response = self.scope(QUERY)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
        self.assertIn('not configured', response.json()['error'])

    def test_scope_proposes_the_sibling_and_reports_shared_servers(self):
        data = self.scope(QUERY).json()
        self.assertEqual((data['datacenter'], data['default_sibling']), (P1, P2))
        self.assertIn('ON-BOTH', data['sibling']['shared'])

    def test_same_datacenter_as_sibling_is_refused(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.scope(QUERY, sibling=P1).status_code, 400)

    def test_preview_writes_nothing(self):
        response = self.post_json('businesscontinuity:bc_preview', self.payload())
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.content)
        self.assertEqual(self.value(self.in_scope), 'OLD ACTION')
        self.assertEqual(self.value(self.sibling), 'OLD ACTION')

    def test_job_applies_the_rule_and_resets_the_sibling(self):
        status = self.run_job()
        self.assertIsNone(status['error'])
        self.assertEqual(self.value(self.in_scope), DEFAULT_TEXTS[RULE_HYPERVISOR])
        self.assertEqual(self.value(self.sibling), 'EMPTY')
        self.assertEqual(self.sibling.global_history[0]['fromsource'], 'BC Rules Reset')

    def test_server_on_both_sites_gets_the_rule_and_is_not_reset(self):
        self.run_job()
        self.assertEqual(self.value(self.shared), DEFAULT_TEXTS[RULE_HYPERVISOR])

    def test_other_datacenters_are_untouched(self):
        self.run_job()
        self.assertEqual(self.value(self.elsewhere), 'OLD ACTION')
        self.assertEqual(self.elsewhere.global_history, [])

    def test_without_sibling_nothing_is_reset(self):
        status = self.run_job(sibling=None)
        self.assertEqual(status['stats']['reset'], 0)
        self.assertEqual(self.value(self.sibling), 'OLD ACTION')

    def test_identical_rerun_writes_nothing(self):
        self.run_job(sibling=None)
        before = ServerUnique.objects.get(pk=self.in_scope.pk).global_history
        status = self.run_job(sibling=None)
        self.assertEqual(status['stats']['updated'], 0)
        self.assertEqual(ServerUnique.objects.get(pk=self.in_scope.pk).global_history, before)

    def test_exception_on_the_affected_site_is_ignored(self):
        excepted = make_server('EXCEPTED', bc_exception='YES', rows=[hypervisor(P1)])
        data = self.post_json('businesscontinuity:bc_preview', self.payload()).json()
        self.assertEqual(data['exceptions']['count'], 1)
        self.assertEqual(data['exceptions']['servers'], ['EXCEPTED'])
        self.assertEqual(data['total'], 2)
        status = self.run_job()
        self.assertEqual(status['stats']['exceptions']['count'], 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.value(excepted), 'OLD ACTION')
        self.assertEqual(excepted.global_history, [])
        self.assertEqual(self.value(self.in_scope), DEFAULT_TEXTS[RULE_HYPERVISOR])

    def test_exception_on_the_sibling_site_is_reset_and_announced(self):
        excepted = make_server('SIBLING-EXCEPTED', bc_exception='YES', rows=[hypervisor(P2)])
        data = self.post_json('businesscontinuity:bc_preview', self.payload()).json()
        self.assertEqual(data['sibling']['exceptions_reset'], {'count': 1, 'servers': ['SIBLING-EXCEPTED'], 'truncated': False})
        status = self.run_job()
        self.assertEqual(status['stats']['exceptions_reset'], 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.value(excepted), 'EMPTY')

    def test_only_exceptions_in_scope_still_resets_the_sibling(self):
        ServerUnique.objects.filter(pk__in=[self.in_scope.pk, self.shared.pk]).update(bc_exception='YES')
        status = self.run_job()
        self.assertIsNone(status['error'])
        self.assertEqual((status['stats']['total'], status['stats']['exceptions']['count']), (0, 2))
        self.assertEqual(self.value(self.sibling), 'EMPTY')
        self.assertEqual(self.value(self.in_scope), 'OLD ACTION')

    def test_blank_text_is_refused_and_empty_is_accepted(self):
        for label, blank in (('empty', ''), ('spaces', '   '), ('null', None)):
            with self.subTest(label):
                response = self.post_json('businesscontinuity:bc_start', self.payload(rule_texts={RULE_HYPERVISOR: blank}))
                self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
                self.assertEqual(self.value(self.in_scope), 'OLD ACTION')
        response = self.post_json('businesscontinuity:bc_preview', self.payload(rule_texts={RULE_HYPERVISOR: 'EMPTY'}))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)

    def test_non_editor_cannot_start(self):
        with mock.patch.object(rules, 'is_editor', return_value=False):
            response = self.post_json('businesscontinuity:bc_start', self.payload())
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 403)
        self.assertEqual(self.value(self.in_scope), 'OLD ACTION')

================================================================================
# tests/test_edits.py
