"""Bulk edit, DR reset and single edit: what is written, and what the history says."""
from unittest import mock

from django.urls import reverse

from businesscontinuity.models import ServerUnique
from businesscontinuity.tests.base import BCTestCase, make_server


def reload(su):
    su.refresh_from_db()
    return su


class BulkEditTests(BCTestCase):

    def test_review_writes_nothing_and_counts_real_changes(self):
        a = make_server('SRV1')
        make_server('SRV2', priority_asset='YES')
        data = self.bulk('server=SRV1,SRV2', priority_asset='YES', dry_run='1').json()
        self.assertEqual(data['status'], 'preview')
        self.assertEqual((data['servers'], data['changed'], data['unchanged']), (2, 1, 1))
        self.assertEqual(data['lists']['changed'][0]['changes'], [{'field': 'priority_asset', 'old': 'NO', 'new': 'YES'}])
        self.assertEqual(reload(a).priority_asset, 'NO')

    def test_servers_are_counted_not_inventory_lines(self):
        make_server('SRV1', rows=[{'DATACENTER': 'A'}, {'DATACENTER': 'B'}])
        self.assertEqual(self.bulk('server=SRV1', annotation='NEW', dry_run='1').json()['servers'], 1)

    def test_only_real_changes_reach_the_history(self):
        changed = make_server('SRV1')
        same = make_server('SRV2', priority_asset='YES', action_during_lp='NEW')
        self.run_bulk('server=SRV1,SRV2', priority_asset='YES', action_during_lp='NEW')
        self.assertEqual(reload(changed).priority_asset, 'YES')
        self.assertEqual(reload(same).global_history, [])

    def test_a_dash_and_an_empty_text_mean_unchanged(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        self.run_bulk('server=SRV1', priority_asset='-', in_live_play='-', cluster='-', annotation='NEW', action_during_lp='', cluster_type='')
        reload(su)
        self.assertEqual((su.priority_asset, su.action_during_lp, su.annotation, su.cluster_type), ('NO', 'OLD ACTION', 'NEW', 'OLD TYPE'))

    def test_back_to_empty_is_traced(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        self.run_bulk('server=SRV1', action_during_lp='EMPTY', annotation='EMPTY')
        reload(su)
        self.assertEqual(su.action_during_lp, 'EMPTY')
        self.assertEqual(set(su.global_history[0]['changes']), {'action_during_lp', 'annotation'})
        self.assertIn('EMPTY', su.action_during_lp_history)

    def test_filters_limit_the_selection(self):
        make_server('SRV1')
        other = make_server('OTHER')
        self.run_bulk('server=SRV1', annotation='NEW')
        self.assertEqual(reload(other).annotation, 'OLD NOTE')

    def test_empty_term_in_the_query_does_not_widen_the_selection(self):
        make_server('SRV1')
        make_server('SRV2')
        other = make_server('OTHER')
        self.assertEqual(self.bulk('server=SRV1,,SRV2', annotation='NEW', dry_run='1').json()['servers'], 2)
        self.assertEqual(reload(other).annotation, 'OLD NOTE')

    def test_nothing_to_change_starts_no_job(self):
        make_server('SRV1', priority_asset='YES')
        data = self.bulk('server=SRV1', priority_asset='YES').json()
        self.assertEqual(data['status'], 'warning')
        self.assertNotIn('job_id', data)

    def test_invalid_choice_is_refused(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        response = self.bulk('server=SRV1', priority_asset='WRONG')
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).priority_asset, 'NO')

    def test_too_long_value_is_refused(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        response = self.bulk('server=SRV1', cluster_type='X' * 256)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
        self.assertIn('too long', response.json()['message'])
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).cluster_type, 'OLD TYPE')

    def test_servers_in_exception_are_left_alone_by_default(self):
        excepted = make_server('SRV1', bc_exception='YES')
        normal = make_server('SRV2')
        data = self.bulk('server=SRV1,SRV2', annotation='NEW', dry_run='1').json()
        self.assertEqual((data['changed'], data['unchanged'], data['skipped_exceptions']), (1, 0, 1))
        self.assertEqual(data['lists']['skipped_exceptions'], ['SRV1'])
        status = self.run_bulk('server=SRV1,SRV2', annotation='NEW')
        self.assertIn('1 in exception ignored', status['stats']['message'])
        self.assertEqual(reload(excepted).annotation, 'OLD NOTE')
        self.assertEqual(excepted.global_history, [])
        self.assertEqual(reload(normal).annotation, 'NEW')

    def test_option_includes_servers_in_exception(self):
        excepted = make_server('SRV1', bc_exception='YES')
        data = self.bulk('server=SRV1', annotation='NEW', include_exceptions='1', dry_run='1').json()
        self.assertEqual((data['changed'], data['exceptions'], data['skipped_exceptions']), (1, 1, 0))
        self.run_bulk('server=SRV1', annotation='NEW', include_exceptions='1')
        self.assertEqual(reload(excepted).annotation, 'NEW')

    def test_lifting_exceptions_needs_the_option(self):
        excepted = make_server('SRV1', bc_exception='YES')
        self.assertEqual(self.bulk('server=SRV1', bc_exception='NO').json()['status'], 'warning')
        self.run_bulk('server=SRV1', bc_exception='NO', include_exceptions='1')
        self.assertEqual(reload(excepted).bc_exception, 'NO')
        self.assertEqual(excepted.global_history[0]['changes'], {'bc_exception': {'old': 'YES', 'new': 'NO'}})

    def test_only_excepted_servers_selected_warns(self):
        make_server('SRV1', bc_exception='YES')
        data = self.bulk('server=SRV1', annotation='NEW').json()
        self.assertEqual(data['status'], 'warning')
        self.assertIn('1 server in exception ignored', data['message'])

    def test_selection_must_match_the_page(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        make_server('SRV2')
        for dry_run in ('1', ''):
            with self.subTest(dry_run=dry_run):
                response = self.bulk('server=SRV1,SRV2', annotation='NEW', expected_servers='3', dry_run=dry_run)
                self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 409)
                self.assertIn('would target 2 servers, but the page shows 3', response.json()['message'])
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).annotation, 'OLD NOTE')
        self.assertEqual(self.bulk('server=SRV1,SRV2', annotation='NEW', expected_servers='2', dry_run='1').json()['servers'], 2)

    def test_exceptions_do_not_change_the_selection_count(self):
        make_server('SRV1', bc_exception='YES')
        make_server('SRV2')
        for include in ('', '1'):
            with self.subTest(include=include):
                data = self.bulk('server=SRV1,SRV2', annotation='NEW', expected_servers='2', include_exceptions=include, dry_run='1').json()
                self.assertEqual((data['status'], data['servers']), ('preview', 2))
        status = self.run_bulk('server=SRV1,SRV2', annotation='NEW', expected_servers='2')
        self.assertIsNone(status['error'])

    def test_applied_changes_must_match_the_review(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        other = make_server('SRV2')
        reviewed = self.bulk('server=SRV1,SRV2', annotation='NEW', dry_run='1').json()['fingerprint']
        ServerUnique.objects.filter(pk=other.pk).update(annotation='NEW')  # someone else edits in between
        response = self.bulk('server=SRV1,SRV2', annotation='NEW', fingerprint=reviewed)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 409)
        self.assertIn('no longer the ones you reviewed', response.json()['message'])
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).annotation, 'OLD NOTE')
        fresh = self.bulk('server=SRV1,SRV2', annotation='NEW', dry_run='1').json()['fingerprint']
        self.run_bulk('server=SRV1,SRV2', annotation='NEW', fingerprint=fresh)
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).annotation, 'NEW')

    def test_failure_is_reported(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        with mock.patch.object(ServerUnique.objects, 'bulk_update', side_effect=RuntimeError('simulated failure')):
            status = self.run_bulk('server=SRV1', annotation='NEW')
        self.assertIn('simulated failure', status['error'])
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).annotation, 'OLD NOTE')


class ResetTests(BCTestCase):

    def reset(self, hostname, keep_history, fields='annotation'):
        response = self.client.post(reverse('businesscontinuity:reset_dr'), {
            'confirm_token': 'CONFIRM', 'keep_history': keep_history, 'fields': fields, 'query': f'server=@{hostname}',
        })
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200, response.content)
        return self.job_status('businesscontinuity:reset_dr_status', response.json()['job_id'])

    def old_history(self):
        return [{'date': '2026-01-01 10:00:00', 'user': 'x', 'fromsource': 'Bulk Edit', 'changes': {'cluster': {'old': 'NO', 'new': 'YES'}}}]

    def test_wipe_leaves_a_single_traced_entry(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1', annotation_history='old', action_during_lp_history='kept', global_history=self.old_history())
        self.reset('SRV1', '0')
        reload(su)
        self.assertEqual(su.annotation, 'EMPTY')
        self.assertEqual(len(su.global_history), 1)
        entry = su.global_history[0]
        self.assertEqual((entry['user'], entry['fromsource']), ('tester', 'DR Reset'))
        self.assertEqual(entry['changes']['annotation'], {'old': 'OLD NOTE', 'new': 'EMPTY'})
        self.assertIn('history_wiped', entry['changes'])
        self.assertFalse(su.annotation_history)
        self.assertEqual(su.action_during_lp_history, 'kept')

    def test_wipe_is_traced_even_with_nothing_to_reset(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1', annotation='EMPTY', global_history=self.old_history())
        self.reset('SRV1', '0')
        self.assertEqual(list(reload(su).global_history[0]['changes']), ['history_wiped'])

    def test_keeping_the_history(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1', annotation_history='old', global_history=self.old_history())
        self.reset('SRV1', '1')
        reload(su)
        self.assertEqual(len(su.global_history), 2)
        self.assertEqual(su.annotation_history, 'old')

    def test_exception_can_be_reset(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1', bc_exception='YES')
        self.reset('SRV1', '1', fields='bc_exception')
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).bc_exception, 'EMPTY')

    def test_other_servers_are_untouched(self):
        make_server('SRV1')
        other = make_server('OTHER')
        self.reset('SRV1', '0')
        self.assertEqual(reload(other).annotation, 'OLD NOTE')


class SingleEditTests(BCTestCase):

    def edit(self, su, note, **extra):
        return self.client.post(reverse('businesscontinuity:edit_server_info', kwargs={'server_unique_id': su.hostname}), {
            'priority_asset': su.priority_asset, 'in_live_play': su.in_live_play, 'action_during_lp': su.action_during_lp,
            'annotation': note, 'cluster': su.cluster, 'cluster_type': su.cluster_type,
            'referrer': '/businesscontinuity/', 'query': '', **extra,
        })

    def test_exception_is_edited_and_traced(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        self.assertEqual(self.edit(su, su.annotation, bc_exception='YES').status_code, 302)
        reload(su)
        self.assertEqual(su.bc_exception, 'YES')
        self.assertEqual(su.global_history[-1]['changes']['bc_exception'], {'old': 'NO', 'new': 'YES'})

    def test_choices_left_empty_keep_the_stored_values(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1', priority_asset='EMPTY', in_live_play='YES', cluster='EMPTY')
        self.edit(su, 'NEW', priority_asset='', in_live_play='', cluster='')
        reload(su)
        self.assertEqual((su.priority_asset, su.in_live_play, su.cluster, su.annotation), ('EMPTY', 'YES', 'EMPTY', 'NEW'))
        changes = su.global_history[-1]['changes']
        self.assertEqual(changes['priority_asset']['new'], 'Not set or not modified')

    def test_choices_are_saved(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        self.edit(su, su.annotation, priority_asset='YES', cluster='EMPTY')
        reload(su)
        self.assertEqual((su.priority_asset, su.cluster), ('YES', 'EMPTY'))

    def test_exception_kept_when_the_form_leaves_it_empty(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1', bc_exception='YES')
        self.edit(su, 'NEW', bc_exception='')
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).bc_exception, 'YES')

    def test_author_comes_from_the_session_not_from_the_form(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        self.assertEqual(self.edit(su, 'NEW', loggedonuser='someone-else').status_code, 302)
        reload(su)
        self.assertEqual(su.annotation, 'NEW')
        self.assertEqual(su.global_history[-1]['user'], 'tester')
        self.assertNotIn('someone-else', su.annotation_history)

================================================================================
# tests/test_filters.py
================================================================================
"""construct_query: same syntax and same rules in every app having its own copy of it."""
import ast
import importlib
import os

from django.apps import apps
from django.conf import settings
from django.db.models import Q
from django.test import SimpleTestCase, TestCase

from businesscontinuity.models import Server
from businesscontinuity.tests.base import make_server

MODULES = {
    'businesscontinuity': ['businesscontinuity.views'],
    'inventory': ['inventory.views', 'inventory.views_dbcache'],
    'applications': ['applications.views'],
    'reportapp': ['reportapp.views'],
    'discrepancies': ['discrepancies.views'],
    'hardware': ['hardware.views'],
    'storage': ['storage.views'],
}


def all_construct_queries():
    """Yield (module name, construct_query), even for an app not installed here."""
    for app, modules in MODULES.items():
        installed = apps.is_installed(app) or any(c.name == app for c in apps.get_app_configs())
        for name in modules:
            if installed:
                yield name, importlib.import_module(name).construct_query
                continue
            path = os.path.join(settings.BASE_DIR, *name.split('.')) + '.py'
            with open(path, encoding='utf-8') as f:
                tree = ast.parse(f.read())
            node = next(n for n in tree.body if isinstance(n, ast.FunctionDef) and n.name == 'construct_query')
            space = {'Q': Q}
            exec(compile(ast.Module([node], []), path, 'exec'), space)
            yield name, space['construct_query']


class SyntaxTests(SimpleTestCase):

    def test_empty_terms_are_ignored(self):
        for name, construct_query in all_construct_queries():
            with self.subTest(name):
                self.assertEqual(construct_query('F', ['A', '', 'B']), construct_query('F', ['A', 'B']))
                self.assertEqual(construct_query('F', ['']), Q())
                self.assertEqual(construct_query('F', ['!A', '']), construct_query('F', ['!A']))
                self.assertEqual(construct_query('F', ['@A', '']), construct_query('F', ['@A']))

    def test_order_of_the_terms_does_not_matter(self):
        for name, construct_query in all_construct_queries():
            with self.subTest(name):
                self.assertEqual(construct_query('F', ['!B', 'A']), construct_query('F', ['A', '!B']))
                self.assertEqual(construct_query('F', ['!B', '@A', 'C']), construct_query('F', ['@A', 'C', '!B']))

    def test_excluded_exact_value_in_every_app(self):
        for name, construct_query in all_construct_queries():
            with self.subTest(name):
                self.assertEqual(construct_query('F', ['!@B']), construct_query('F', ['@!B']))
                self.assertEqual(construct_query('F', ['!@B']), Q() & (Q() & ~Q(F__iexact='B')))


class ResultTests(TestCase):
    """What the user actually gets, on real rows."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        for hostname in ('PROD-TEST', 'PROD-X', 'DEV-TEST', 'DEV'):
            make_server(hostname)

    def select(self, terms):
        from businesscontinuity.views import construct_query
        return sorted(Server.objects.filter(construct_query('SERVER_ID', terms)).values_list('SERVER_ID', flat=True))

    def test_values_and_exclusions_in_any_order(self):
        for terms in (['prod', '!test'], ['!test', 'prod']):
            with self.subTest(terms=terms):
                self.assertEqual(self.select(terms), ['PROD-X'])
        for terms in (['prod', 'dev', '!test'], ['!test', 'dev', 'prod']):
            with self.subTest(terms=terms):
                self.assertEqual(self.select(terms), ['DEV', 'PROD-X'])

    def test_exclusions_alone(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.select(['!test']), ['DEV', 'PROD-X'])
        self.assertEqual(self.select(['!@dev']), ['DEV-TEST', 'PROD-TEST', 'PROD-X'])
        self.assertEqual(self.select(['!test', '!@dev']), ['PROD-X'])

    def test_exact_value_and_its_exclusion(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.select(['@DEV']), ['DEV'])
        self.assertEqual(self.select(['@DEV', '!@DEV']), [])
        self.assertEqual(self.select(['dev', '!@dev']), ['DEV-TEST'])

    def test_expand_still_works(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.select(['#@PROD-X']), ['PROD-X'])
        self.assertEqual(self.select(['#prod', '!test']), ['PROD-X'])

# tests/test_import.py
================================================================================
"""CSV imports: server file, full export, applications, journal and report."""
import csv
import io
from unittest import mock

from django.test import Client
from django.urls import reverse

from businesscontinuity.models import CsvImportLog, Server, ServerUnique
from businesscontinuity.tests.base import APP_HEADER, CLASSIC_HEADER, EXPORT_HEADER, BCTestCase, make_server
from businesscontinuity import views


def reload(su):
    su.refresh_from_db()
    return su


class ServerImportTests(BCTestCase):

    def test_only_real_changes_are_written(self):
        changed = make_server('SRV1')
        same = make_server('SRV2')
        status = self.run_import(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;NEW ACTION;;;;\r\nSRV2;;;OLD ACTION;;;;\r\n')
        self.assertEqual((status['stats']['changed'], status['stats']['unchanged']), (1, 1))
        self.assertEqual(reload(changed).action_during_lp, 'NEW ACTION')
        self.assertIn('NEW ACTION', changed.action_during_lp_history)
        self.assertEqual(len(changed.global_history), 1)
        self.assertEqual(reload(same).global_history, [])
        self.assertFalse(same.action_during_lp_history)

    def test_empty_cell_leaves_the_field_alone(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        self.run_import(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;YES;;;;;;\r\n')
        reload(su)
        self.assertEqual((su.priority_asset, su.annotation, su.cluster_type), ('YES', 'OLD NOTE', 'OLD TYPE'))

    def test_values_are_cleaned(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        self.run_import(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1; yes ;No; TEXT ;;EMPTY;;\r\n')
        reload(su)
        self.assertEqual((su.priority_asset, su.in_live_play, su.action_during_lp, su.cluster), ('YES', 'NO', 'TEXT', 'EMPTY'))

    def test_lower_case_stored_value_is_not_a_change(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1', priority_asset='yes')
        self.assertEqual(self.preview(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;YES;;;;;;\r\n')['changed'], 0)
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).priority_asset, 'yes')

    def test_preview_writes_nothing_and_shows_old_and_new(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        data = self.preview(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;NEW ACTION;;;;\r\n')
        self.assertEqual(data['field_counts'], {'action_during_lp': 1})
        self.assertEqual(data['lists']['changed'][0]['changes'],
                         [{'field': 'action_during_lp', 'old': 'OLD ACTION', 'new': 'NEW ACTION'}])
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).action_during_lp, 'OLD ACTION')
        self.assertFalse(CsvImportLog.objects.exists())

    def test_invalid_value_ignores_the_whole_line(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        data = self.preview(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;WRONG;;NEW ACTION;;;;\r\n')
        self.assertEqual((data['changed'], data['invalid']), (0, 1))
        self.assertIn("invalid priority_asset value 'WRONG'", data['lists']['invalid'][0])
        self.assertIn('Line 2', data['lists']['invalid'][0])
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).action_during_lp, 'OLD ACTION')

    def test_too_long_value_ignores_the_whole_line(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        ok = make_server('SRV2')
        data = self.preview(CLASSIC_HEADER + f'SRV1;;;NEW;;;{"X" * 256};\r\nSRV2;;;;;;{"X" * 255};\r\n')
        self.assertEqual((data['changed'], data['invalid']), (1, 1))
        self.assertIn('cluster_type is too long (max 255 characters)', data['lists']['invalid'][0])
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).action_during_lp, 'OLD ACTION')
        self.assertEqual(reload(ok).cluster_type, 'OLD TYPE')

    def test_long_text_without_limit_is_accepted(self):
        make_server('SRV1')
        self.assertEqual(self.preview(CLASSIC_HEADER + f'SRV1;;;{"X" * 1000};;;;\r\n')['changed'], 1)

    def test_server_listed_with_different_values_is_ignored(self):
        make_server('SRV1')
        data = self.preview(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;FIRST;;;;\r\nSRV1;;;SECOND;;;;\r\n')
        self.assertEqual((data['changed'], data['invalid']), (0, 2))

    def test_identical_duplicate_is_written_once(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        self.run_import(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;SAME;;;;\r\nSRV1;;;SAME;;;;\r\n')
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).action_during_lp_history.count('SAME'), 1)

    def test_unknown_server_and_missing_hostname(self):
        data = self.preview(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'NOPE;;;X;;;;\r\n;;;Y;;;;\r\n')
        self.assertEqual((data['not_found'], data['no_hostname']), (1, 1))
        self.assertEqual(data['lists']['not_found'], ['NOPE'])

    def test_quoted_text_with_separator_and_line_break(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        self.run_import(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;"LINE 1; PART 2\nLINE 2";;;;\r\n')
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).action_during_lp, 'LINE 1; PART 2\nLINE 2')

    def test_refused_files(self):
        for label, content, message in (
            ('wrong header', 'a;b\r\n1;2\r\n', 'Header incorrect.'),
            ('wrong column count', CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;YES\r\n', '1 error detected.'),
            ('not utf-8', (CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;café;;;;\r\n').encode('latin-1'), 'This file is not UTF-8 encoded.'),
        ):
            with self.subTest(label):
                response = self.upload(content, dry_run='1')
                self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
                self.assertEqual(response.json()['error'], message)

    def test_utf8_bom_is_accepted(self):
        make_server('SRV1')
        self.assertEqual(self.preview(b'\xef\xbb\xbf' + (CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;NEW;;;;\r\n').encode())['changed'], 1)

    def test_lists_are_capped_but_counts_are_not(self):
        for i in range(3):
            make_server(f'SRV{i}')
        with mock.patch.object(views, 'IMPORT_LISTED_SERVERS_CAP', 2):
            data = self.preview(CLASSIC_HEADER + ''.join(f'SRV{i};;;NEW;;;;\r\n' for i in range(3)))
        self.assertEqual((data['changed'], len(data['lists']['changed'])), (3, 2))

    def test_failure_while_saving_reports_progress_and_can_be_resumed(self):
        servers = [make_server(f'SRV{i:03d}') for i in range(150)]
        content = CLASSIC_HEADER + ''.join(f'{s.hostname};;;NEW;;;;\r\n' for s in servers)
        real, calls = ServerUnique.objects.bulk_update, []

        def fail_second_batch(*args, **kwargs):
            calls.append(1)
            if len(calls) == 2:
                raise RuntimeError('simulated failure')
            return real(*args, **kwargs)

        with mock.patch.object(ServerUnique.objects, 'bulk_update', side_effect=fail_second_batch):
            status = self.run_import(content)
        stats = status['stats']
        self.assertTrue(stats['failed'])
        self.assertEqual((stats['changed'], stats['total'], stats['not_saved']['count']), (100, 150, 50))
        self.assertEqual(ServerUnique.objects.filter(action_during_lp='NEW').count(), 100)
        self.assertEqual(self.preview(content)['changed'], 50)
        self.assertFalse(self.run_import(content)['stats']['failed'])
        self.assertEqual(ServerUnique.objects.filter(action_during_lp='NEW').count(), 150)


class ExceptionImportTests(BCTestCase):

    def test_file_is_the_source_and_exceptions_are_counted(self):
        excepted = make_server('SRV1', bc_exception='YES')
        make_server('SRV2')
        data = self.preview(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;NEW;;;;\r\nSRV2;;;NEW;;;;\r\n')
        self.assertEqual((data['changed'], data['exceptions']), (2, 1))
        self.assertEqual(data['lists']['exceptions'], ['SRV1'])
        self.run_import(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;NEW;;;;\r\nSRV2;;;NEW;;;;\r\n')
        self.assertEqual(reload(excepted).action_during_lp, 'NEW')

    def test_the_file_can_set_and_lift_an_exception(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        self.run_import(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;;;;;yes\r\n')
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).bc_exception, 'YES')
        self.assertEqual(su.global_history[0]['changes'], {'bc_exception': {'old': 'NO', 'new': 'YES'}})
        data = self.preview(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;;;;;NO\r\n')
        self.assertEqual((data['changed'], data['exceptions']), (1, 1))

    def test_invalid_exception_value_ignores_the_line(self):
        make_server('SRV1')
        data = self.preview(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;NEW;;;;MAYBE\r\n')
        self.assertEqual((data['changed'], data['invalid']), (0, 1))
        self.assertIn("invalid bc_exception value 'MAYBE'", data['lists']['invalid'][0])

    def test_file_without_the_column_is_refused(self):
        old_header = CLASSIC_HEADER.replace(';bc_exception', '')
        response = self.upload(old_header + 'SRV1;;;NEW;;;\r\n', dry_run='1')
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
        self.assertEqual(response.json()['error'], 'Header incorrect.')
        old_export = EXPORT_HEADER.replace(';bc_exception', '')
        self.assertEqual(self.upload(old_export + 'SRV1;A;;;NEW;;;\r\n', dry_run='1').status_code, 400)

    def test_application_import_counts_exceptions(self):
        make_server('SRV1', bc_exception='YES', rows=[{'DAP_AUID': 'A1'}])
        data = self.preview(APP_HEADER + 'A1;YES\r\n', import_type='application')
        self.assertEqual((data['changed'], data['exceptions']), (1, 1))

    def test_report_names_the_exceptions(self):
        make_server('SRV1', bc_exception='YES')
        status = self.run_import(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;NEW;;;;\r\n')
        response = self.client.get(reverse('businesscontinuity:import_csv_report', kwargs={'log_id': status['stats']['log_id']}))
        self.assertIn('server in exception', response.content.decode())


class ExportImportTests(BCTestCase):

    def test_one_change_per_server_whatever_the_number_of_lines(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1', rows=[{'DATACENTER': 'A'}, {'DATACENTER': 'B'}])
        data = self.preview(EXPORT_HEADER + 'SRV1;A;YES;NO;NEW;OLD NOTE;NO;OLD TYPE;\r\nSRV1;B;YES;NO;NEW;OLD NOTE;NO;OLD TYPE;\r\n')
        self.assertTrue(data['is_export'])
        self.assertEqual((data['lines'], data['servers'], data['changed']), (2, 1, 1))
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).priority_asset, 'NO')

    def test_empty_cell_of_an_export_clears_the_field(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1')
        self.run_import(EXPORT_HEADER + 'SRV1;A;NO;NO;OLD ACTION;;NO;OLD TYPE;NO\r\n')
        reload(su)
        self.assertIsNone(su.annotation)
        self.assertEqual(su.global_history[0]['changes'], {'annotation': {'old': 'OLD NOTE', 'new': '(empty)'}})
        self.assertEqual(self.preview(EXPORT_HEADER + 'SRV1;A;NO;NO;OLD ACTION;;NO;OLD TYPE;NO\r\n')['changed'], 0)

    def test_lines_that_disagree_refuse_the_file(self):
        make_server('SRV1')
        response = self.upload(EXPORT_HEADER + 'SRV1;A;YES;NO;X;Y;NO;;\r\nSRV1;B;NO;NO;X;Y;NO;;\r\n', dry_run='1')
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
        self.assertIn('conflicting line', response.json()['error'])
        self.assertIn('priority_asset', response.json()['detailed_errors'][0])


class ApplicationImportTests(BCTestCase):

    def preview_apps(self, lines):
        return self.preview(APP_HEADER + lines, import_type='application')

    def run_apps(self, lines):
        return self.run_import(APP_HEADER + lines, import_type='application')

    def test_yes_wins_on_the_same_application(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1', rows=[{'DAP_AUID': 'A1'}])
        self.run_apps('A1;NO\r\nA1;YES\r\n')
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).in_live_play, 'YES')

    def test_yes_wins_on_a_server_hosting_two_listed_applications(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1', rows=[{'DAP_AUID': 'A1'}, {'DAP_AUID': 'A2'}])
        data = self.preview_apps('A1;NO\r\nA2;YES\r\n')
        self.assertEqual(data['lists']['changed'][0]['changes'][0]['via'], 'A1=NO, A2=YES')
        self.run_apps('A1;NO\r\nA2;YES\r\n')
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).in_live_play, 'YES')

    def test_the_file_is_the_source_an_absent_application_has_no_influence(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1', in_live_play='YES', rows=[{'DAP_AUID': 'A1'}, {'DAP_AUID': 'A2'}])
        self.run_apps('A1;NO\r\n')
        self.assertEqual(reload(su).in_live_play, 'NO')

    def test_servers_of_other_applications_are_untouched(self):
        other = make_server('SRV2', in_live_play='YES', rows=[{'DAP_AUID': 'A2'}])
        make_server('SRV1', rows=[{'DAP_AUID': 'A1'}])
        self.run_apps('A1;YES\r\n')
        self.assertEqual(reload(other).in_live_play, 'YES')
        self.assertEqual(other.global_history, [])

    def test_same_file_twice_adds_no_history(self):
        su = make_server('SRV1', rows=[{'DAP_AUID': 'A1'}])
        self.run_apps('A1;YES\r\n')
        self.assertEqual(self.preview_apps('A1;YES\r\n')['changed'], 0)
        self.assertEqual(len(reload(su).global_history), 1)

    def test_ignored_lines_are_listed(self):
        make_server('SRV1', rows=[{'DAP_AUID': 'A1'}])
        data = self.preview_apps('NOPE;YES\r\nA1;MAYBE\r\n;YES\r\n')
        self.assertEqual((data['not_found'], data['invalid'], data['no_hostname'], data['changed']), (1, 1, 1, 0))
        self.assertEqual(data['lists']['not_found'], ['NOPE'])

    def test_refused_files(self):
        for label, content, message in (
            ('wrong column count', APP_HEADER + 'A1;YES;EXTRA\r\n', '1 error detected.'),
            ('not utf-8', (APP_HEADER + 'café;YES\r\n').encode('latin-1'), 'This file is not UTF-8 encoded.'),
        ):
            with self.subTest(label):
                response = self.upload(content, import_type='application', dry_run='1')
                self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
                self.assertEqual(response.json()['error'], message)


class JournalTests(BCTestCase):

    def test_import_is_journaled_with_its_author_and_counts(self):
        make_server('SRV1')
        make_server('SRV2')
        self.run_import(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;NEW;;;;\r\nSRV2;;;OLD ACTION;;;;\r\nNOPE;;;X;;;;\r\n', name='my.csv')
        log = CsvImportLog.objects.get()
        self.assertEqual((log.username, log.filename, log.file_format), ('tester', 'my.csv', 'Server Import'))
        self.assertEqual((log.updated, log.unchanged, log.ignored, log.failed), (1, 1, 1, False))

    def test_export_and_application_formats(self):
        make_server('SRV1', rows=[{'DAP_AUID': 'A1'}])
        self.run_import(EXPORT_HEADER + 'SRV1;A;YES;NO;X;Y;NO;Z;\r\n')
        self.run_import(APP_HEADER + 'A1;YES\r\n', import_type='application')
        self.assertEqual(list(CsvImportLog.objects.order_by('id').values_list('file_format', flat=True)),
                         ['Server Export', 'Application Import'])

    def test_failure_is_journaled(self):
        make_server('SRV1')
        with mock.patch.object(ServerUnique.objects, 'bulk_update', side_effect=RuntimeError('simulated failure')):
            self.run_import(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;NEW;;;;\r\n')
        log = CsvImportLog.objects.get()
        self.assertTrue(log.failed)
        self.assertIn('simulated failure', log.message)
        self.assertIn('not saved', log.report_csv)

    def test_report_has_every_outcome(self):
        make_server('SRV1')
        make_server('SRV2')
        status = self.run_import(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;NEW;;;;\r\nSRV2;;;OLD ACTION;;;;\r\nNOPE;;;X;;;;\r\nSRV3;WRONG;;;;;;\r\n')
        response = self.client.get(reverse('businesscontinuity:import_csv_report', kwargs={'log_id': status['stats']['log_id']}))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        rows = list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(response.content.decode()), delimiter=';'))
        self.assertIn({'name': 'SRV1', 'status': 'updated', 'field': 'action_during_lp', 'old': 'OLD ACTION', 'new': 'NEW', 'detail': ''}, rows)
        self.assertEqual({(r['name'], r['status']) for r in rows},
                         {('SRV1', 'updated'), ('SRV2', 'already up to date'), ('NOPE', 'not found'), ('', 'line ignored')})

    def test_only_the_latest_reports_are_kept(self):
        make_server('SRV1')
        CsvImportLog.objects.bulk_create([
            CsvImportLog(username='old', filename=f'{i}.csv', file_format='Server Import', report_csv='BIG') for i in range(35)
        ])
        self.run_import(CLASSIC_HEADER + 'SRV1;;;NEW;;;;\r\n')
        self.assertEqual(CsvImportLog.objects.count(), 36)
        self.assertEqual(CsvImportLog.objects.exclude(report_csv='').count(), views.IMPORT_REPORTS_KEPT)

    def test_report_of_a_pruned_import_answers_404(self):
        log = CsvImportLog.objects.create(username='old', filename='a.csv', file_format='Server Import')
        response = self.client.get(reverse('businesscontinuity:import_csv_report', kwargs={'log_id': log.id}))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 404)

    def test_report_needs_an_editor(self):
        log = CsvImportLog.objects.create(username='old', filename='a.csv', file_format='Server Import', report_csv='SECRET')
        response = Client().get(reverse('businesscontinuity:import_csv_report', kwargs={'log_id': log.id}))
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 403)
        self.assertNotIn(b'SECRET', response.content)

    def test_upload_needs_an_editor(self):
        response = Client().post(reverse('businesscontinuity:bulk_import_csv'), {})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 403)
        self.assertEqual(Server.objects.count(), 0)

# tests/test_selection.py

"""
Every mass operation must select exactly the servers the server list shows.
A filter forgotten in one of them once reset every server before a DR test.
"""
import copy
import json
import os
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest import mock
from urllib.parse import urlencode

from django.urls import reverse

from businesscontinuity import bc_rules_views as rules
from businesscontinuity import views
from businesscontinuity.models import Server, ServerUnique
from businesscontinuity.tests.base import BCTestCase, make_server

LABELS_PATH = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(views.__file__), 'field_labels.json')
SERVER_FIELDS = {field.name for field in Server._meta.get_fields()}
UNIQUE_FIELDS = {field.name for field in ServerUnique._meta.get_fields()}


def load_labels():
    with open(LABELS_PATH, encoding='utf-8') as f:
        return json.load(f)


class SameSelectionEverywhereTests(BCTestCase):
    """For each filter of field_labels.json, compare the page with the bulk edit and the shared builder."""

    @classmethod
    def setUpTestData(cls):
        super().setUpTestData()
        cls.labels = load_labels()
        cls.filters = []
        rows = {'A': {}, 'B': {}}
        values = {'A': {}, 'B': {}}
        for field, info in cls.labels['fields'].items():
            if not info.get('inputname') or field == 'SERVER_ID':
                continue
            for side in ('A', 'B'):
                value = f'{side}-{field}'[:100]
                if info.get('model_extra') == 'yes':
                    values[side][field] = value
                elif field in SERVER_FIELDS:
                    rows[side][field] = value
            cls.filters.append((field, info['inputname'], f'A-{field}'[:100]))
        make_server('HOST-A', rows=[rows['A']], **values['A'])
        make_server('HOST-B', rows=[rows['B']], **values['B'])
        make_server('HOST-C')
        cls.filters.append(('SERVER_ID', cls.labels['fields']['SERVER_ID']['inputname'], 'HOST-A'))

    def page_count(self, params):
        response = self.client.get(reverse('businesscontinuity:server_view'), params)
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        return response.context['unique_servers_count']

    def bulk_count(self, params):
        data = self.bulk(urlencode(params), annotation='X', dry_run='1').json()
        return data.get('servers', data)

    def builder_count(self, params):
        qs, _ = views._build_filtered_queryset(SimpleNamespace(user=self.user), urlencode(params))
        return qs.values('server_unique_id').distinct().count()

    def test_every_field_is_known_by_the_models(self):
        for field, info in self.labels['fields'].items():
            if info.get('inputname'):
                with self.subTest(field):
                    known = UNIQUE_FIELDS if info.get('model_extra') == 'yes' else SERVER_FIELDS
                    self.assertIn(field, known)

    def test_each_filter_selects_the_same_servers_everywhere(self):
        self.assertGreater(len(self.filters), 20)
        for field, input_name, value in self.filters:
            with self.subTest(field=field, input=input_name):
                params = {input_name: f'@{value}'}
                self.assertEqual(self.page_count(params), 1, 'the page itself must filter')
                self.assertEqual(self.bulk_count(params), 1, 'bulk edit')
                self.assertEqual(self.builder_count(params), 1, 'Apply BC Rules and DR reset')

    def test_a_new_or_renamed_field_is_filtered_everywhere_at_once(self):
        """The incident: a field renamed in field_labels.json must not be ignored by a mass operation."""
        renamed = copy.deepcopy(self.labels)
        renamed['fields']['annotation']['inputname'] = 'brandnewname'
        real_load = json.load

        def patched_load(f, *args, **kwargs):
            data = real_load(f, *args, **kwargs)
            return renamed if 'fields' in data and 'annotation' in data['fields'] else data

        params = {'brandnewname': '@A-annotation'}
        with mock.patch('businesscontinuity.views.json.load', side_effect=patched_load):
            self.assertEqual(self.page_count(params), 1)
            self.assertEqual(self.bulk_count(params), 1)
            self.assertEqual(self.builder_count(params), 1)


class CountSafetyTests(BCTestCase):
    """The page sends the number of servers it shows: every mass operation refuses another number."""

    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        labels = copy.deepcopy(rules._load_field_labels())
        labels['bc_datacenter_siblings'] = {'TEST-DC-A': 'TEST-DC-B'}
        patcher = mock.patch.object(rules, '_load_field_labels', return_value=labels)
        patcher.start()
        self.addCleanup(patcher.stop)
        self.a = make_server('SRV1', rows=[{'DATACENTER': 'TEST-DC-A', 'MACHINE_TYPE': 'HYPERVISOR'}])
        self.b = make_server('SRV2', rows=[{'DATACENTER': 'TEST-DC-A', 'MACHINE_TYPE': 'HYPERVISOR'}])

    def test_selection_mismatch_message(self):
        self.assertIsNone(views.selection_mismatch(2, '2'))
        self.assertIsNone(views.selection_mismatch(2, ''))
        self.assertIn('would target 2 servers, but the page shows 3', views.selection_mismatch(2, '3'))

    def test_apply_bc_rules(self):
        for count, ok in (('2', True), ('5', False)):
            with self.subTest(count=count):
                query = f'datacenter=%40TEST-DC-A&expected_servers={count}'
                response = self.client.get(reverse('businesscontinuity:bc_scope'), {'query': query})
                self.assertEqual(response.status_code == 200, ok, response.content)
                response = self.post_json('businesscontinuity:bc_start', {'query': query, 'sibling': None, 'rule_texts': {}})
                self.assertEqual(response.status_code == 200, ok, response.content)
        self.assertEqual(ServerUnique.objects.filter(action_during_lp='OLD ACTION').count(), 0)

    def test_apply_bc_rules_refused_writes_nothing(self):
        response = self.post_json('businesscontinuity:bc_start',
                                  {'query': 'datacenter=%40TEST-DC-A&expected_servers=9', 'sibling': None, 'rule_texts': {}})
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 400)
        self.assertIn('but the page shows 9', response.json()['error'])
        self.assertEqual(ServerUnique.objects.filter(action_during_lp='OLD ACTION').count(), 2)

    def test_dr_reset(self):
        preview = self.client.get(reverse('businesscontinuity:reset_dr_preview'),
                                  {'query': 'datacenter=%40TEST-DC-A', 'expected_servers': '3'})
        self.assertEqual(preview.status_code, 409)
        response = self.client.post(reverse('businesscontinuity:reset_dr'), {
            'confirm_token': 'CONFIRM', 'keep_history': '1', 'fields': 'annotation',
            'query': 'datacenter=%40TEST-DC-A', 'expected_servers': '3',
        })
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 409)
        self.assertIn('but the page shows 3', response.json()['error'])
        self.assertEqual(ServerUnique.objects.filter(annotation='OLD NOTE').count(), 2)
        response = self.client.post(reverse('businesscontinuity:reset_dr'), {
            'confirm_token': 'CONFIRM', 'keep_history': '1', 'fields': 'annotation',
            'query': 'datacenter=%40TEST-DC-A', 'expected_servers': '2',
        })
        self.assertEqual(response.status_code, 200)
        self.assertEqual(ServerUnique.objects.filter(annotation='EMPTY').count(), 2)
